```text
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { sealChannelCredential } from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-crypto';
import { OutboundService } from '../src/domain/inbox/outbound.service';
import { Channel, type ChannelMessage, type ChannelMessageKind, type ChannelSendOptions, type ChannelSendResult, type TextMessage } from '../src/domain/channels/transport/channel';
import { ChannelAdapterFactory } from '../src/integrations/channels/channel-adapter-factory';
import { ChannelAdapterRegistry } from '../src/integrations/channels/channel-adapter-registry';
import type { ResolvedChannelCredential } from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-resolver';
import { MetaChannelFactory } from '../src/integrations/channels/meta-channel';
import { EvolutionChannelFactory } from '../src/integrations/channels/evolution-channel';
import { MetaHttpClient } from '../src/integrations/meta/meta.client';
import { MetaInitialMessageClient } from '../src/integrations/meta/initial-message.client';
import {
  contratarCanalNaoOficial, criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, evolutionFake, limparBanco, login, metaFake, subirApp,
  criarConversaFixture, criarRotaConversaFixture, obterSelecaoRespostaV2,
} from './helpers';

class OpaqueRecipientChannel extends Channel {
  constructor(private readonly sent: TextMessage[]) { super(); }
  readonly supportedKinds: readonly ChannelMessageKind[] = Object.freeze(['text']);
  async sendMessage(message: ChannelMessage, options?: ChannelSendOptions): Promise<ChannelSendResult> {
    if (message.kind !== 'text') return { outcome: 'BLOCKED', reason: 'UNSUPPORTED_KIND' };
    let claimed: boolean;
    try { claimed = await options?.beforeDispatch?.() ?? false; } catch { claimed = false; }
    if (!claimed) return { outcome: 'BLOCKED', reason: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED' };
    this.sent.push(message);
    return { outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: `opaque-fake-${this.sent.length}` };
  }
}

class OpaqueRecipientFactory extends ChannelAdapterFactory {
  readonly origin = 'OFICIAL' as const;
  constructor(private readonly sent: TextMessage[]) { super(); }
  locallyConfigured() { return true; }
  create() { return new OpaqueRecipientChannel(this.sent); }
}

class MutatingMetaFactory extends ChannelAdapterFactory {
  readonly origin = 'OFICIAL' as const;
  private readonly delegate = new MetaChannelFactory(metaFake, new MetaInitialMessageClient(metaFake));
  constructor(private readonly mutateBeforeFence: () => Promise<void>) { super(); }
  locallyConfigured() { return this.delegate.locallyConfigured(); }
  create(credential: ResolvedChannelCredential, beforeDispatch: () => Promise<boolean>) {
    return this.delegate.create(credential, async () => {
      await this.mutateBeforeFence();
      return beforeDispatch();
    });
  }
}

class MutatingEvolutionFactory extends ChannelAdapterFactory {
  readonly origin = 'NAO_OFICIAL' as const;
  private readonly delegate = new EvolutionChannelFactory(evolutionFake);
  constructor(private readonly mutateBeforeFence: () => Promise<void>) { super(); }
  locallyConfigured() { return this.delegate.locallyConfigured(); }
  create(credential: ResolvedChannelCredential, beforeDispatch: () => Promise<boolean>) {
    return this.delegate.create(credential, async () => {
      await this.mutateBeforeFence();
      return beforeDispatch();
    });
  }
}

describe('common Channel outbound (PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let buId: string;
  let conversationId: string;
  let identityId: string;
  let cookie: string;

  const seedHistoricalInbound = async (db: PrismaService, id: string, unitId: string,
    origin: 'OFICIAL' | 'NAO_OFICIAL' = 'OFICIAL', proof?: { providerResourceIdentityId: string; officialInboundAt: Date }) => {
    const createdAt = proof?.officialInboundAt ?? new Date();
    if (proof && origin !== 'OFICIAL') throw new Error('Only a selected current Official inbound may carry trusted window proof.');
    return db.message.create({ data: {
      conversationId: id, buId: unitId, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO', content: 'explicit historical inbound',
      channelOrigin: origin, status: 'RECEBIDA', createdAt,
      ...(proof ? { providerResourceIdentityId: proof.providerResourceIdentityId, officialInboundAt: proof.officialInboundAt } : {}),
    } });
  };
  const currentOfficialProof = (providerResourceIdentityId: string) => ({ providerResourceIdentityId, officialInboundAt: new Date() });
  const replyBody = async (id: string, session: string, text: string, channelType: 'OFICIAL' | 'NAO_OFICIAL' = 'OFICIAL') => {
    const selected = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: (await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id }, select: { buId: true } })).buId, type: channelType } });
    return { texto: text, ...await obterSelecaoRespostaV2(app, id, session, selected.id) };
  };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
    expect(app.getHttpServer().listening).toBe(true);
    expect(app.getHttpServer().address()).toMatchObject({ address: '127.0.0.1', family: 'IPv4' });
  });
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Channel outbound fixture', 'channel-outbound@example.test');
    buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const binding = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'OFICIAL', '987654321', { requiresWindow: false, provedBusinessPhone: '5584999990000', provedWabaId: 'synthetic-channel-outbound-waba' });
    identityId = binding.identity.id;
    const conversation = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, {
      buId, contact: '5511990000001', contactType: 'PESSOA', displayName: 'Opaque recipient', status: 'QUEUED',
    });
    conversationId = conversation.id;
    await criarRotaConversaFixture(prisma, { conversationId, channelId: binding.channel.id });
    cookie = await login(app, 'channel-outbound@example.test');
  });
  afterAll(async () => { if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma); await app?.close(); });

  it('sends a trusted opaque recipient through one bound adapter and finalizes without stealing a later owner', async () => {
    await seedHistoricalInbound(prisma, conversationId, buId, 'OFICIAL', currentOfficialProof(identityId));
    const gate = await prisma.conversation.update({ where: { id: conversationId }, data: {
      status: 'IN_SERVICE', attendantName: 'Claim before send', attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO',
    } });
    const response = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send(await replyBody(conversationId, cookie, 'opaque recipient test')).expect(201);
    expect(response.body).toMatchObject({ canal: 'OFICIAL' });
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toEqual([{ para: '5511990000001', texto: 'opaque recipient test' }]);
    const message = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT' } });
    expect(message).toMatchObject({ externalId: expect.any(String), content: 'opaque recipient test', authorUserId: expect.any(String) });
    const finalized = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: gate.id } });
    expect(finalized).toMatchObject({ attendantName: 'Claim before send', attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO' });
  });

  it('runs an opaque user recipient through an independent Channel fake and never calls provider clients', async () => {
    const sent: TextMessage[] = [];
    const registry = new ChannelAdapterRegistry([new OpaqueRecipientFactory(sent)]);
    const isolated = await subirApp(registry);
    try {
      await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
      const tenant = await criarMantenedora(isolated.app, 'Opaque fake contract', 'opaque-fake@example.test');
      const localBuId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
      const localBinding = await criarCanalVerificado(isolated.prisma, localBuId, 'OFICIAL', 'opaque-fake-resource', { requiresWindow: false, provedWabaId: 'synthetic-opaque-fake-waba' });
      const localConversation = await criarConversaFixture(isolated.prisma, {
        buId: localBuId, contact: '5511990000001', contactType: 'PESSOA', displayName: 'Opaque fake recipient', status: 'QUEUED',
      });
      await criarRotaConversaFixture(isolated.prisma, { conversationId: localConversation.id, channelId: localBinding.channel.id });
      await seedHistoricalInbound(isolated.prisma, localConversation.id, localBuId, 'OFICIAL', currentOfficialProof(localBinding.identity.id));
      const localCookie = await login(isolated.app, 'opaque-fake@example.test');
      await request(isolated.app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${localConversation.id}/messages`)
        .set('Cookie', localCookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'wire-independent fake', ...await obterSelecaoRespostaV2(isolated.app, localConversation.id, localCookie, localBinding.channel.id) }).expect(201);
      expect(sent).toEqual([{ kind: 'text', recipientRef: '5511990000001', text: 'wire-independent fake' }]);
      expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
      expect(evolutionFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
      expect(await isolated.prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: localConversation.id, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(1);
    } finally {
      await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
      await isolated.app.close();
    }
  });

  it('blocks real binding changes inside Meta/Evolution adapter pre-dispatch callbacks', async () => {
    const cases = [
      { provider: 'OFICIAL' as const, changed: 'generation' as const },
      { provider: 'OFICIAL' as const, changed: 'pairingEpoch' as const },
      { provider: 'OFICIAL' as const, changed: 'credentialVersion' as const },
      { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL' as const, changed: 'generation' as const },
      { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL' as const, changed: 'pairingEpoch' as const },
    ];
    for (const { provider, changed } of cases) {
      let mutate!: () => Promise<void>;
      const previousModuleSwitch = process.env.LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL;
      if (provider === 'NAO_OFICIAL') process.env.LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL = 'true';
      const registry = new ChannelAdapterRegistry([provider === 'OFICIAL'
        ? new MutatingMetaFactory(() => mutate())
        : new MutatingEvolutionFactory(() => mutate())]);
      const isolated = await subirApp(registry);
      try {
        await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
        const tenant = await criarMantenedora(isolated.app, `Pre-dispatch ${changed}`, `pre-dispatch-${changed}@example.test`);
        if (provider === 'NAO_OFICIAL') {
          await contratarCanalNaoOficial(isolated.prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
        }
        const localBinding = await criarCanalVerificado(isolated.prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, provider,
          `pre-dispatch-${provider}-${changed}`, { requiresWindow: false, ...(provider === 'OFICIAL' ? { provedWabaId: `synthetic-pre-dispatch-${changed}-waba` } : {}) });
        const localConversation = await criarConversaFixture(isolated.prisma, {
          buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contact: '5511990000002', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED',
        });
        await criarRotaConversaFixture(isolated.prisma, { conversationId: localConversation.id, channelId: localBinding.channel.id });
        await seedHistoricalInbound(isolated.prisma, localConversation.id, tenant.businessUnit.id, provider,
          provider === 'OFICIAL' ? currentOfficialProof(localBinding.identity.id) : undefined);
        const localCookie = await login(isolated.app, `pre-dispatch-${changed}@example.test`);
        let changedOnce = false;
        mutate = async () => {
          if (changedOnce) return;
          changedOnce = true;
          if (changed === 'generation') {
            await isolated.prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: localBinding.channel.id }, data: { generation: { increment: 1 } } });
          } else if (changed === 'pairingEpoch') {
            await isolated.prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: localBinding.channel.id }, data: { pairingEpoch: { increment: 1 } } });
          } else {
            await isolated.prisma.channelCredential.update({ where: { channelId: localBinding.channel.id }, data: { version: { increment: 1 } } });
          }
        };
        const metaCallsBefore = metaFake.providerCalls;
        const evolutionCallsBefore = evolutionFake.providerCalls;
        const tuple = await obterSelecaoRespostaV2(isolated.app, localConversation.id, localCookie, localBinding.channel.id);
        await request(isolated.app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${localConversation.id}/messages`)
          .set('Cookie', localCookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: `blocked ${changed}`, ...tuple })
          .expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body).toMatchObject({ code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'BLOCKED' }));
        expect(await isolated.prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: localConversation.id, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(0);
        expect(metaFake.providerCalls).toBe(metaCallsBefore);
        expect(evolutionFake.providerCalls).toBe(evolutionCallsBefore);
      } finally {
        await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
        await isolated.app.close();
        if (previousModuleSwitch === undefined) delete process.env.LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL;
        else process.env.LH_CHANNEL_UNOFFICIAL = previousModuleSwitch;
      }
    }
  });

  it('persists nothing for contradictory successful Meta envelopes', async () => {
    process.env.GRAPH_VERSION = 'v26.0';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ success: false, messages: [{ id: 'contradictory' }] }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ success: false, messages: [{}] }) });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    const isolatedMeta = new MetaHttpClient();
    const isolated = await subirApp(new ChannelAdapterRegistry([new MetaChannelFactory(isolatedMeta, new MetaInitialMessageClient(isolatedMeta))]));
    try {
      await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
      const tenant = await criarMantenedora(isolated.app, 'Contradictory Meta ACK', 'contradictory-meta@example.test');
      const localBinding = await criarCanalVerificado(isolated.prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', 'contradictory-meta-resource', { requiresWindow: false, provedWabaId: 'synthetic-contradictory-meta-waba' });
      const conversation = await criarConversaFixture(isolated.prisma, {
        buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contact: '5511990000001', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED',
      });
      await criarRotaConversaFixture(isolated.prisma, { conversationId: conversation.id, channelId: localBinding.channel.id });
      await seedHistoricalInbound(isolated.prisma, conversation.id, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', currentOfficialProof(localBinding.identity.id));
      const session = await login(isolated.app, 'contradictory-meta@example.test');
      for (let requestIndex = 1; requestIndex <= 2; requestIndex += 1) {
        await request(isolated.app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversation.id}/messages`)
          .set('Cookie', session).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: `contradictory result ${requestIndex}`, ...await obterSelecaoRespostaV2(isolated.app, conversation.id, session, localBinding.channel.id) })
          .expect(400).expect(({ body }) => expect(body).toMatchObject({
            outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED',
          }));
        expect((await request(isolated.app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversation.id}/messages`)
          .set('Cookie', session)).body.messages.filter((message: { direction: string }) => message.direction === 'OUT')).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(fetchFake).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(requestIndex);
        expect(await isolated.prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: conversation.id, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(0);
      }
    } finally {
      vi.unstubAllGlobals();
      await limparBanco(isolated.prisma);
      await isolated.app.close();
    }
  });

  it('requires BFF session and CSRF and rejects a foreign-account public send before dispatch', async () => {
    const sentBefore = metaFake.enviados.length;
    const outgoingBefore = await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT' } });
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .send({ texto: 'without session' }).expect(401);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).send({ texto: 'without CSRF header' }).expect(403);

    const foreign = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Foreign public destination', 'foreign-public-destination@example.test');
    const foreignCookie = await login(app, 'foreign-public-destination@example.test');
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', foreignCookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'valid session without BU grant' }).expect(404);
    const key = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/bff/integracao/api-keys').set('Cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ nome: 'Scoped channel key', buIds: [buId] }).expect(201);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/v1/messages').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${key.body.chave}`)
      .send({ account_id: foreign.businessUnit.id, telefone: '5511990000001', texto: 'foreign API key send' })
      .expect(403).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('SEM_ACESSO_A_CONTA'));
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(sentBefore);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(outgoingBefore);
    expect(evolutionFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('holds two tenants in flight with distinct scoped resources and credentials', async () => {
    const tenantB = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Parallel tenant B', 'parallel-tenant-b@example.test');
    const channelA = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL' } });
    const bindingA = {
      channel: channelA,
      identity: await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channelA.id } }),
    };
    const bindingB = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, tenantB.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', 'parallel-resource-b', { requiresWindow: false, provedWabaId: 'synthetic-parallel-waba-b' });
    const credentialA = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: bindingA.channel.id } });
    const credentialB = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: bindingB.channel.id } });
    const key = Buffer.from(process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY!, 'base64');
    const tokenA = 'tenant-a-distinct-scoped-token';
    const tokenB = 'tenant-b-distinct-scoped-token';
    for (const [binding, credential, token] of [[bindingA, credentialA, tokenA], [bindingB, credentialB, tokenB]] as const) {
      const envelope = sealChannelCredential(token, credential.keyId, key, {
        purpose: 'active', provider: 'OFICIAL', buId: binding.channel.buId,
        channelId: binding.channel.id, generation: binding.channel.generation, version: credential.version,
      });
      await prisma.channelCredential.update({ where: { channelId: binding.channel.id }, data: {
        nonce: new Uint8Array(envelope.nonce), ciphertext: new Uint8Array(envelope.ciphertext), tag: new Uint8Array(envelope.tag),
      } });
      metaFake.credentialByPhone.set(binding.channel.phoneNumberId!, token);
    }
    const conversationA = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, {
      buId, contact: '5511990000003', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED',
    });
    const conversationB = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, {
      buId: tenantB.businessUnit.id, contact: '5511990000004', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED',
    });
    await criarRotaConversaFixture(prisma, { conversationId: conversationA.id, channelId: bindingA.channel.id });
    await criarRotaConversaFixture(prisma, { conversationId: conversationB.id, channelId: bindingB.channel.id });
    await seedHistoricalInbound(prisma, conversationA.id, buId, 'OFICIAL', currentOfficialProof(bindingA.identity.id));
    await seedHistoricalInbound(prisma, conversationB.id, tenantB.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', currentOfficialProof(bindingB.identity.id));
    const cookieB = await login(app, 'parallel-tenant-b@example.test');
    let arrived = 0;
    let markBothEntered!: () => void;
    let releaseBoth!: () => void;
    const bothEntered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { markBothEntered = resolve; });
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseBoth = resolve; });
    metaFake.sendHook = async () => { arrived += 1; if (arrived === 2) markBothEntered(); await held; };
    const tupleA = await obterSelecaoRespostaV2(app, conversationA.id, cookie, bindingA.channel.id);
    const tupleB = await obterSelecaoRespostaV2(app, conversationB.id, cookieB, bindingB.channel.id);
    const pendingA = request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationA.id}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'tenant A parallel', ...tupleA }).then((response) => response);
    const pendingB = request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationB.id}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookieB).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'tenant B parallel', ...tupleB }).then((response) => response);
    await bothEntered;
    expect(metaFake.credentialCalls.map(({ phoneNumberId, accessToken }) => ({ phoneNumberId, accessToken })).sort((a, b) => a.phoneNumberId.localeCompare(b.phoneNumberId)))
      .toEqual([
        { phoneNumberId: channelA.phoneNumberId!, accessToken: tokenA },
        { phoneNumberId: 'parallel-resource-b', accessToken: tokenB },
      ]);
    releaseBoth();
    const [responseA, responseB] = await Promise.all([pendingA, pendingB]);
    expect(responseA.status).toBe(201);
    expect(responseB.status).toBe(201);
    const [messageA, messageB] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { conversationId: conversationA.id, direction: 'OUT' } }),
      prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { conversationId: conversationB.id, direction: 'OUT' } }),
    ]);
    expect(messageA).toMatchObject({ buId, providerResourceIdentityId: bindingA.identity.id, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', content: 'tenant A parallel' });
    expect(messageB).toMatchObject({ buId: tenantB.businessUnit.id, providerResourceIdentityId: bindingB.identity.id, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', content: 'tenant B parallel' });
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([

      { para: '5511990000004', texto: 'tenant B parallel' },
    ]));
  });

      .send({ buId, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'REAUTORIZAR', channelId: (await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL' } })).id, expectedGeneration: 1 })

    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/number-registrations/${created.body.attempt.id}/confirm`)

  it('accepts a held acknowledged send when only the active credential version rotates', async () => {

    } });

  it('breaks equal-timestamp origin ties deterministically by descending message id', async () => {
    const evolution = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'NAO_OFICIAL', `outbound-tie-evolution-${Date.now()}`, { requiresWindow: false });
    const sameTime = new Date('2026-10-20T12:00:00.000Z');

  it('rejects cross-BU and mismatched verified-ingress bindings before dispatch', async () => {
    const setor = await prisma.setor.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { mantenedoraId: (await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: buId } })).mantenedoraId } });
    const foreign = await prisma.businessUnit.create({

    });
    const foreignChannel = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, foreign.id, 'OFICIAL', 'foreign-resource-transport', { requiresWindow: false });
    const outbound = app.get(OutboundService);

      },
    })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409, response: { code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'BLOCKED' } });
    await expect(outbound.send({

        providerResourceIdentityId: foreignChannel.identity.id, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', generation: 1, pairingEpoch: 0,

  it('preserves a human BFF claim that overlaps a public API accepted send', async () => {
    const phone = '5584999912345';
    const conversation = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId, contact: phone, contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED' });
    const official = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL' } });
    const officialIdentity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: official.id } });
    await criarRotaConversaFixture(prisma, { conversationId: conversation.id, channelId: official.id });
    const officialInboundAt = new Date();
    await prisma.message.create({ data: {
      conversationId: conversation.id, buId, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO', content: 'public inbound', channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL',
      status: 'RECEBIDA', providerResourceIdentityId: officialIdentity.id, createdAt: officialInboundAt, officialInboundAt,
    } });
    const key = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/bff/integracao/api-keys').set('Cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ nome: 'Channel overlap key', buIds: [buId] }).expect(201);
    metaFake.sendHook = async () => {
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversation.id}/claim`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(201);
    };
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/v1/messages').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${key.body.chave}`)
      .send({ account_id: buId, telefone: phone, texto: 'public outbound during claim' }).expect(201);
    const claimed = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversation.id } });
    expect(claimed).toMatchObject({ status: 'IN_SERVICE', attendantUserId: expect.any(String), attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO' });
    expect(claimed.attendantName).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('completes five real-PostgreSQL rounds of twenty overlapping sends with one accepted Message each', async () => {
    const operationsPerRound = 20;
    const operationCounts: number[] = [];
    const acceptedCounts: number[] = [];
    const recordedCounts: number[] = [];
    const perOwnedConversationRowCounts: number[][] = [];
    for (let round = 0; round < 5; round += 1) {
      const conversations = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: operationsPerRound }, (_, index) => criarConversaFixture(prisma, {
        buId, contact: `551199${String(round * operationsPerRound + index + 1000).padStart(6, '0')}`, contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED',
      })));
      const official = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL' } });
      await Promise.all(conversations.map(({ id }) => criarRotaConversaFixture(prisma, { conversationId: id, channelId: official.id })));
      const officialIdentity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: official.id } });
      await Promise.all(conversations.map(({ id }) => seedHistoricalInbound(prisma, id, buId, 'OFICIAL', currentOfficialProof(officialIdentity.id))));
      const tuples = await Promise.all(conversations.map(({ id }) => obterSelecaoRespostaV2(app, id, cookie, official.id)));
      let arrivals = 0;
      let markBarrier!: () => void;
      let release!: () => void;
      const barrier = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { markBarrier = resolve; });
      const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
      metaFake.sendHook = async () => {
        arrivals += 1;
        if (arrivals === operationsPerRound) markBarrier();
        await gate;
      };
      const pending = conversations.map((conversation, index) => request(app.getHttpServer())
        .post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversation.id}/messages`).set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .send({ texto: `round ${round} outbound ${index}`, ...tuples[index] }).then((response) => response, (error) => ({ status: 0, error })));
      let responses: Array<{ status: number }>;
      let barrierTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
      try {
        await Promise.race([barrier, new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
          barrierTimer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('overlap barrier timed out')), 20_000);
        })]);
      } finally {
        if (barrierTimer) clearTimeout(barrierTimer);
        release();
        responses = await Promise.all(pending);
        metaFake.sendHook = null;
      }
      const accepted = responses.filter((response) => response.status === 201).length;
      const recorded = await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: { in: conversations.map(({ id }) => id) }, direction: 'OUT' } });
      const groupedRows = await prisma.message.groupBy({
        by: ['conversationId'],
        where: { conversationId: { in: conversations.map(({ id }) => id) }, direction: 'OUT' },
        _count: { _all: true },
      });
      const rowCountByConversation = new Map(groupedRows.map((row) => [row.conversationId, row._count._all]));
      const ownedConversationRowCounts = conversations.map(({ id }) => rowCountByConversation.get(id) ?? 0);
      operationCounts.push(responses.length);
      acceptedCounts.push(accepted);
      recordedCounts.push(recorded);
      perOwnedConversationRowCounts.push(ownedConversationRowCounts);
      expect(accepted).toBe(operationsPerRound);
      expect(recorded).toBe(operationsPerRound);
      expect(ownedConversationRowCounts).toEqual(Array(operationsPerRound).fill(1));
    }
    console.log(`[CHANNEL-OUTBOUND-OVERLAP]${JSON.stringify({ repetitions: 5, operationsPerRepetition: operationsPerRound, operationCounts, acceptedCounts, recordedCounts, perOwnedConversationRowCounts, limits: 'Disposable PostgreSQL and provider fake; each distinct owned conversation has exactly one OUT row in this bounded fixture; no same-key exactly-once, capacity, or SLO claim.' })}`);
  });
});
```

```text
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { META_APP_SECRET, criarCanalVerificado, criarConversasFixtureEmLote, criarMantenedora, limparBanco, login, metaFake, subirApp, typebotFake } from './helpers';

describe('manual conversation open (PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let tenant: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  const resource = 'manual-open-resource';

  beforeAll(async () => { ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp(undefined, true)); await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1'); });
  afterAll(async () => { if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma); await app?.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Manual open', 'manual-open@example.test');
    await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', resource, { provedWabaId: '123456789' });
    metaFake.enviados = [];
    typebotFake.enabled = true;
    typebotFake.starts = 0;
  });

  it('creates a queued empty row once, then reuses it without changing unread/status/owner even if readiness later changes', async () => {
    const cookie = await login(app, 'manual-open@example.test');
    const path = `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations/open`;
    const created = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(path).set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'manual-open')
      .send({ telefone: '+55 (11) 98765-4321' }).expect(201);
    expect(created.body).toMatchObject({ criada: true, contact: '5511987654321', conversaId: expect.any(String) });
    const original = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: created.body.conversaId } });
    expect(original).toMatchObject({ status: 'QUEUED', unreadCount: 0, lastMessageAt: null, attendantUserId: null });
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);

    await prisma.conversation.update({ where: { id: original.id }, data: {
      status: 'IN_SERVICE', unreadCount: 7, attendantUserId: tenant.owner.id,
      attendantName: 'Manual owner', attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO',
    } });
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'OFICIAL' } });
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel.id }, data: { status: 'DESCONECTADO' } });
    const reused = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(path).set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'manual-open')
      .send({ telefone: '5511987654321' }).expect(200);
    expect(reused.body).toEqual({ conversaId: original.id, criada: false, contact: '5511987654321' });
    expect(await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: original.id } })).toMatchObject({
      status: 'IN_SERVICE', unreadCount: 7, lastMessageAt: null,
      attendantUserId: tenant.owner.id, attendantName: 'Manual owner', attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO',
    });
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: original.id } })).toBe(0);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('serializes concurrent opens on the shared BU/contact advisory key', async () => {
    const cookie = await login(app, 'manual-open@example.test');
    const path = `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations/open`;
    const responses = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
      .post(path).set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'manual-open').send({ telefone: '5584999988776' })));
    expect(responses.map(({ status }) => status).sort()).toEqual([200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 201]);
    expect(new Set(responses.map(({ body }) => body.conversaId)).size).toBe(1);
    expect(responses.filter(({ body }) => body.criada)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contact: '5584999988776' } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(0);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('reuses a formatted legacy WhatsApp PERSON row before channel readiness', async () => {
    const cookie = await login(app, 'manual-open@example.test');
    const contact = await prisma.contact.create({ data: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, displayName: 'Synthetic legacy peer' } });
    await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: contact.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: 'PERSON',
      scopeReference: 'BU', externalPeerId: '5511987654000',
    } });
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: contact.id, contact: '+55 (11) 98765-4000',
      contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'IN_SERVICE', unreadCount: 6,
    } });
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'OFICIAL' } });
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel.id }, data: { status: 'DESCONECTADO' } });

    const reused = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations/open`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'manual-open').send({ telefone: '5511987654000' }).expect(200);
    expect(reused.body).toEqual({ conversaId: conversation.id, criada: false, contact: '5511987654000' });
    expect(await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversation.id } })).toMatchObject({
      contact: '+55 (11) 98765-4000', status: 'IN_SERVICE', unreadCount: 6,
    });
  });

  it('serializes a manual open racing with first inbound identity creation', async () => {
    const cookie = await login(app, 'manual-open@example.test');
    const contact = '5584999988777';
    const body = { entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: {
      metadata: { phone_number_id: resource },
      messages: [{ id: 'manual-open-inbound-race', from: contact, timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), type: 'text', text: { body: 'synthetic race' } }],
    } }] }] };
    const signature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(JSON.stringify(body)).digest('hex')}`;
    const path = `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations/open`;
    const [opened, inbound] = await Promise.all([
      request(app.getHttpServer()).post(path).set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'manual-open').send({ telefone: contact }),
      request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('x-hub-signature-256', signature).send(body),
    ]);
    expect([200, 201]).toContain(opened.status);
    expect(inbound.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contact } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.contactIdentity.count({ where: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: 'PERSON', scopeReference: 'BU', externalPeerId: contact,
    } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, externalId: 'manual-open-inbound-race' } })).toBe(1);
  });

  it('completes five waves of twenty same-contact opens with one row and one creation', async () => {
    const cookie = await login(app, 'manual-open@example.test');
    const path = `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations/open`;
    const providerCallsBefore = metaFake.providerCalls;
    const waves: Array<Array<{ status: number; body: { conversaId: string; criada: boolean } }>> = [];
    const contacts: string[] = [];
    for (let wave = 0; wave < 5; wave += 1) {
      const contact = `55849999887${String(wave).padStart(2, '0')}`;
      contacts.push(contact);
      waves.push(await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 20 }, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
        .post(path).set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'manual-open').send({ telefone: contact }))));
    }
    const responses = waves.flat();
    const evidence = {
      waves: waves.length,
      opensPerWave: 20,
      openCount: responses.length,
      createdCount: responses.filter(({ body }) => body.criada).length,
      reusedCount: responses.filter(({ body }) => !body.criada).length,
      createdPerWave: waves.map((wave) => wave.filter(({ body }) => body.criada).length),
      statusCounts: responses.reduce<Record<string, number>>((counts, response) => {
        counts[String(response.status)] = (counts[String(response.status)] ?? 0) + 1;
        return counts;
      }, {}),
      providerCallDelta: metaFake.providerCalls - providerCallsBefore,
    };
    console.log(`[CONVERSATION-OPEN-BCI]${JSON.stringify(evidence)}`);
    expect(evidence).toMatchObject({ waves: 5, opensPerWave: 20, openCount: 100, createdCount: 5, reusedCount: 95, createdPerWave: [1, 1, 1, 1, 1], statusCounts: { '200': 95, '201': 5 }, providerCallDelta: 0 });
    expect(new Set(responses.map(({ body }) => body.conversaId)).size).toBe(5);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contact: { in: contacts } } })).toBe(5);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it('uses the bounded SQL-only expression index for the canonical inbox order', async () => {
    await criarConversasFixtureEmLote(prisma, Array.from({ length: 15000 }, (_, index) => ({
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id,
      contact: `559${String(index).padStart(10, '0')}`,
      contactType: 'PESSOA' as const,
      status: 'QUEUED' as const,
      createdAt: new Date(Date.UTC(2024, 0, 1) + index * 1000),
      ...(index % 3 === 0 ? { lastMessageAt: new Date(Date.UTC(2025, 0, 1) + index * 1000) } : {}),
    })));
    await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe('ANALYZE conversations');
    const planRows = await prisma.$queryRawUnsafe<Array<{ 'QUERY PLAN': string }>>(`
      EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS, FORMAT TEXT)
      SELECT id FROM conversations WHERE bu_id = '${tenant.businessUnit.id}'
        AND contact_type = 'PESSOA'::"ContactType" AND status = 'QUEUED'::"ConversationStatus"
      ORDER BY COALESCE(last_message_at, created_at) DESC, id DESC
      LIMIT 50`);
    const plan = planRows.map(({ 'QUERY PLAN': line }) => line).join('\n');
    console.log(`[INBOX-ORDER-EXPLAIN]${JSON.stringify({ rowCount: 50, plan })}`);
    expect(plan).toContain('conversations_inbox_order_idx');
    expect(plan).toMatch(/actual time=.*rows=50/i);
    expect(plan).toMatch(/Buffers:/i);
    expect(plan).not.toMatch(/Sort Method: external/i);
  });

  it('keeps OUT-heavy QUEUED inbox listing batched and performs no per-row IN/message hydration', async () => {
    const cookie = await login(app, 'manual-open@example.test');
    await criarConversasFixtureEmLote(prisma, Array.from({ length: 60 }, (_, index) => ({
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contact: `5598${String(index).padStart(9, '0')}`,
      contactType: 'PESSOA' as const, status: 'QUEUED' as const,
      createdAt: new Date(Date.UTC(2025, 0, 1) + index * 1000),
    })));
    const conversations = await prisma.conversation.findMany({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id }, select: { id: true } });
    await prisma.message.createMany({ data: conversations.map(({ id }, index) => ({
      conversationId: id, buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, direction: 'OUT' as const, messageType: 'TEXTO' as const,
      content: `synthetic outbound ${index}`, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL' as const, status: 'ENVIADA' as const,
      externalId: `synthetic-out-${index}`,
    })) });

    const queries: string[] = [];
    let collecting = true;
    prisma.$on('query', (event) => { if (collecting) queries.push(event.query); });
    const listed = await request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations?aba=QUEUED&limite=50`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    collecting = false;
    expect(listed.body).toMatchObject({ total: 60, pagina: 1, temMais: true });
    expect(listed.body.conversations).toHaveLength(50);
    const conversationQueries = queries.filter((query) => /\bconversations\b/i.test(query));
    const messageQueries = queries.filter((query) => /\bmessages\b/i.test(query));
    expect(conversationQueries).toHaveLength(3); // count + ordered page IDs + one batched projection
    expect(conversationQueries.some((query) => /ORDER BY COALESCE\(last_message_at, created_at\)/i.test(query))).toBe(true);
    expect(conversationQueries.filter((query) => /\bid\b.*\bIN\b/i.test(query))).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(messageQueries).toHaveLength(0);
    console.log(`[INBOX-OUT-HEAVY-QUERY-COUNT]${JSON.stringify({ rows: 60, listed: 50, conversationQueries: conversationQueries.length, messageQueries: messageQueries.length })}`);
  });

  it('does not fire NOVA_CONVERSA on manual open or on the first inbound into that row', async () => {
    const cookie = await login(app, 'manual-open@example.test');
    const bot = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: {
      mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'manual-open-new-conversation',
      publishedTypebotId: 'manual-open-new-conversation-published', name: 'Manual open',
      editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/manual-open',
    } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });
    const contact = '5584999988001';
    const path = `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations/open`;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(path).set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'manual-open')
      .send({ telefone: contact }).expect(201);
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(0);

    const body = { entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: {
      metadata: { phone_number_id: resource },
      messages: [{ id: 'manual-open-first-inbound', from: contact, timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), type: 'text', text: { body: 'ajuda' } }],
    } }] }] };
    const signature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(JSON.stringify(body)).digest('hex')}`;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('x-hub-signature-256', signature).send(body).expect(200);
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, direction: 'IN' } })).toBe(1);
  });

  it('still applies PALAVRA_CHAVE to the first inbound after a manual open', async () => {
    const cookie = await login(app, 'manual-open@example.test');
    const bot = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: {
      mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'manual-open-keyword',
      publishedTypebotId: 'manual-open-keyword-published', name: 'Manual keyword',
      editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/manual-keyword',
    } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'PALAVRA_CHAVE', value: 'ajuda', buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });
    const contact = '5584999988002';
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations/open`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'manual-open').send({ telefone: contact }).expect(201);
    const body = { entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: {
      metadata: { phone_number_id: resource },
      messages: [{ id: 'manual-open-keyword-inbound', from: contact, timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), type: 'text', text: { body: 'preciso de ajuda' } }],
    } }] }] };
    const signature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(JSON.stringify(body)).digest('hex')}`;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('x-hub-signature-256', signature).send(body).expect(200);
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

### api-app/test/historico-atividades.e2e-spec.ts — 7919 bytes SHA256 529cfad68489bcb6b2d1368505d58f9c4f1e2a18deac8074b86b0204d7f427b1

```text

import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { criarMantenedora, criarUsuario, limparBanco, login, subirApp, criarConversaFixture } from './helpers';

describe('histórico de atividades (ADR-0020 e 0022)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let A: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let B: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let cookie: string;

  const req = () => request(app.getHttpServer());
  const post = (c: string, url: string, body: object) =>
    req().post(url).set('Cookie', c).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send(body);
  const listar = (c: string, q = '') => req().get(`/api/bff/historico/atividades${q}`).set('Cookie', c);

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  });
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    A = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Alfa', 'dono@alfa.test');
    B = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Beta', 'dono@beta.test');
    cookie = await login(app, 'dono@alfa.test');
  });

  describe('o CHECK torna o vazamento IRREPRESENTÁVEL (ADR-0020 §1.1)', () => {
    it('escopo BU sem bu_id falha NO BANCO, não no serviço', async () => {
      await expect(
        prisma.$executeRaw`
          INSERT INTO activity_log (id, mantenedora_id, escopo, categoria, entidade, texto, action)
          VALUES (gen_random_uuid()::text, ${A.mantenedora.id}, 'BU', 'CRIAR', 'SETOR', 'x', 'x')
        `,
      ).rejects.toThrow();
    });

    it('escopo CONTA COM bu_id falha pelo mesmo CHECK', async () => {
      await expect(
        prisma.$executeRaw`
          INSERT INTO activity_log (id, mantenedora_id, bu_id, escopo, categoria, entidade, texto, action)
          VALUES (gen_random_uuid()::text, ${A.mantenedora.id}, ${A.businessUnit.id}, 'CONTA', 'CRIAR', 'SETOR', 'x', 'x')
        `,
      ).rejects.toThrow();
    });
  });

  describe('a escrita acontece dentro da transação', () => {
    it('criar setor grava EXATAMENTE uma linha, com a frase pronta', async () => {
      await post(cookie, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Comercial' }).expect(201);

      const linhas = await prisma.activityLog.findMany({ where: { categoria: 'CRIAR', entidade: 'SETOR' } });
      expect(linhas).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(linhas[0].texto).toBe('criou o setor Comercial');
      expect(linhas[0].escopo).toBe('CONTA');
      expect(linhas[0].buId).toBeNull();
    });

    it('falhar a transação NÃO deixa linha', async () => {
      await post(cookie, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Comercial' }).expect(201);
      const antes = await prisma.activityLog.count();

      // Nome duplicado: a transação inteira é desfeita.
      await post(cookie, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Comercial' }).expect(409);

      expect(await prisma.activityLog.count()).toBe(antes);
    });

    it('mover número grava MOVER com de/para preenchidos', async () => {
      const c = (await post(cookie, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Comercial' })).body.id;
      const s = (await post(cookie, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Suporte' })).body.id;
      const bu = (await post(cookie, '/api/bff/business-units', { nome: 'Num', setorId: c })).body.id;

      await post(cookie, `/api/bff/business-units/${bu}/setor`, { setorId: s }).expect(201);

      const l = await prisma.activityLog.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { categoria: 'MOVER' } });
      expect(l.escopo).toBe('BU');
      expect(l.buId).toBe(bu);
      expect(l.de).toBe('Comercial');
      expect(l.para).toBe('Suporte');
      expect(l.texto).toBe('moveu o número Num do setor Comercial para Suporte');
    });
  });

  describe('a frase é CONGELADA — cita o nome que era verdade naquele dia', () => {
    it('renomear o setor NÃO altera nenhuma frase já gravada', async () => {
      const id = (await post(cookie, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Comercial' })).body.id;

      await req()
        .patch(`/api/bff/setores/${id}`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie)
        .set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .send({ nome: 'Vendas' })
        .expect(200);

      const criacao = await prisma.activityLog.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { categoria: 'CRIAR', entidade: 'SETOR' } });
      expect(criacao.texto).toBe('criou o setor Comercial');
    });
  });

  describe('leitura — o trio (ADR-0012 §2)', () => {
    it('autorizado: ADMIN lê, mais recente primeiro', async () => {
      await post(cookie, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Comercial' }).expect(201);
      const r = await listar(cookie).expect(200);
      expect(r.body.atividades[0].texto).toBe('criou o setor Comercial');
      expect(r.body.total).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    });

    it('papel insuficiente: ATENDENTE recebe 403 — gravar é do produto, ler é de quem administra', async () => {
      await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'atendente@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      const c = await login(app, 'atendente@alfa.test');
      const r = await listar(c).expect(403);
      expect(r.body.code).toBe('PAPEL_INSUFICIENTE');
    });

    it('cross-tenant: o ADMIN de A não vê NENHUMA linha de B', async () => {
      const cookieB = await login(app, 'dono@beta.test');
      await post(cookieB, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Só da Beta' }).expect(201);

      const r = await listar(cookie).expect(200);
      const textos = r.body.atividades.map((a: { texto: string }) => a.texto);
      expect(textos.some((t: string) => t.includes('Só da Beta'))).toBe(false);
      expect(await prisma.activityLog.count({ where: { mantenedoraId: B.mantenedora.id } })).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    });

    it('filtra por categoria e por entidade', async () => {
      await post(cookie, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Comercial' }).expect(201);
      const r = await listar(cookie, '?categoria=MOVER').expect(200);
      expect(r.body.atividades).toHaveLength(0);
    });
  });

  describe('o que NÃO entra', () => {
    it('trocar a própria senha não gera linha', async () => {
      const antes = await prisma.activityLog.count();
      await post(cookie, '/api/bff/me/password', { atual: 'senha-de-teste-1', nova: 'outra-senha-99' }).expect(200);
      expect(await prisma.activityLog.count()).toBe(antes);
    });

    it('listar e filtrar não geram linha — leitura não registra', async () => {
      const antes = await prisma.activityLog.count();
      await req().get('/api/bff/setores').set('Cookie', cookie).expect(200);
      await listar(cookie).expect(200);
      expect(await prisma.activityLog.count()).toBe(antes);
    });
  });

  describe('exportação é a exceção declarada (ADR-0022 §3)', () => {
    it('exportar CSV grava EXPORTACAO com filtros e quantidade no detalhe', async () => {
      const conversa = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId: A.businessUnit.id, contact: '5511999998888', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED' });
      await prisma.message.create({
        data: {
          conversationId: conversa.id,
          buId: A.businessUnit.id,
          direction: 'IN',
          messageType: 'TEXTO',
          content: 'oi',
          channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL',
          status: 'RECEBIDA',
        },
      });

      await req()
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico/csv?contato=5511999998888`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie)
        .expect(200);

      const l = await prisma.activityLog.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { categoria: 'EXPORTACAO' } });
      expect(l.escopo).toBe('BU');
      expect(l.buId).toBe(A.businessUnit.id);
      expect(l.texto).toContain('exportou');
      expect(l.detalhe).toMatchObject({ formato: 'CSV', filtros: { contato: '5511999998888' } });
    });
  });
});
```

### api-app/test/historico.e2e-spec.ts — 8491 bytes SHA256 2053df9de7e420b853cd41d457f0cdeb90d183b6fe4c423e98831c29e22c8453

```text

import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { criarMantenedora, criarUsuario, limparBanco, login, subirApp, criarConversaFixture } from './helpers';

describe('histórico (Consultar)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let A: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let B: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let cookie: string;

  const CONTATO = '5584911112222';
  const OUTRO = '5584933334444';

  /** Semeia N mensagens alternando entrada e saída, com datas crescentes. */
  const semear = async (buId: string, contato: string, quantas: number) => {
    const conversa = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId, contact: contato, contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'CLOSED', lastMessageAt: new Date() });
    for (let i = 0; i < quantas; i++) {
      await prisma.message.create({
        data: {
          conversationId: conversa.id,
          buId,
          direction: i % 2 === 0 ? 'IN' : 'OUT',
          messageType: 'TEXTO',
          content: `mensagem ${i + 1}`,
          channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL',
          status: i % 2 === 0 ? 'RECEBIDA' : 'ENVIADA',
          authorName: i % 2 === 0 ? null : 'Atendente',
          createdAt: new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 0, 1, 0, i)),
        },
      });
    }
    return conversa;
  };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    A = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Alfa', 'dono@alfa.test');
    B = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Beta', 'dono@beta.test');
    await prisma.businessUnit.update({ where: { id: A.businessUnit.id }, data: { phone: '5584900000000' } });
    await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: A.businessUnit.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: 'WHATSAPP_RESOURCE', scopeReference: 'BU',
      externalResourceId: '5584900000000',
    } });
    cookie = await login(app, 'dono@alfa.test');
  });

  describe('o par número → número', () => {
    it('a resposta traz os DOIS números do par, não só o contato', async () => {
      await semear(A.businessUnit.id, CONTATO, 3);
      const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie)
        .expect(200);

      expect(res.body.numeroDaBu).toBe('5584900000000');
      expect(res.body.pares[0].contato).toBe(CONTATO);
    });

    it('filtra pelo número do contato', async () => {
      await semear(A.businessUnit.id, CONTATO, 2);
      await semear(A.businessUnit.id, OUTRO, 2);

      const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico?contato=933334444`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie)
        .expect(200);

      expect(res.body.pares).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(res.body.pares[0].contato).toBe(OUTRO);
    });
  });

  describe('paginação — 20 é o corpo da página, não o tamanho do que existe', () => {
    it('devolve as ÚLTIMAS 20 e avisa que há mais', async () => {
      await semear(A.businessUnit.id, CONTATO, 25);

      const p1 = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico/mensagens/${CONTATO}`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie)
        .expect(200);

      expect(p1.body.mensagens).toHaveLength(20);
      expect(p1.body.total).toBe(25);
      expect(p1.body.temMais).toBe(true);
      // As últimas primeiro: a 25ª é a mais recente.
      expect(p1.body.mensagens[0].texto).toBe('mensagem 25');
    });

    it('a segunda página traz o resto e fecha', async () => {
      await semear(A.businessUnit.id, CONTATO, 25);

      const p2 = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico/mensagens/${CONTATO}?pagina=2`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie)
        .expect(200);

      expect(p2.body.mensagens).toHaveLength(5);
      expect(p2.body.temMais).toBe(false);
      expect(p2.body.mensagens.at(-1).texto).toBe('mensagem 1');
    });

    it('a lista de pares também pagina', async () => {
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) await semear(A.businessUnit.id, `55849111122${i}${i}`, 1);
      const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico?por_pagina=2`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie)
        .expect(200);

      expect(res.body.pares).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(res.body.total).toBe(3);
      expect(res.body.temMais).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  describe('consultar não é atender', () => {
    it('ler o histórico NÃO zera as não lidas', async () => {
      const conversa = await semear(A.businessUnit.id, CONTATO, 3);
      await prisma.conversation.update({ where: { id: conversa.id }, data: { unreadCount: 3 } });

      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico/mensagens/${CONTATO}`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie)
        .expect(200);

      const depois = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversa.id } });
      expect(depois.unreadCount).toBe(3);
    });
  });

  describe('CSV', () => {
    it('usa os mesmos filtros e sai uma linha por mensagem', async () => {
      await semear(A.businessUnit.id, CONTATO, 3);
      await semear(A.businessUnit.id, OUTRO, 2);

      const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico/csv?contato=${CONTATO}`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie)
        .expect(200);

      expect(res.headers['content-type']).toContain('text/csv');
      const linhas = res.text.trim().split('\n');
      expect(linhas[0]).toContain('numero_da_bu;contato');
      expect(linhas).toHaveLength(4); // cabeçalho + 3 mensagens
      expect(linhas[1]).toContain('5584900000000');
    });

    it('escapa texto com ponto e vírgula, aspas e quebra de linha', async () => {
      const conversa = await semear(A.businessUnit.id, CONTATO, 0);
      await prisma.message.create({
        data: {
          conversationId: conversa.id, buId: A.businessUnit.id, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO',
          content: 'oi; "tudo bem"\nsegunda linha', channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA',
        },
      });

      const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico/csv`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie)
        .expect(200);

      expect(res.text).toContain('"oi; ""tudo bem""');
    });
  });

  describe('permissão — o trio (ADR-0012 §2)', () => {
    it('autorizado: ATENDENTE no escopo consulta — o grant é a permissão (ADR-0018)', async () => {
      await semear(A.businessUnit.id, CONTATO, 2);
      await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'atendente@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      const cookie = await login(app, 'atendente@alfa.test');

      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie)
        .expect(200);
    });

    // O teste de CAPACIDADE_AUSENTE morreu com o eixo de capacidades (ADR-0018
    // §3). O que restringe hoje é o escopo, e ele é provado pelo cross-tenant
    // logo abaixo.

    it('CROSS-TENANT: o dono de Beta recebe 404 no histórico da BU de Alfa', async () => {
      await semear(A.businessUnit.id, CONTATO, 2);
      const cookieBeta = await login(app, 'dono@beta.test');

      const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieBeta)
        .expect(404);
      expect(res.body.code).toBe('NAO_ENCONTRADO');
      expect(B.businessUnit.id).toBeTruthy();
    });

    it('CROSS-TENANT: o CSV também é barrado, e não vaza no corpo', async () => {
      await semear(A.businessUnit.id, CONTATO, 2);
      const cookieBeta = await login(app, 'dono@beta.test');

      const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/historico/csv`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieBeta)
        .expect(404);
      expect(res.text).not.toContain(CONTATO);
    });
  });
});
```

```text
import request from 'supertest';
import { createHmac, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { resolveOrCreateContactIdentity, whatsappPeerAddress } from '../src/domain/inbox/contact-identity';
import {
  criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, instagramFake, INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET, META_APP_SECRET,
  limparBanco, login, subirApp, typebotFake,
} from './helpers';

describe('Instagram consumer isolation (real Nest and PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
    await limparBanco(prisma);
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  });

  it('keeps history, legacy channel projection, public v1, and Typebot on canonical WhatsApp routes when two Instagram accounts use the same numeric peer', async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram consumer isolation', 'instagram-consumer-isolation@example.test');
    const cookie = await login(app, 'instagram-consumer-isolation@example.test');
    const peer = '5584991234567';
    const wa = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', 'synthetic-consumer-wa-provider', {
      provedBusinessPhone: '5584999990000',
    });
    const waBusiness = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: wa.channel.businessIdentityId } });
    const legacyDisplayPhone = '5584000000001';
    await prisma.businessUnit.update({ where: { id: tenant.businessUnit.id }, data: { phone: legacyDisplayPhone } });
    const waContact = await prisma.$transaction((tx) => resolveOrCreateContactIdentity(tx, whatsappPeerAddress({
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, externalPeerId: peer, contactType: 'PESSOA', displayName: 'WA peer',
    })));
    const waConversation = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: waContact.contactId, contact: peer, contactType: 'PESSOA',
      displayName: 'WA peer', unreadCount: 1,
    } });
    await prisma.conversationRoute.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, conversationId: waConversation.id, contactId: waContact.contactId,
      contactIdentityId: waContact.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP', contactScopeReference: waContact.scopeReference,
      businessScopeReference: waBusiness.scopeReference, businessIdentityId: waBusiness.id,
    } });
    await prisma.message.create({ data: {
      conversationId: waConversation.id, buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO',
      content: 'Mensagem legítima WhatsApp', channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA',
      externalId: 'synthetic-consumer-wa-in', providerResourceIdentityId: wa.identity.id, officialInboundAt: new Date(),
    } });

    typebotFake.enabled = true;
    const bot = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: {
      mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'consumer-isolation-bot', publishedTypebotId: 'consumer-isolation-published',
      status: 'ATIVO', name: 'Consumer isolation', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/consumer-isolation',
    } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });
    const startsBeforeIg = typebotFake.starts;
    const sessionsBeforeIg = await prisma.typebotSession.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });

    const accounts = [
      { id: 'synthetic-consumer-ig-account-a', username: 'consumer_account_a' },
      { id: 'synthetic-consumer-ig-account-b', username: 'consumer_account_b' },
    ];
    const signatures = (event: string) => `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET).update(event).digest('hex')}`;
    for (const account of accounts) {
      instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: account.id, username: account.username };
      const reservation = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/instagram-authorizations`)
        .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', randomUUID())
        .send({ operation: 'CONNECT' }).expect(201);
      const state = new URL(reservation.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state');
      expect(state).toBeTruthy();
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback').set('cookie', cookie)
        .query({ state, code: `synthetic-consumer-code-${account.id}` }).expect(303);
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${reservation.body.id}/confirm`)
        .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({}).expect(200);

      const event = JSON.stringify({ object: 'instagram', entry: [{ id: account.id, messaging: [{
        sender: { id: peer }, recipient: { id: account.id }, timestamp: Date.now(),
        message: { mid: `synthetic-consumer-in-${account.id}`, text: `Mensagem Instagram ${account.username}` },
      }] }] });
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/instagram').set('content-type', 'application/json')
        .set('x-hub-signature-256', signatures(event)).send(event).expect(200);
    }

    const igRoutes = await prisma.conversationRoute.findMany({
      where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', contactIdentity: { externalPeerId: peer } },
      include: { businessIdentity: true, contactIdentity: true, conversation: true },
      orderBy: { businessIdentity: { externalResourceId: 'asc' } },
    });
    expect(igRoutes).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(new Set(igRoutes.map((route) => route.contactId)).size).toBe(2);
    expect(new Set(igRoutes.map((route) => route.businessIdentity.externalResourceId))).toEqual(new Set(accounts.map((account) => account.id)));
    expect(igRoutes.every((route) => route.contactId !== waContact.contactId)).toBe(true);

    const history = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/historico`)
      .set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    expect(history.body.total).toBe(1);
    expect(history.body.numeroDaBu).toBe(waBusiness.externalResourceId);
    expect(history.body.numeroDaBu).not.toBe(legacyDisplayPhone);
    expect(history.body.pares.map((row: { conversaId: string }) => row.conversaId)).toEqual([waConversation.id]);
    const historyThread = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/historico/mensagens/${peer}`)
      .set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    expect(historyThread.body.mensagens.map((message: { texto: string }) => message.texto)).toEqual(['Mensagem legítima WhatsApp']);
    const csv = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/historico/csv`)
      .set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    expect(csv.text).toContain('Mensagem legítima WhatsApp');
    expect(csv.text).not.toContain('Mensagem Instagram');
    expect(csv.text).toContain(waBusiness.externalResourceId);
    expect(csv.text).not.toContain(legacyDisplayPhone);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: waConversation.id } })).toMatchObject({ unreadCount: 1 });

    const legacyProjection = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}`)
      .set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    expect(legacyProjection.body.canais.map((channel: { type: string }) => channel.type)).toEqual(['OFICIAL']);
    expect(legacyProjection.body.canais).toHaveLength(1);
    const legacyList = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/business-units').set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    const listedBusinessUnit = legacyList.body.businessUnits.find((businessUnit: { id: string }) => businessUnit.id === tenant.businessUnit.id);
    expect(listedBusinessUnit.channels.map((channel: { type: string }) => channel.type)).toEqual(['OFICIAL']);

    const keyResponse = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/bff/integracao/api-keys')
      .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ nome: 'Consumer isolation', buIds: [] }).expect(201);
    const authorization = { Authorization: `Bearer ${keyResponse.body.chave as string}` };
    const accountsResponse = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/v1/accounts').set(authorization).expect(200);
    expect(accountsResponse.body.accounts).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(accountsResponse.body.accounts[0]).toMatchObject({ id: tenant.businessUnit.id, numero: waBusiness.externalResourceId });
    expect(accountsResponse.body.accounts[0].canais.map((channel: { tipo: string }) => channel.tipo)).toEqual(['OFICIAL']);
    const publicList = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/v1/conversations?account_id=${tenant.businessUnit.id}`)
      .set(authorization).expect(200);
    expect(publicList.body.conversations).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(publicList.body.conversations[0].telefone).toBe(peer);
    const publicThread = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/v1/conversations/${peer}/messages?account_id=${tenant.businessUnit.id}`)
      .set(authorization).expect(200);
    expect(publicThread.body.messages.map((message: { texto?: string; text?: string }) => message.text ?? message.texto)).toContain('Mensagem legítima WhatsApp');
    expect(JSON.stringify(publicThread.body)).not.toContain('Mensagem Instagram');
    const instagramConversationStates = await Promise.all(igRoutes.map(async (route) => ({
      id: route.conversation.id,
      status: (await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: route.conversation.id } })).status,
    })));
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/v1/conversations/${peer}/claim`).set(authorization)
      .send({ account_id: tenant.businessUnit.id, atendente: 'Synthetic partner' }).expect(201);
    expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: waConversation.id } })).status).toBe('IN_SERVICE');
    expect(await Promise.all(instagramConversationStates.map(async (route) =>
      (await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: route.id } })).status))).toEqual(instagramConversationStates.map((route) => route.status));
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/v1/conversations/${peer}/release`).set(authorization)
      .send({ account_id: tenant.businessUnit.id }).expect(201);
    expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: waConversation.id } })).status).toBe('QUEUED');

    const igPeerMessageCount = await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } });
    expect(igPeerMessageCount).toBe(2);
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(startsBeforeIg);
    expect(await prisma.typebotSession.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(sessionsBeforeIg);
    expect(typebotFake.continues).toBe(0);
    expect(typebotFake.startedBots).toHaveLength(0);

    // Signed Instagram ingress now uses IncomingService for common acceptance and
    // creation-winner publication. This scenario keeps the Typebot isolation proof;
    // the CP4 realtime case owns the positive common-event and signed WhatsApp control.
    const waWebhook = JSON.stringify({ entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: {
      metadata: { phone_number_id: 'synthetic-consumer-wa-provider' },
      contacts: [{ profile: { name: 'Typebot WhatsApp control' } }],
      messages: [{ id: 'synthetic-consumer-wa-typebot-control', from: '5584997654321', timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), type: 'text', text: { body: 'ajuda' } }],
    } }] }] });
    // Keep a positive WhatsApp automation control on the ordinary Meta webhook boundary.
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(waWebhook).digest('hex')}`)
      .send(waWebhook).expect(200);
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(startsBeforeIg + 1);
    expect(typebotFake.startedBots[0]).toMatchObject({ botId: 'consumer-isolation-published', contact: '5584997654321' });
    expect(await prisma.typebotSession.count({ where: { botId: bot.id, buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(1);
  });
});
```
