### api-app/test/instagram-cp4-display.e2e-spec.ts — 39319 bytes SHA256 875e4b7251c403f5ea563390ed6da7fd7b3f5bff0ee9c9a612c991efe449a675

```text

import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import request from 'supertest';
import { createHmac, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { ChannelCredentialResolver } from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-resolver';
import * as channelCredentialCrypto from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-crypto';
import {
  criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET, limparBanco, login, META_APP_SECRET,
  instagramFake, metaFake, evolutionFake, subirApp,
} from './helpers';

type Display = {
  platform: 'WHATSAPP' | 'INSTAGRAM' | null;
  recipientLabel: string;
  recipientPhone: string | null;
  accountLabel: string | null;
  instagramUsername: string | null;
};
type ListedConversation = {
  id: string; contact: string; unreadCount: number; display?: Display;
};

const domainStatements = (events: Array<{ query: string; params: string }>) => events.filter(({ query }) =>
  !/^\s*(?:BEGIN|START\s+TRANSACTION|COMMIT|ROLLBACK|SAVEPOINT|RELEASE|SET\s+TRANSACTION)\b/i.test(query));
const assertBoundedReadOnly = (events: Array<{ query: string; params: string }>, maximum = 8) => {
  const statements = domainStatements(events);
  expect(statements.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(maximum);
  expect(statements.some(({ query }) => /\b(?:INSERT|UPDATE|DELETE|MERGE|TRUNCATE)\b/i.test(query))).toBe(false);
};
const assertReadOnly = (events: Array<{ query: string; params: string }>) => {
  expect(domainStatements(events).some(({ query }) => /\b(?:INSERT|UPDATE|DELETE|MERGE|TRUNCATE)\b/i.test(query))).toBe(false);
};
const providerCallCounts = () => [instagramFake.calls.length, metaFake.providerCalls, evolutionFake.providerCalls];
const observeMetadataOnly = async <T>(app: INestApplication, read: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {
  const decrypt = vi.spyOn(channelCredentialCrypto, 'openChannelCredential');
  const resolve = vi.spyOn(app.get(ChannelCredentialResolver), 'resolve');
  try {
    const result = await read();
    expect(decrypt).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(resolve).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    return result;
  } finally {
    resolve.mockRestore();
    decrypt.mockRestore();
  }
};

describe('Instagram CP4 safe conversation display (real Nest, sessions and PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let tenant: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let cookie = '';
  const queryEvents: Array<{ query: string; params: string }> = [];

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp(undefined, true));
    prisma.$on('query', (event) => queryEvents.push(event));
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    queryEvents.length = 0;
    tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP4 safe display', `instagram-cp4-display-${randomUUID()}@example.test`);
    cookie = await login(app, tenant.owner.email);
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = null;
    metaFake.wabaRecursos = [];
  });

  afterAll(async () => { if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma); await app?.close(); });

  const authorize = async (
    accountId: string, username: string, operation: 'CONNECT' | 'REAUTHORIZE' = 'CONNECT',
    channelId?: string, expectedGeneration?: number,
  ) => {
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: accountId, username };
    const payload = operation === 'CONNECT' ? { operation } : { operation, channelId, expectedGeneration };
    const reservation = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/instagram-authorizations`)
      .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send(payload).expect(201);
    const state = new URL(reservation.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state');
    expect(state).toBeTruthy();
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback').set('cookie', cookie)
      .query({ state, code: `synthetic-cp4-display-${randomUUID()}` }).expect(303);
    const body = operation === 'CONNECT' ? {} : { expectedGeneration };
    return request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${reservation.body.id}/confirm`)
      .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send(body).then(async (response) => {
        return { reservation: reservation.body, response, attempt: await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: reservation.body.id } }) };
      });
  };

  const signedInstagramBatch = (accountId: string, messages: Array<{ peer: string; id: string; text: string }>) => {
    const body = JSON.stringify({ object: 'instagram', entry: [{ id: accountId, messaging: messages.map(({ peer, id, text }) => ({
      sender: { id: peer }, recipient: { id: accountId }, timestamp: Date.now(), message: { mid: id, text },
    })) }] });
    const signature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET).update(body).digest('hex')}`;
    return request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/instagram').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', signature).send(body);
  };

  const list = (pagina: number, limite: number) => request(app.getHttpServer())
    .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations`).set('cookie', cookie).query({ pagina, limite });

  it('projects two same-peer IG accounts safely across a real 50-row paged inbox and current credential mapping', async () => {
    const accountA = `synthetic-cp4-display-a-${randomUUID()}`;
    const accountB = `synthetic-cp4-display-b-${randomUUID()}`;
    const sharedPeer = `opaque-cp4-display-shared-${randomUUID()}`;
    await authorize(accountA, 'display_account_a').then(({ response }) => expect(response.status).toBe(200));
    await authorize(accountB, 'display_account_b').then(({ response }) => expect(response.status).toBe(200));

    const peersA = [sharedPeer, ...Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, index) => `opaque-cp4-a-peer-${index}-${randomUUID()}`)];
    const peersB = [sharedPeer, ...Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, index) => `opaque-cp4-b-peer-${index}-${randomUUID()}`)];
    await signedInstagramBatch(accountA, peersA.map((peer, index) => ({ peer, id: `synthetic-cp4-display-a-${index}-${randomUUID()}`, text: `A message ${index}` }))).expect(200);
    await signedInstagramBatch(accountB, peersB.map((peer, index) => ({ peer, id: `synthetic-cp4-display-b-${index}-${randomUUID()}`, text: `B message ${index}` }))).expect(200);

    const businessIdentityA = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({
      where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', externalResourceId: accountA },
    });
    const businessIdentityB = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({
      where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', externalResourceId: accountB },
    });
    const routesA = await prisma.conversationRoute.findMany({
      where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM',
        contactIdentity: { platform: 'INSTAGRAM', namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', scopeReference: businessIdentityA.scopeReference },
        businessIdentity: { id: businessIdentityA.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', externalResourceId: accountA } },
      include: { conversation: true, contactIdentity: true, businessIdentity: true },
    });
    const routesB = await prisma.conversationRoute.findMany({
      where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM',
        contactIdentity: { platform: 'INSTAGRAM', namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', scopeReference: businessIdentityB.scopeReference },
        businessIdentity: { id: businessIdentityB.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', externalResourceId: accountB } },
      include: { conversation: true, contactIdentity: true, businessIdentity: true },
    });
    expect(routesA).toHaveLength(25);
    expect(routesB).toHaveLength(25);
    const sharedA = routesA.find((route) => route.contactIdentity.externalPeerId === sharedPeer
      && route.businessIdentity.externalResourceId === accountA);
    const sharedB = routesB.find((route) => route.contactIdentity.externalPeerId === sharedPeer
      && route.businessIdentity.externalResourceId === accountB);
    if (!sharedA || !sharedB) throw new Error('Expected both account-qualified routes for the shared peer.');
    const originalSharedRouteA = await prisma.conversationRoute.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: sharedA.id } });
    const originalSharedRouteB = await prisma.conversationRoute.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: sharedB.id } });
    expect(sharedA.conversationId).not.toBe(sharedB.conversationId);
    expect(sharedA.contactId).not.toBe(sharedB.contactId);
    expect(routesA.every((route) => route.conversation.unreadCount === 1)).toBe(true);
    expect(routesB.every((route) => route.conversation.unreadCount === 1)).toBe(true);

    const providerCallsBeforeRead = providerCallCounts();
    const pageIds: string[] = [];
    for (const page of [1, 2, 3]) {
      const start = queryEvents.length;
      const result = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => list(page, 20).expect(200));
      assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(start));
      expect(result.body.total).toBe(50);
      expect(result.body.pagina).toBe(page);
      expect(result.body.temMais).toBe(page < 3);
      const rows = result.body.conversations as ListedConversation[];
      expect(rows).toHaveLength(page < 3 ? 20 : 10);
      expect(rows.every((row) => row.display && row.display.platform === 'INSTAGRAM'
        && typeof row.display.recipientLabel === 'string' && row.display.recipientPhone === null
        && typeof row.display.accountLabel === 'string')).toBe(true);
      pageIds.push(...rows.map((row) => row.id));
    }
    expect(new Set(pageIds).size).toBe(50);
    const fullPageStart = queryEvents.length;
    const fullPage = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => list(1, 50).expect(200));
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(fullPageStart));
    expect(fullPage.body.conversations).toHaveLength(50);
    const serializedList = JSON.stringify(fullPage.body);
    expect(serializedList).not.toContain(accountA);
    expect(serializedList).not.toContain(accountB);
    expect(serializedList).not.toContain(sharedPeer);
    expect(serializedList).not.toMatch(/synthetic-(?:long-lived|short)-token|ciphertext|candidateNonce|accessToken/i);
    expect(providerCallCounts()).toEqual(providerCallsBeforeRead);

    const rowA = (fullPage.body.conversations as ListedConversation[]).find((row) => row.id === sharedA.conversationId)!;
    const rowB = (fullPage.body.conversations as ListedConversation[]).find((row) => row.id === sharedB.conversationId)!;
    expect((fullPage.body.conversations as ListedConversation[]).every((row) => row.unreadCount === 1)).toBe(true);
    expect(rowA.display!.instagramUsername).toBe('display_account_a');
    expect(rowB.display!.instagramUsername).toBe('display_account_b');
    expect(rowA.display!.recipientLabel).not.toBe(sharedPeer);
    expect(rowB.display!.recipientLabel).not.toBe(sharedPeer);
    expect(rowA.display!.recipientPhone).toBeNull();
    expect(rowB.display!.recipientPhone).toBeNull();

    const channelA = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({
      where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'INSTAGRAM', businessIdentityId: sharedA.businessIdentityId }, include: { credential: true },
    });
    const wrongAccountId = `synthetic-cp4-display-wrong-${randomUUID()}`;
    const catalogBeforeWrongStart = queryEvents.length;
    const catalogBeforeWrong = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channel-connections`).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(catalogBeforeWrongStart));
    const catalogAccountBefore = (catalogBeforeWrong.body.connections as Array<{ channelId: string; display: Display }>)
      .find(({ channelId }) => channelId === channelA.id);
    if (!catalogAccountBefore) throw new Error('Expected the original Instagram account in the successful connection catalog.');
    expect(catalogAccountBefore.display.instagramUsername).toBe('display_account_a');
    const rotation = await authorize(accountA, 'display_account_a_rotated', 'REAUTHORIZE', channelA.id, channelA.generation);
    expect(rotation.response.status).toBe(200);
    expect(rotation.attempt.mappedCredentialVersion).not.toBeNull();
    const wrong = await authorize(wrongAccountId, 'wrong_display_candidate', 'REAUTHORIZE', channelA.id, channelA.generation);
    expect(wrong.response.status).toBe(409);
    const catalogAfterWrongStart = queryEvents.length;
    const catalogAfterWrong = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channel-connections`).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(catalogAfterWrongStart));
    const catalogAccountAfter = (catalogAfterWrong.body.connections as Array<{ channelId: string; display: Display }>)
      .find(({ channelId }) => channelId === channelA.id);
    if (!catalogAccountAfter) throw new Error('Expected the original Instagram account after rejecting a wrong OAuth candidate.');
    expect(catalogAccountAfter.display.instagramUsername).toBe('display_account_a_rotated');
    expect(JSON.stringify(catalogAfterWrong.body)).not.toContain('wrong_display_candidate');
    expect(JSON.stringify(catalogAfterWrong.body)).not.toContain(wrongAccountId);
    const currentMapped = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findFirstOrThrow({
      where: { channelId: channelA.id, professionalUserId: accountA, mappedCredentialVersion: rotation.attempt.mappedCredentialVersion },
    });
    expect(currentMapped.username).toBe('display_account_a_rotated');
    const cancelledMapped = await request(app.getHttpServer())
      .post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${currentMapped.id}/cancel`).set('cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({}).expect(200);
    expect(cancelledMapped.body).toMatchObject({ id: currentMapped.id, buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, status: 'CANCELLED' });
    const cancelledAttempt = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: currentMapped.id } });
    expect(cancelledAttempt).toMatchObject({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, channelId: channelA.id,
      professionalUserId: accountA, username: 'display_account_a_rotated', confirmationState: 'COMPLETED',
      mappedCredentialVersion: rotation.attempt.mappedCredentialVersion, status: 'CANCELLED' });
    const providerCallsAfterCancel = providerCallCounts();
    const catalogAfterCancelStart = queryEvents.length;
    const catalogAfterCancel = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channel-connections`).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(catalogAfterCancelStart));
    const cancelledCatalogAccount = (catalogAfterCancel.body.connections as Array<{ channelId: string; display: Display }>)
      .find(({ channelId }) => channelId === channelA.id);
    if (!cancelledCatalogAccount) throw new Error('Expected a cancelled completed mapping in the safe connection catalog.');
    expect(cancelledCatalogAccount.display).toMatchObject({ instagramUsername: 'display_account_a_rotated' });
    expect(providerCallCounts()).toEqual(providerCallsAfterCancel);

    const routeB = await prisma.conversationRoute.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: sharedB.id } });
    const channelB = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({
      where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'INSTAGRAM', businessIdentityId: routeB.businessIdentityId },
    });
    const currentAttemptB = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findFirstOrThrow({
      where: { channelId: channelB.id, professionalUserId: accountB, mappedCredentialVersion: { not: null } },
      orderBy: { mappedCredentialVersion: 'desc' },
    });
    await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.update({ where: { id: currentAttemptB.id }, data: { username: null } });
    const providerCallsAfterLifecycle = providerCallsAfterCancel;

    const listAfterRotationStart = queryEvents.length;
    const afterRotation = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => list(1, 50).expect(200));
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(listAfterRotationStart));
    const rotatedA = (afterRotation.body.conversations as ListedConversation[]).find((row) => row.id === sharedA.conversationId)!;
    const missingNameB = (afterRotation.body.conversations as ListedConversation[]).find((row) => row.id === sharedB.conversationId)!;
    expect(rotatedA.display!.instagramUsername).toBe('display_account_a_rotated');
    expect(rotatedA.display!.accountLabel).toContain('display_account_a_rotated');
    expect(missingNameB.display!.instagramUsername).toBeNull();
    expect(missingNameB.display!.accountLabel).not.toContain('display_account_b');

    const threadStart = queryEvents.length;
    const thread = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${sharedA.conversationId}/messages`)
      .query({ marcarLida: 'false' }).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
    const threadDomainSqlCount = domainStatements(queryEvents.slice(threadStart)).length;
    expect(threadDomainSqlCount).toEqual(expect.any(Number));
    assertReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(threadStart));
    expect(thread.body.conversa.display).toMatchObject({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', recipientPhone: null, instagramUsername: 'display_account_a_rotated' });
    expect(thread.body.conversa.display.recipientLabel).not.toBe(sharedPeer);
    const replyStart = queryEvents.length;
    const replyOptions = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${sharedA.conversationId}/reply-options`).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
    const replyOptionsDomainSqlCount = domainStatements(queryEvents.slice(replyStart)).length;
    expect(replyOptionsDomainSqlCount).toEqual(expect.any(Number));
    assertReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(replyStart));
    expect(JSON.stringify(replyOptions.body)).not.toMatch(/synthetic-(?:long-lived|short)-token|ciphertext|accessToken/i);
    expect(replyOptions.body.schemaVersion).toBe(2);
    const igReplyOption = (replyOptions.body.options as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).find((option) => option.channelId === channelA.id)!;
    expect(igReplyOption).toMatchObject({ schemaVersion: 2, channelId: channelA.id, generation: channelA.generation });
    expect(igReplyOption.accountLabel).toContain('display_account_a_rotated');
    expect(igReplyOption.recipientLabel).not.toBe(sharedPeer);
    expect(igReplyOption.routeId).toBe(sharedA.id);
    expect(igReplyOption.contextFingerprint).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i);
    expect(providerCallCounts()).toEqual(providerCallsAfterLifecycle);

    const ambiguousPeerA = `opaque-cp4-ambiguous-a-${randomUUID()}`;
    const ambiguousPeerB = `opaque-cp4-ambiguous-b-${randomUUID()}`;
    const ambiguousContact = await prisma.contact.create({ data: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, displayName: 'Synthetic ambiguous graph' } });
    const ambiguousConversation = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: ambiguousContact.id, contact: `opaque-cp4-legacy-${randomUUID()}`,
      contactType: 'PESSOA', displayName: 'Synthetic ambiguous graph',
    } });
    const contactIdentityA = await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: ambiguousContact.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM',
      namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', scopeReference: businessIdentityA.scopeReference, externalPeerId: ambiguousPeerA,
    } });
    const contactIdentityB = await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: ambiguousContact.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM',
      namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', scopeReference: businessIdentityB.scopeReference, externalPeerId: ambiguousPeerB,
    } });
    const businessA = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: sharedA.businessIdentityId } });
    const businessB = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: sharedB.businessIdentityId } });
    const ambiguousRouteA = await prisma.conversationRoute.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, conversationId: ambiguousConversation.id, contactId: ambiguousContact.id,
      contactIdentityId: contactIdentityA.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', contactScopeReference: businessA.scopeReference,
      businessScopeReference: businessA.scopeReference, businessIdentityId: businessA.id,
    } });
    const ambiguousRouteB = await prisma.conversationRoute.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, conversationId: ambiguousConversation.id, contactId: ambiguousContact.id,
      contactIdentityId: contactIdentityB.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', contactScopeReference: businessB.scopeReference,
      businessScopeReference: businessB.scopeReference, businessIdentityId: businessB.id,
    } });
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: sharedA.id } })).toEqual(originalSharedRouteA);
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: sharedB.id } })).toEqual(originalSharedRouteB);
    expect(await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: businessA.id } })).toEqual(businessA);
    expect(await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: businessB.id } })).toEqual(businessB);
    expect(new Set([ambiguousRouteA.businessIdentityId, ambiguousRouteB.businessIdentityId]))
      .toEqual(new Set([businessA.id, businessB.id]));

    const ambiguousListStart = queryEvents.length;
    const ambiguousList = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => list(1, 20).expect(200));
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(ambiguousListStart));
    expect(ambiguousList.body.total).toBe(51);
    const ambiguousRow = (ambiguousList.body.conversations as ListedConversation[])
      .find(({ id }) => id === ambiguousConversation.id);
    if (!ambiguousRow) throw new Error('Expected the synthetic ambiguous conversation in the paged inbox.');
    expect(ambiguousRow.display).toMatchObject({ platform: null, accountLabel: null, instagramUsername: null, recipientPhone: null });
    expect(ambiguousRow.display!.recipientLabel).not.toBe(ambiguousPeerA);
    expect(ambiguousRow.display!.recipientLabel).not.toBe(ambiguousPeerB);
    expect(ambiguousRow.display!.recipientLabel).not.toBe(ambiguousConversation.contact);
    const ambiguousThreadStart = queryEvents.length;
    const ambiguousThread = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${ambiguousConversation.id}/messages`).query({ marcarLida: 'false' }).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
    const ambiguousThreadDomainSqlCount = domainStatements(queryEvents.slice(ambiguousThreadStart)).length;
    expect(ambiguousThreadDomainSqlCount).toEqual(expect.any(Number));
    assertReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(ambiguousThreadStart));
    expect(ambiguousThread.body.conversa.display)
      .toMatchObject({ platform: null, accountLabel: null, instagramUsername: null, recipientPhone: null });
    const ambiguousOptionsStart = queryEvents.length;
    const ambiguousOptions = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${ambiguousConversation.id}/reply-options`).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
    const ambiguousOptionsDomainSqlCount = domainStatements(queryEvents.slice(ambiguousOptionsStart)).length;
    expect(ambiguousOptionsDomainSqlCount).toEqual(expect.any(Number));
    assertReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(ambiguousOptionsStart));
    expect(ambiguousOptions.body.schemaVersion).toBe(2);
    const ambiguousReplyChoices = ambiguousOptions.body.options as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
    expect(ambiguousReplyChoices.every((item) => [ambiguousRouteA.id, ambiguousRouteB.id].includes(String(item.routeId)))).toBe(true);
    if (ambiguousReplyChoices.length === 2) {
      expect(new Set(ambiguousReplyChoices.map((item) => item.routeId))).toEqual(new Set([ambiguousRouteA.id, ambiguousRouteB.id]));
      expect(new Set(ambiguousReplyChoices.map((item) => item.channelId))).toEqual(new Set([channelA.id, channelB.id]));
    }

    const orphanPeer = `opaque-cp4-unrouted-${randomUUID()}`;
    const orphanContact = await prisma.contact.create({ data: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, displayName: 'Unqualified legacy label' } });
    const orphan = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: orphanContact.id, contact: orphanPeer,
      contactType: 'PESSOA', displayName: 'Unqualified legacy label',
    } });
    const orphanThread = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${orphan.id}/messages`).query({ marcarLida: 'false' }).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
    expect(orphanThread.body.conversa.display).toMatchObject({ platform: null, recipientPhone: null, accountLabel: null, instagramUsername: null });
    expect(orphanThread.body.conversa.display.recipientLabel).not.toBe(orphanPeer);
    expect(orphanThread.body.conversa.display.recipientLabel).not.toBe(orphan.displayName);
    expect(providerCallCounts()).toEqual(providerCallsAfterLifecycle);

    const foreign = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP4 display foreign', `instagram-cp4-display-foreign-${randomUUID()}@example.test`);
    const foreignCookie = await login(app, foreign.owner.email);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${sharedA.conversationId}/messages`).set('cookie', foreignCookie).expect(404);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations`).set('cookie', foreignCookie).expect(404);
  });

  it('retains only proven account labels through disconnect, catalog reload and same-account reauthorization', async () => {
    const accountA = `synthetic-cp4-display-disconnect-a-${randomUUID()}`;
    const accountB = `synthetic-cp4-display-disconnect-b-${randomUUID()}`;
    const peerA = `opaque-cp4-display-disconnect-peer-a-${randomUUID()}`;
    const peerB = `opaque-cp4-display-disconnect-peer-b-${randomUUID()}`;
    await authorize(accountA, 'disconnect_account_a').then(({ response }) => expect(response.status).toBe(200));
    await authorize(accountB, 'disconnect_account_b').then(({ response }) => expect(response.status).toBe(200));
    await signedInstagramBatch(accountA, [{ peer: peerA, id: `mid-${randomUUID()}`, text: 'account A message' }]).expect(200);
    await signedInstagramBatch(accountB, [{ peer: peerB, id: `mid-${randomUUID()}`, text: 'account B message' }]).expect(200);
    const channelA = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'INSTAGRAM', businessIdentity: { externalResourceId: accountA } } });
    const channelB = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'INSTAGRAM', businessIdentity: { externalResourceId: accountB } } });
    const identityA = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channelA.businessIdentityId! } });
    const identityB = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channelB.businessIdentityId! } });
    const conversationA = await prisma.conversationRoute.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', businessIdentityId: identityA.id } });
    const conversationB = await prisma.conversationRoute.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', businessIdentityId: identityB.id } });
    const catalog = async () => {
      const start = queryEvents.length;
      const result = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channel-connections`).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
      assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(start));
      return result.body.connections as Array<{ channelId: string; generation: number; status: string; identityVerified: boolean; providerConnectionState: string; receiptVerified: boolean; display: Display }>;
    };
    const conversationList = async () => {
      const start = queryEvents.length;
      const result = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => list(1, 20).expect(200));
      assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(start));
      return result.body.conversations as ListedConversation[];
    };
    const thread = async (conversationId: string) => {
      const start = queryEvents.length;
      const result = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`).query({ marcarLida: 'false' }).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
      assertReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(start));
      return result.body.conversa.display as Display;
    };
    const providerCallsBeforeReads = providerCallCounts();
    const originalCatalog = await catalog();
    const originalList = await conversationList();
    const originalA = originalCatalog.find(({ channelId }) => channelId === channelA.id)!;
    const originalB = originalCatalog.find(({ channelId }) => channelId === channelB.id)!;
    expect(originalA.display.instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_a');
    expect(originalA.identityVerified).toBe(true);
    expect(originalB.display.instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_b');
    expect(originalList.find(({ id }) => id === conversationA.conversationId)?.display?.instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_a');
    expect(originalList.find(({ id }) => id === conversationB.conversationId)?.display?.instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_b');
    expect(providerCallCounts()).toEqual(providerCallsBeforeReads);

    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channel-connections/${channelA.id}/disconnect`)
      .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ expectedGeneration: channelA.generation }).expect(200);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channel-connections/${channelB.id}/disconnect`)
      .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ expectedGeneration: channelB.generation }).expect(200);
    const disconnectedA = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channelA.id }, include: { credential: true } });
    const disconnectedB = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channelB.id }, include: { credential: true } });
    expect(disconnectedA).toMatchObject({ status: 'DESCONECTADO', providerConnectionState: 'DISCONNECTED', receiptVerifiedAt: null, credential: null });
    expect(disconnectedB).toMatchObject({ status: 'DESCONECTADO', providerConnectionState: 'DISCONNECTED', receiptVerifiedAt: null, credential: null });
    expect(await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identityA.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', externalResourceId: accountA, scopeReference: identityA.scopeReference });
    expect(await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identityB.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', externalResourceId: accountB, scopeReference: identityB.scopeReference });
    const providerCallsAfterDisconnect = providerCallCounts();
    const disconnectedCatalog = await catalog();
    const disconnectedList = await conversationList();
    expect(disconnectedCatalog.find(({ channelId }) => channelId === channelA.id)?.display.instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_a');
    expect(disconnectedCatalog.find(({ channelId }) => channelId === channelB.id)?.display.instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_b');
    expect(disconnectedCatalog.find(({ channelId }) => channelId === channelA.id)).toMatchObject({
      status: 'DESCONECTADO', identityVerified: true, providerConnectionState: 'DISCONNECTED', receiptVerified: false,
    });
    expect(disconnectedList.find(({ id }) => id === conversationA.conversationId)?.display?.instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_a');
    expect(disconnectedList.find(({ id }) => id === conversationB.conversationId)?.display?.instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_b');
    expect((await thread(conversationA.conversationId)).instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_a');
    expect((await thread(conversationB.conversationId)).instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_b');
    expect(providerCallCounts()).toEqual(providerCallsAfterDisconnect);

    const ownerBeforeReauthorization = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channelA.id } });
    const cooldownUntil = (ownerBeforeReauthorization.instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt?.getTime() ?? 0) + 10_000;
    const cooldownWaitMs = Math.max(0, cooldownUntil - Date.now() + 50);
    if (cooldownWaitMs > 10_050) throw new Error('Expected the Instagram resource cooldown to be within its bounded natural wait');
    if (cooldownWaitMs > 0) await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, cooldownWaitMs));
    const reauthorization = await authorize(accountA, 'disc_a_reauth', 'REAUTHORIZE', channelA.id, disconnectedA.generation);
    expect(reauthorization.response.status).toBe(200);
    const providerCallsAfterReauthorization = providerCallCounts();
    const reauthorizedCatalog = await catalog();
    const reauthorizedList = await conversationList();
    expect(reauthorizedCatalog.find(({ channelId }) => channelId === channelA.id)?.display.instagramUsername).toBe('disc_a_reauth');
    expect(reauthorizedCatalog.find(({ channelId }) => channelId === channelB.id)?.display.instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_b');
    expect(reauthorizedList.find(({ id }) => id === conversationA.conversationId)?.display?.instagramUsername).toBe('disc_a_reauth');
    expect(reauthorizedList.find(({ id }) => id === conversationB.conversationId)?.display?.instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_b');
    expect((await thread(conversationA.conversationId)).instagramUsername).toBe('disc_a_reauth');
    expect((await thread(conversationB.conversationId)).instagramUsername).toBe('disconnect_account_b');
    expect(providerCallCounts()).toEqual(providerCallsAfterReauthorization);
  });

  it('uses the qualified WhatsApp business identity and recipient address, not contradictory BU or provider metadata', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const ownNumber = '5584999990000';
    const recipient = '5584991234567';
    const phoneNumberId = `synthetic-cp4-display-phone-${randomUUID()}`;

      contacts: [{ profile: { name: 'Qualified WA display contact' } }],
      messages: [{ id: `synthetic-cp4-display-wa-${randomUUID()}`, from: recipient, timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), type: 'text', text: { body: 'qualified WhatsApp display' } }],
    } }] }] });
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(event).digest('hex')}`).send(event).expect(200);
    const route = await prisma.conversationRoute.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, platform: 'WHATSAPP' }, include: { conversation: true } });
    const providerCallsBeforeRead = providerCallCounts();
    const start = queryEvents.length;
    const result = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => list(1, 50).expect(200));
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(start));
    const row = (result.body.conversations as ListedConversation[]).find(({ id }) => id === route.conversationId)!;
    expect(row.display).toBeDefined();
    expect(row.display!.platform).toBe('WHATSAPP');
    expect(row.display!.instagramUsername).toBeNull();
    expect(row.display!.recipientPhone?.replace(/\D/g, '')).toBe(recipient);
    expect(row.display!.accountLabel?.replace(/\D/g, '')).toBe(ownNumber);
    const safeJson = JSON.stringify(row.display);
    expect(safeJson).not.toContain(wabaId);
    expect(safeJson).not.toContain(phoneNumberId);
    expect(safeJson).not.toContain('5584000000001');
    expect(providerCallCounts()).toEqual(providerCallsBeforeRead);
    const threadStart = queryEvents.length;
    const thread = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${route.conversationId}/messages`).query({ marcarLida: 'false' }).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
    const waThreadDomainSqlCount = domainStatements(queryEvents.slice(threadStart)).length;
    expect(waThreadDomainSqlCount).toEqual(expect.any(Number));
    assertReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(threadStart));
    expect(thread.body.conversa.display).toMatchObject({ platform: 'WHATSAPP', recipientPhone: row.display!.recipientPhone, accountLabel: row.display!.accountLabel });
    const waReplyStart = queryEvents.length;
    const waReplyOptions = await observeMetadataOnly(app, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${route.conversationId}/reply-options`).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200));
    const waReplyOptionsDomainSqlCount = domainStatements(queryEvents.slice(waReplyStart)).length;
    expect(waReplyOptionsDomainSqlCount).toEqual(expect.any(Number));
    assertReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(waReplyStart));
    expect(waReplyOptions.body.schemaVersion).toBe(2);
    const waReplyOption = (waReplyOptions.body.options as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).find((option) => option.channelId === wa.channel.id)!;
    expect(waReplyOption).toMatchObject({ schemaVersion: 2, channelId: wa.channel.id, routeId: route.id });
    expect(String(waReplyOption.accountLabel).replace(/\D/g, '')).toBe(ownNumber);
    expect(waReplyOption.recipientLabel).toBe('Qualified WA display contact');
    expect(waReplyOption.contextFingerprint).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: wa.identity.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: wa.channel.id, externalResourceId: phoneNumberId });
    expect(providerCallCounts()).toEqual(providerCallsBeforeRead);
  });
});
```

```text
import request from 'supertest';

import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';

import { criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, limparBanco, login, metaFake, subirApp } from './helpers';

describe('Instagram CP4 initial recipient and pending WhatsApp projections', () => {

    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
    await limparBanco(prisma);
  });

    if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  });

  it('qualifies only one canonical BU-local WhatsApp person without a route or provider activity', async () => {

    const cookie = await login(app, 'instagram-cp4-recipient@example.test');
    const wa = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', 'synthetic-cp4-wa-resource');
    const createConversation = async (contact: string, contactType: 'PESSOA' | 'GRUPO' = 'PESSOA') => {
      const contactRow = await prisma.contact.create({ data: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, displayName: 'Fixture peer' } });
      const conversation = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
        buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: contactRow.id, contact, contactType,
      } });
      return { contactRow, conversation };
    };

    await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: canonical.contactRow.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP',
      namespace: 'PERSON', scopeReference: 'BU', externalPeerId: canonical.conversation.contact,
    } });
    const instagram = await createConversation('5584991234568');
    await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: instagram.contactRow.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM',
      namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', scopeReference: 'synthetic-ig-account', externalPeerId: 'synthetic-ig-peer',
    } });
    const mixed = await createConversation('5584991234574');
    await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: mixed.contactRow.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP',
      namespace: 'PERSON', scopeReference: 'BU', externalPeerId: mixed.conversation.contact,
    } });
    await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: mixed.contactRow.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM',
      namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', scopeReference: 'synthetic-mixed-ig-account', externalPeerId: 'synthetic-mixed-ig-peer',
    } });
    const malformed = await createConversation('5584991234569');
    await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: malformed.contactRow.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP',
      namespace: 'PERSON', scopeReference: 'BU', externalPeerId: 'not-a-canonical-peer',
    } });
    const ambiguous = await createConversation('5584991234570');
    await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: ambiguous.contactRow.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP',
      namespace: 'PERSON', scopeReference: 'BU', externalPeerId: ambiguous.conversation.contact,
    } });
    await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: ambiguous.contactRow.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP',
      namespace: 'PERSON', scopeReference: 'BU', externalPeerId: '5584991234571',
    } });
    const group = await createConversation('5584991234572', 'GRUPO');
    await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: group.contactRow.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP',
      namespace: 'GROUP', scopeReference: 'BU', externalPeerId: 'synthetic-group-peer',
    } });
    const otherTenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP4 other BU', 'instagram-cp4-other-bu@example.test');
    const foreign = await prisma.contact.create({ data: { buId: otherTenant.businessUnit.id } });
    await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {

      namespace: 'PERSON', scopeReference: 'BU', externalPeerId: '5584991234573',
    } });
    const crossBu = await createConversation('5584991234573');
    const beforeRoutes = await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });
    const beforeAttempts = await prisma.initialMessageIntent.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });
    const beforeProviderCalls = metaFake.providerCalls;
    const beforeSends = metaFake.enviados.length;
    const responseFor = async (id: string) => request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${id}/start-options`).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    expect((await responseFor(canonical.conversation.id)).body.initialRecipientPlatform).toBe('WHATSAPP');
    for (const candidate of [instagram, mixed, malformed, ambiguous, group, crossBu]) {
      expect((await responseFor(candidate.conversation.id)).body.initialRecipientPlatform).toBeNull();
    }
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(beforeRoutes);
    expect(await prisma.initialMessageIntent.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(beforeAttempts);
    expect(metaFake.providerCalls).toBe(beforeProviderCalls);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(beforeSends);
    expect(wa.channel.id).toBeTruthy();

  it('projects pending official and unofficial WhatsApp channels with current-attempt metadata while excluding Instagram', async () => {
    const ownedTenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP4 pending unofficial', 'instagram-cp4-pending-unofficial@example.test');
    const ownedCookie = await login(app, 'instagram-cp4-pending-unofficial@example.test');
    const owned = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, ownedTenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', 'synthetic-cp4-owned-wa');
    const pendingUnofficial = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: {
      buId: ownedTenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTANDO',
      providerConnectionState: 'PENDING', ownNumberVerified: false,
    } });
    const ownedAttempt = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.create({ data: {
      mantenedoraId: ownedTenant.mantenedora.id, buId: ownedTenant.businessUnit.id, channelId: pendingUnofficial.id,
      type: 'NAO_OFICIAL', userId: ownedTenant.owner.id, sessionHash: 'synthetic-session-hash',
      idempotencyKey: randomUUID(), requestHash: 'a'.repeat(64), expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000),
    } });
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: pendingUnofficial.id }, data: { currentAttemptId: ownedAttempt.id } });

    const pendingTenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP4 pending official', 'instagram-cp4-pending-official@example.test');
    const pendingCookie = await login(app, 'instagram-cp4-pending-official@example.test');
    const pendingOfficial = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: {
      buId: pendingTenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTANDO',
      providerConnectionState: 'PENDING', ownNumberVerified: false,
    } });
    const officialAttempt = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.create({ data: {
      mantenedoraId: pendingTenant.mantenedora.id, buId: pendingTenant.businessUnit.id, channelId: pendingOfficial.id,
      type: 'OFICIAL', userId: pendingTenant.owner.id, sessionHash: 'synthetic-session-hash',
      idempotencyKey: randomUUID(), requestHash: 'b'.repeat(64), expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000),
    } });
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: pendingOfficial.id }, data: { currentAttemptId: officialAttempt.id } });

    await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: {
      buId: ownedTenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'INSTAGRAM', status: 'CONECTANDO',
      providerConnectionState: 'PENDING',
    } });

    const ownedDetail = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${ownedTenant.businessUnit.id}`)
      .set('cookie', ownedCookie).expect(200);
    expect(ownedDetail.body.papel).toBe('OWNER');
    expect(ownedDetail.body.canais).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      expect.objectContaining({ id: owned.channel.id, type: 'OFICIAL' }),
      expect.objectContaining({ id: pendingUnofficial.id, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL', currentAttemptId: ownedAttempt.id }),
    ]));
    expect(JSON.stringify(ownedDetail.body)).not.toContain('INSTAGRAM');
    expect(JSON.stringify(ownedDetail.body)).not.toContain('credential');
    const ownedList = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/business-units').set('cookie', ownedCookie).expect(200);
    const projectedOwned = ownedList.body.businessUnits.find((row: { id: string }) => row.id === ownedTenant.businessUnit.id);
    expect(projectedOwned.channels).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      expect.objectContaining({ id: owned.channel.id, type: 'OFICIAL' }),
      expect.objectContaining({ id: pendingUnofficial.id, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL', currentAttemptId: ownedAttempt.id }),
    ]));
    expect(ownedList.body.businessUnits.some((row: { id: string }) => row.id === pendingTenant.businessUnit.id)).toBe(false);

    const conflictingOfficial = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: {
      buId: ownedTenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTANDO',
      providerConnectionState: 'PENDING', ownNumberVerified: false,
    } });
    const conflictingAttempt = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.create({ data: {
      mantenedoraId: ownedTenant.mantenedora.id, buId: ownedTenant.businessUnit.id,
      channelId: conflictingOfficial.id, type: 'OFICIAL', userId: ownedTenant.owner.id,
      sessionHash: 'synthetic-conflict-session', idempotencyKey: randomUUID(),
      requestHash: 'c'.repeat(64), expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000),
    } });
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: conflictingOfficial.id }, data: { currentAttemptId: conflictingAttempt.id } });
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${ownedTenant.businessUnit.id}`)
      .set('cookie', ownedCookie).expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('CHANNEL_GRAPH_AMBIGUOUS'));
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/business-units')
      .set('cookie', ownedCookie).expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('CHANNEL_GRAPH_AMBIGUOUS'));

    const pendingDetail = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${pendingTenant.businessUnit.id}`)
      .set('cookie', pendingCookie).expect(200);
    expect(pendingDetail.body.canais).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({
      id: pendingOfficial.id, type: 'OFICIAL', currentAttemptId: officialAttempt.id,
    })]);
    const pendingList = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/business-units').set('cookie', pendingCookie).expect(200);
    const projectedPending = pendingList.body.businessUnits.find((row: { id: string }) => row.id === pendingTenant.businessUnit.id);
    expect(projectedPending.channels).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({
      id: pendingOfficial.id, type: 'OFICIAL', currentAttemptId: officialAttempt.id,
    })]);
  });
});
```

### api-app/test/instagram-cp4-realtime.e2e-spec.ts — 19222 bytes SHA256 e77142d62cd6ac7e3726353653aed7a39e96afb82f589149113349a5befb3b1c

```text

import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createHmac, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { WebSocket } from 'ws';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { RealtimeService } from '../src/realtime/realtime.service';
import {
  criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET, limparBanco, login,
  META_APP_SECRET, instagramFake, subirApp, typebotFake,
} from './helpers';

type SocketEvent = Record<string, unknown>;
type Probe = { socket: WebSocket; events: SocketEvent[]; connected: SocketEvent; waitFor: (predicate: (event: SocketEvent) => boolean, label: string) => Promise<SocketEvent> };

const bounded = async <T>(promise: Promise<T>, label: string, timeoutMs = 5_000): Promise<T> => {
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  try {
    return await Promise.race([promise, new Promise<T>((_, reject) => {
      timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`Timed out waiting for ${label}`)), timeoutMs);
    })]);
  } finally { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); }
};

const connect = async (url: string, cookie: string): Promise<Probe> => {
  const socket = new WebSocket(url, { headers: { cookie } });
  const events: SocketEvent[] = [];
  const waiters: Array<{ predicate: (event: SocketEvent) => boolean; resolve: (event: SocketEvent) => void }> = [];
  socket.on('message', (raw) => {
    const event = JSON.parse(raw.toString()) as SocketEvent;
    events.push(event);
    const index = waiters.findIndex(({ predicate }) => predicate(event));
    if (index >= 0) waiters.splice(index, 1)[0]!.resolve(event);
  });
  await bounded(new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    socket.once('open', resolve);
    socket.once('error', reject);
  }), 'native WebSocket open');
  const waitFor = (predicate: (event: SocketEvent) => boolean, label: string) => {
    const existing = events.find(predicate);
    if (existing) return Promise.resolve(existing);
    return bounded(new Promise<SocketEvent>((resolve) => waiters.push({ predicate, resolve })), label);
  };
  const connected = await waitFor((event) => event.tipo === 'conectado', 'native WebSocket connected scope');
  return { socket, events, connected, waitFor };
};

const settleByPingPong = async (probe: Probe) => bounded(new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
  const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Native WebSocket ping did not receive pong')), 3_000);
  probe.socket.once('pong', () => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve(); });
  probe.socket.ping();
}), 'native WebSocket pong');

describe('Instagram CP4 realtime ingress (real Nest, PostgreSQL, HTTP and WebSocket)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let tenant: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let cookie = '';
  const queryEvents: Array<{ query: string; params: string }> = [];
  const probes: Probe[] = [];

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp(undefined, true));
    prisma.$on('query', (event) => queryEvents.push(event));
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
    app.get(RealtimeService).ligar(app.getHttpServer());
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    for (const probe of probes.splice(0)) probe.socket.close();
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    queryEvents.length = 0;
    tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP4 realtime', `instagram-cp4-realtime-${randomUUID()}@example.test`);
    cookie = await login(app, tenant.owner.email);
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    for (const probe of probes) probe.socket.close();
    if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  });

  const authorize = async (session: string, buId: string, accountId: string, username: string) => {
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: accountId, username };
    const reservation = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/instagram-authorizations`)
      .set('cookie', session).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', randomUUID())
      .send({ operation: 'CONNECT' }).expect(201);
    const state = new URL(reservation.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state');
    expect(state).toBeTruthy();
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback').set('cookie', session)
      .query({ state, code: `synthetic-cp4-realtime-${randomUUID()}` }).expect(303);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${reservation.body.id}/confirm`)
      .set('cookie', session).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({}).expect(200);
  };

  const signedInstagram = (accountId: string, messages: Array<{ peer: string; id: string; text: string; timestamp?: number }>) => {
    const body = JSON.stringify({ object: 'instagram', entry: [{ id: accountId, messaging: messages.map(({ peer, id, text, timestamp }) => ({
      sender: { id: peer }, recipient: { id: accountId }, timestamp: timestamp ?? Date.now(), message: { mid: id, text },
    })) }] });
    return { body, signature: `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET).update(body).digest('hex')}` };
  };

  it('publishes distinct account-scoped accepted messages once, scopes sockets by BU, and keeps Typebot WhatsApp-only', async () => {
    const accountA = `synthetic-cp4-realtime-a-${randomUUID()}`;
    const accountB = `synthetic-cp4-realtime-b-${randomUUID()}`;
    const sharedPeer = `opaque-cp4-peer-${randomUUID()}`;
    await authorize(cookie, tenant.businessUnit.id, accountA, 'realtime_account_a');
    await authorize(cookie, tenant.businessUnit.id, accountB, 'realtime_account_b');

    const foreign = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP4 realtime foreign', `instagram-cp4-realtime-foreign-${randomUUID()}@example.test`);
    const foreignCookie = await login(app, foreign.owner.email);
    const address = app.getHttpServer().address();
    if (!address || typeof address === 'string') throw new Error('Expected a listening native HTTP server address.');
    const wsUrl = `ws://127.0.0.1:${address.port}/ws`;
    const authorized = await connect(wsUrl, cookie);
    const foreignSocket = await connect(wsUrl, foreignCookie);
    probes.push(authorized, foreignSocket);
    expect(authorized.connected.escopo).toContain(tenant.businessUnit.id);
    expect(foreignSocket.connected.escopo).not.toContain(tenant.businessUnit.id);
    const foreignAccount = `synthetic-cp4-realtime-foreign-${randomUUID()}`;
    await authorize(foreignCookie, foreign.businessUnit.id, foreignAccount, 'realtime_foreign_account');

    const timestampA = Date.now() - 1_000;
    const timestampB = Date.now() - 900;
    const messageIdA = `synthetic-cp4-realtime-a-${randomUUID()}`;
    const messageIdB = `synthetic-cp4-realtime-b-${randomUUID()}`;
    const igA = signedInstagram(accountA, [{ peer: sharedPeer, id: messageIdA, text: 'account A message', timestamp: timestampA }]);
    const igB = signedInstagram(accountB, [{ peer: sharedPeer, id: messageIdB, text: 'account B message', timestamp: timestampB }]);
    const foreignIg = signedInstagram(foreignAccount, [{ peer: sharedPeer, id: `synthetic-cp4-realtime-foreign-${randomUUID()}`, text: 'foreign BU message' }]);

    const eventB = await authorized.waitFor((event) => event.tipo === 'mensagem' && event.buId === tenant.businessUnit.id

    'Instagram account B qualified realtime route event');
    expect(eventB.direcao).toBe('IN');
    expect(eventA.conversaId).not.toBe(eventB.conversaId);
    await settleByPingPong(foreignSocket);
    expect(foreignSocket.events.filter((event) => event.tipo === 'mensagem')).toHaveLength(0);

    });
    const foreignRoute = await prisma.conversationRoute.findFirstOrThrow({

    const acceptedTimes = await prisma.message.findMany({ where: { externalId: { in: [messageIdA, messageIdB] } }, select: { externalId: true, officialInboundAt: true } });
    expect(new Map(acceptedTimes.map((message) => [message.externalId, message.officialInboundAt?.getTime()]))).toEqual(new Map([
      [messageIdA, timestampA], [messageIdB, timestampB],

    const queryAfterFirst = queryEvents.length;

    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: accountAResource.currentChannelId! }, select: { receiptVerifiedAt: true } }))
      .toEqual(receiptBeforeReplay);
    const routesAfterReplay = await prisma.conversationRoute.findMany({

    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { buId: foreign.businessUnit.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(1);
    expect(queryEvents.slice(queryAfterFirst).some(({ query }) => /typebot_(triggers|sessions)/i.test(query))).toBe(false);

    const listedAfterReplay = await request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations`).set('cookie', cookie)
      .query({ pagina: 1, limite: 50 }).expect(200);
    const visibleConversationIds = (listedAfterReplay.body.conversations as Array<{ id: string }>).map(({ id }) => id);
    expect(visibleConversationIds).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([routeA.conversationId, routeB.conversationId]));
    const threadAfterReplay = await request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${routeA.conversationId}/messages`).set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    const retainedMessageA = messagesBeforeReplay.find((message) => message.externalId === messageIdA);
    if (!retainedMessageA) throw new Error('Expected the original accepted Instagram message after replay.');
    expect(threadAfterReplay.body.messages.map((message: { id: string }) => message.id)).toContain(retainedMessageA.id);
    expect(threadAfterReplay.body.messages.find((message: { id: string }) => message.id === retainedMessageA.id).content)
      .toBe('account A message');

    const phone = '5584991234567';
    const ownPhone = '5584999990000';
    const phoneNumberId = `synthetic-cp4-realtime-wa-${randomUUID()}`;
    const wabaId = `synthetic-cp4-realtime-waba-${randomUUID()}`;
    const wa = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', phoneNumberId, {
      provedBusinessPhone: ownPhone, provedWabaId: wabaId,
    });
    const waBody = { entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: {
      metadata: { phone_number_id: phoneNumberId }, contacts: [{ profile: { name: 'Typebot WA control' } }],
      messages: [{ id: `synthetic-cp4-typebot-wa-${randomUUID()}`, from: phone, timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), type: 'text', text: { body: 'start control' } }],
    } }] }] };
    const serializedWa = JSON.stringify(waBody);
    const typebotQueriesStart = queryEvents.length;
    const startsBeforeWa = typebotFake.starts;
    const fakeCallsBeforeWa = typebotFake.startedBots.length;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(serializedWa).digest('hex')}`)
      .send(serializedWa).expect(200);
    expect(queryEvents.slice(typebotQueriesStart).some(({ query }) => /typebot_sessions/i.test(query))).toBe(true);
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(startsBeforeWa + 1);
    expect(typebotFake.startedBots).toHaveLength(fakeCallsBeforeWa + 1);
    expect(typebotFake.startedBots.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ botId: bot.publishedTypebotId, contact: phone });
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: wa.identity.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: wa.channel.id, externalResourceId: phoneNumberId });
  });
});
```

### api-app/test/instagram-cp4-window-config.e2e-spec.ts — 12011 bytes SHA256 ce67fe5404d062b986df8756ae56208d93d808e0da7a4d94fd07c55f3921518d

```text

import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, afterEach, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createHmac, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { OutboundService } from '../src/domain/inbox/outbound.service';
import { criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, limparBanco, login, META_APP_SECRET, metaFake, subirApp } from './helpers';

const PHONE_NUMBER_ID = '987654321';
const WABA_ID = '123456789';
const BUSINESS_PHONE = '5584999990000';
const RECIPIENT = '5511987654321';
const INITIAL_MESSAGE_URL = `https://graph.facebook.com/v26.0/${PHONE_NUMBER_ID}/messages`;

type CapturedMetaPost = { url: string; method: string; authorization: string | null; contentType: string | null; body: Record<string, unknown> };

describe('CP4 configured WhatsApp text-window policy on native BFF and PostgreSQL', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let originalFetch: typeof globalThis.fetch | undefined;
  let wirePosts: CapturedMetaPost[] = [];

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp(undefined, true));
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  });
  beforeEach(async () => { await limparBanco(prisma); wirePosts = []; });
  afterEach(() => { restoreFetch(); });
  afterAll(async () => { restoreFetch(); if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma); await app?.close(); });

  function restoreFetch() {
    if (originalFetch) globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;
    originalFetch = undefined;
  }

  function installStrictMetaPostFake() {
    restoreFetch();
    originalFetch = globalThis.fetch;
    globalThis.fetch = (async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
      const url = input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input);
      const method = (init?.method ?? (input instanceof Request ? input.method : 'GET')).toUpperCase();
      const headers = new Headers(input instanceof Request ? input.headers : undefined);
      new Headers(init?.headers).forEach((value, key) => headers.set(key, value));
      if (url !== INITIAL_MESSAGE_URL || method !== 'POST') {
        throw new Error(`Unexpected external request blocked by CP4 fixture: ${method} ${url}`);
      }
      let body: Record<string, unknown>;
      try { body = JSON.parse(String(init?.body ?? '')) as Record<string, unknown>; }
      catch { throw new Error('Meta fixture blocked a non-JSON POST body.'); }
      expect(headers.get('authorization')).toBe('Bearer fixture-scoped-token');
      expect(headers.get('content-type')).toBe('application/json');
      expect(body).toMatchObject({ messaging_product: 'whatsapp', messaging_account_id: WABA_ID,
        to: `+${RECIPIENT}`, type: 'text', text: { body: expect.any(String) } });
      wirePosts.push({ url, method, authorization: headers.get('authorization'), contentType: headers.get('content-type'), body });
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ messaging_product: 'whatsapp', contacts: [{ input: RECIPIENT, wa_id: RECIPIENT }],
        messages: [{ id: `wamid-cp4-window-${wirePosts.length}` }] }), { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
    }) as typeof globalThis.fetch;
  }

  function expectWirePost(text: string) {
    expect(wirePosts).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(wirePosts[0]).toMatchObject({ url: INITIAL_MESSAGE_URL, method: 'POST',
      authorization: 'Bearer fixture-scoped-token', contentType: 'application/json',
      body: { messaging_product: 'whatsapp', messaging_account_id: WABA_ID, to: `+${RECIPIENT}`, type: 'text', text: { body: text } } });
  }

  async function setup(email: string, requiresWindow: boolean) {
    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'CP4 configured window', email);
    const cookie = await login(app, email);
    const binding = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', PHONE_NUMBER_ID,
      { requiresWindow, provedWabaId: WABA_ID, provedBusinessPhone: BUSINESS_PHONE });
    const opened = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/conversations/open`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ telefone: `+${RECIPIENT}` }).expect(201);
    return { tenant, cookie, binding, conversationId: opened.body.conversaId as string };
  }

  it('previews and sends the first official text without prior IN when persisted requiresWindow is false', async () => {
    const { tenant, cookie, binding, conversationId } = await setup('cp4-window-false@example.test', false);
    const base = `/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}`;
    const options = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`${base}/start-options`).set('Cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    expect(options.body.channels).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({ id: binding.channel.id,
      availability: expect.objectContaining({ text: null }) }));
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'IN' } })).toBe(0);
    const message = { tipo: 'TEXTO', texto: 'first configured official text' };
    installStrictMetaPostFake();
    const preview = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`${base}/initial-message-preview`).set('Cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ channelId: binding.channel.id,
        expectedGeneration: binding.channel.generation, mensagem: message }).expect(200);
    expect(preview.body.channel.requiresWindow).toBe(false);
    const sent = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`${base}/initial-messages`).set('Cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('Idempotency-Key', randomUUID())
      .send({ channelId: binding.channel.id, expectedGeneration: binding.channel.generation,
        mensagem: message, previewFingerprint: preview.body.previewFingerprint }).expect(201);
    expect(sent.body.attempt.state).toBe('SUCCEEDED');
    expectWirePost(message.texto);
    expect(await prisma.message.findMany({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT' } })).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      expect.objectContaining({ content: message.texto, externalId: 'wamid-cp4-window-1', status: 'ENVIADA' }),
    ]));
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
    const replyOptions = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`${base}/reply-options`).set('Cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    expect(replyOptions.body.options).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({ requiresWindow: false, available: true, windowOpen: true }));
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: binding.channel.id } })).toMatchObject({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, requiresWindow: false });
  });

  it('keeps true official bindings closed without qualified IN and opens them after current-resource IN', async () => {
    const { cookie, binding, conversationId } = await setup('cp4-window-true@example.test', true);
    const base = `/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}`;
    const before = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`${base}/start-options`).set('Cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    expect(before.body.channels).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({ id: binding.channel.id,
      availability: expect.objectContaining({ text: 'WINDOW_UNQUALIFIED' }) }));
    const message = { tipo: 'TEXTO', texto: 'true window official text' };
    const providerCallsBefore = metaFake.providerCalls;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`${base}/initial-message-preview`).set('Cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ channelId: binding.channel.id,
        expectedGeneration: binding.channel.generation, mensagem: message }).expect(409);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(metaFake.providerCalls).toBe(providerCallsBefore);
    expect(wirePosts).toHaveLength(0);
    const inbound = { entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: {
      metadata: { phone_number_id: PHONE_NUMBER_ID }, contacts: [{ profile: { name: 'CP4 synthetic sender' } }],
      messages: [{ id: `wamid-cp4-current-in-${conversationId}`, from: RECIPIENT, type: 'text',
        timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), text: { body: 'qualified current official inbound' } }],
    } }] }] };
    const signature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(JSON.stringify(inbound)).digest('hex')}`;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', signature).send(inbound).expect(200);
    const acceptedInbound = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'IN', externalId: `wamid-cp4-current-in-${conversationId}` } });
    expect(acceptedInbound).toMatchObject({ buId: binding.channel.buId, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA',
      providerResourceIdentityId: binding.identity.id, content: 'qualified current official inbound' });
    expect(acceptedInbound.officialInboundAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    installStrictMetaPostFake();
    const preview = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`${base}/initial-message-preview`).set('Cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ channelId: binding.channel.id,
        expectedGeneration: binding.channel.generation, mensagem: message }).expect(200);
    expect(preview.body.channel.requiresWindow).toBe(true);
    const sent = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`${base}/initial-messages`).set('Cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('Idempotency-Key', randomUUID())
      .send({ channelId: binding.channel.id, expectedGeneration: binding.channel.generation,
        mensagem: message, previewFingerprint: preview.body.previewFingerprint }).expect(201);
    expect(sent.body.attempt.state).toBe('SUCCEEDED');
    expectWirePost(message.texto);
    expect(await prisma.message.findMany({ where: { conversationId, direction: 'OUT' } })).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      expect.objectContaining({ content: message.texto, externalId: 'wamid-cp4-window-1', status: 'ENVIADA' }),
    ]));
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('rejects a requiresWindow flip after resolver capture inside the real dispatch transaction before provider I/O', async () => {
    const { cookie, binding, conversationId } = await setup('cp4-window-race@example.test', false);
    const base = `/api/bff/conversations/${conversationId}`;
    const message = { tipo: 'TEXTO', texto: 'must not dispatch after binding flip' };
    const preview = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`${base}/initial-message-preview`).set('Cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ channelId: binding.channel.id,
        expectedGeneration: binding.channel.generation, mensagem: message }).expect(200);
    const outbound = app.get(OutboundService);
    const original = outbound.prepareInitial.bind(outbound);
    const captureThenFlip = vi.spyOn(outbound, 'prepareInitial').mockImplementation(async (reference) => {
      const operation = await original(reference);
      await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: binding.channel.id }, data: { requiresWindow: true } });
      return operation;
    });
    installStrictMetaPostFake();
    try {
      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`${base}/initial-messages`).set('Cookie', cookie)
        .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('Idempotency-Key', randomUUID())
        .send({ channelId: binding.channel.id, expectedGeneration: binding.channel.generation,
          mensagem: message, previewFingerprint: preview.body.previewFingerprint });
      expect(response.status).toBe(409);
    } finally { captureThenFlip.mockRestore(); }
    expect(wirePosts).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: binding.channel.id } })).toMatchObject({ requiresWindow: true,
      wabaId: WABA_ID, phoneNumberId: PHONE_NUMBER_ID, ownNumberVerified: true });
  });
});
```
