```text
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { limparBanco, subirApp } from './helpers';

const baselineMigrations = [
  '20260818125556_nucleo_v1_tenancy_identidade_atendimento',
  '20260821175101_modulos_e_marca_por_mantenedora',
  '20260821180000_tres_papeis_owner_admin_atendente',
  '20260821190000_setor_e_proveniencia_do_grant',
  '20260821190500_concessao_de_setor_e_fato_proprio',
  '20260821200000_historico_de_atividades',
  '20260821210000_wizard_de_onboarding',
  '20260821220000_preserva_modulo_ja_em_uso',
  '20260824122559_perfil_do_proprio_usuario',
  '20260824125224_arquivos_logo_e_avatar',
  '20260824125227_arquivos_dono',
  '20260824125334_avatar_e_do_usuario_nao_da_bu',
  '20260824161138_convite_e_recuperacao_de_senha',
  '20260827170000_cor_secundaria',
  '20260831130000_cores_primarias_por_tema',
  '20260901120000_typebot_bots_and_triggers',
  '20260901130000_typebot_sessions',
  '20261015120000_number_onboarding_foundation',
  '20261015130000_number_onboarding_provider_resource_ownership_immutable',
  '20261015140000_number_onboarding_channel_operation_fence',
  '20261015150000_number_onboarding_managed_cleanup',
  '20261016160000_number_onboarding_current_channel_identity_index',
  '20261020170000_message_inbound_window_index',
  '20261021130000_message_template_drafts',
  '20261021150000_message_template_operations',
  '20261022140000_initial_message_orchestration',
  '20261022150000_initial_message_admissions',
  '20261022160000_initial_message_inbox_order',
] as const;

const legacyConversationId = 'cp1-legacy-conversation';
const legacyMessageId = 'cp1-legacy-message';
const legacyProviderIdentityId = 'cp1-legacy-provider-identity';
const legacyAdmissionId = 'cp1-legacy-admission';
const legacyIntentId = 'cp1-legacy-intent';
const createdAt = '2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z';
const deadline = '2026-01-02T04:05:06.000Z';

describe('Instagram cutover preserves seeded legacy records (isolated PostgreSQL schema)', () => {
  let app: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof subirApp>>['app'];
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  const schema = `cp1_cutover_${randomUUID().replaceAll('-', '')}`;
  const quoteSchema = `"${schema}"`;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    if (prisma) {
      await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS ${quoteSchema} CASCADE`);
      await limparBanco(prisma);
    }
    await app?.close();
  });

  it('applies the real 28-migration legacy schema and two CP1 migrations without changing persisted identity or receipt data', async () => {
    await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`CREATE SCHEMA ${quoteSchema}`);
    const runSql = (sql: string) => execFileSync(
      process.execPath,
      ['node_modules/prisma/build/index.js', 'db', 'execute', '--stdin', '--schema', 'prisma/schema.prisma'],
      { cwd: process.cwd(), input: `SET search_path TO ${quoteSchema};\n${sql}`, encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 120_000 },
    );
    const migrationSql = (name: string) => readFileSync(`prisma/migrations/${name}/migration.sql`, 'utf8');
    runSql(baselineMigrations.map(migrationSql).join('\n'));

    runSql(`
        INSERT INTO mantenedoras (id, name, updated_at) VALUES ('cp1-legacy-owner', 'Synthetic legacy owner', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO setores (id, mantenedora_id, name, created_at) VALUES ('cp1-legacy-sector', 'cp1-legacy-owner', 'Synthetic sector', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO business_units (id, mantenedora_id, setor_id, name, phone, updated_at) VALUES ('cp1-legacy-bu', 'cp1-legacy-owner', 'cp1-legacy-sector', 'Synthetic legacy BU', '+5511999990000', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO bu_channels (id, bu_id, type, status, phone_number_id, updated_at) VALUES ('cp1-legacy-channel', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'OFICIAL', 'CONECTADO', 'cp1-wa-resource', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO bu_channels (id, bu_id, type, status, evolution_instance_name, updated_at) VALUES ('cp1-evolution-channel', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'NAO_OFICIAL', 'CONECTADO', 'cp1-evolution-resource', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO provider_resource_identities (id, provider, external_resource_id, bu_id, current_channel_id, created_at) VALUES
          ('${legacyProviderIdentityId}', 'OFICIAL', 'cp1-wa-resource', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'cp1-legacy-channel', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-evolution-provider-identity', 'NAO_OFICIAL', 'cp1-evolution-resource', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'cp1-evolution-channel', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO conversations (id, bu_id, contact, contact_type, display_name, unread_count, attendant_user_id, attendant_name, attendant_origin, created_at, updated_at) VALUES
          ('${legacyConversationId}', 'cp1-legacy-bu', '+55 (11) 99999-0000', 'PESSOA', 'Legacy peer', 7, 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'Synthetic actor', 'INTERNO', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-conv-prepared', 'cp1-legacy-bu', '5511999990001', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic prepared', 2, 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'Synthetic actor', 'INTERNO', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-conv-sending', 'cp1-legacy-bu', '5511999990002', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic sending', 3, 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'Synthetic actor', 'INTERNO', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-conv-accepted', 'cp1-legacy-bu', '5511999990003', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic accepted', 4, 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'Synthetic actor', 'INTERNO', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-conv-uncertain', 'cp1-legacy-bu', '5511999990004', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic uncertain', 5, 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'Synthetic actor', 'INTERNO', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-conv-group', 'cp1-legacy-bu', '120363000000000000@g.us', 'GRUPO', 'Synthetic group', 6, 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'Synthetic actor', 'INTERNO', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-conv-external-accepted', 'cp1-legacy-bu', '5511999990005', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic external accepted', 1, 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'Synthetic actor', 'INTERNO', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO messages (id, conversation_id, bu_id, direction, content, channel_origin, status, external_id, author_user_id, author_name, provider_resource_identity_id, created_at)
          VALUES ('${legacyMessageId}', '${legacyConversationId}', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'IN', 'synthetic receipt-bearing message', 'OFICIAL', 'RECEBIDA', 'cp1-provider-message-id', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'Synthetic actor', '${legacyProviderIdentityId}', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO messages (id, conversation_id, bu_id, direction, content, channel_origin, status, external_id, author_user_id, author_name, provider_resource_identity_id, created_at)
          VALUES ('cp1-incomplete-provenance-message', 'cp1-conv-group', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'IN', 'synthetic incomplete provenance', 'NAO_OFICIAL', 'RECEBIDA', NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO dispatch_admissions (id, conversation_id, bu_id, caller, actor_id, owner_nonce, selected_context, state, expires_at, dispatch_deadline_at, created_at, updated_at) VALUES
          ('${legacyAdmissionId}', '${legacyConversationId}', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'BFF', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'cp1-legacy-admission-nonce', '{"channelId":"cp1-legacy-channel","buId":"cp1-legacy-bu","channelOrigin":"OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"${legacyProviderIdentityId}","generation":1,"pairingEpoch":0,"activeCredentialVersion":null,"requiresWindow":false,"providerAccountReference":null}', 'SETTLED', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-admission-prepared', 'cp1-conv-prepared', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'BFF', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'cp1-admission-nonce-prepared', '{"channelId":"cp1-legacy-channel","buId":"cp1-legacy-bu","channelOrigin":"OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"${legacyProviderIdentityId}","generation":1,"pairingEpoch":0,"activeCredentialVersion":null,"requiresWindow":false,"providerAccountReference":null}', 'PREPARED', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-admission-sending', 'cp1-conv-sending', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'BFF', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'cp1-admission-nonce-sending', '{"channelId":"cp1-legacy-channel","buId":"cp1-legacy-bu","channelOrigin":"OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"${legacyProviderIdentityId}","generation":1,"pairingEpoch":0,"activeCredentialVersion":null,"requiresWindow":false,"providerAccountReference":null}', 'SENDING', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-admission-uncertain', 'cp1-conv-uncertain', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'BFF', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'cp1-admission-nonce-uncertain', '{"channelId":"cp1-evolution-channel","buId":"cp1-legacy-bu","channelOrigin":"NAO_OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"cp1-evolution-provider-identity","generation":1,"pairingEpoch":1,"activeCredentialVersion":null,"requiresWindow":false,"providerAccountReference":null}', 'UNCERTAIN', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO initial_message_intents (id, conversation_id, bu_id, actor_id, idempotency_key, owner_nonce, request_fingerprint, request, selected_context, state, lease_expires_at, dispatch_deadline_at, external_message_id, created_at, updated_at) VALUES
          ('${legacyIntentId}', '${legacyConversationId}', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'cp1-legacy-idempotency', 'cp1-legacy-intent-nonce', 'cp1-preserve-fingerprint', '{"canonicalRequest":{"channelId":"cp1-legacy-channel","expectedGeneration":1,"kind":"text","text":"Synthetic legacy message","previewFingerprint":"cp1-preview-fingerprint","recipient":"+55 (11) 99999-0000"},"originalConversation":{"updatedAt":"2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z","status":"QUEUED","attendantUserId":"cp1-legacy-actor","attendantName":"Synthetic actor","attendantOrigin":"INTERNO"},"renderedText":"Synthetic legacy message","originalActorName":"Synthetic actor"}', '{"channelId":"cp1-legacy-channel","buId":"cp1-legacy-bu","channelOrigin":"OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"${legacyProviderIdentityId}","generation":1,"pairingEpoch":0,"activeCredentialVersion":null,"requiresWindow":false,"providerAccountReference":null}', 'SUCCEEDED', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', NULL, '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-intent-prepared', 'cp1-conv-prepared', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'cp1-idempotency-prepared', 'cp1-intent-nonce-prepared', 'cp1-fingerprint-prepared', '{"canonicalRequest":{"channelId":"cp1-legacy-channel","expectedGeneration":1,"kind":"text","text":"Synthetic legacy message","previewFingerprint":"cp1-preview-fingerprint","recipient":"5511999990001"},"originalConversation":{"updatedAt":"2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z","status":"QUEUED","attendantUserId":"cp1-legacy-actor","attendantName":"Synthetic actor","attendantOrigin":"INTERNO"},"renderedText":"Synthetic legacy message","originalActorName":"Synthetic actor"}', '{"channelId":"cp1-legacy-channel","buId":"cp1-legacy-bu","channelOrigin":"OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"${legacyProviderIdentityId}","generation":1,"pairingEpoch":0,"activeCredentialVersion":null,"requiresWindow":false,"providerAccountReference":null}', 'PREPARED', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', NULL, '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-intent-sending', 'cp1-conv-sending', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'cp1-idempotency-sending', 'cp1-intent-nonce-sending', 'cp1-fingerprint-sending', '{"canonicalRequest":{"channelId":"cp1-legacy-channel","expectedGeneration":1,"kind":"text","text":"Synthetic legacy message","previewFingerprint":"cp1-preview-fingerprint","recipient":"5511999990002"},"originalConversation":{"updatedAt":"2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z","status":"QUEUED","attendantUserId":"cp1-legacy-actor","attendantName":"Synthetic actor","attendantOrigin":"INTERNO"},"renderedText":"Synthetic legacy message","originalActorName":"Synthetic actor"}', '{"channelId":"cp1-legacy-channel","buId":"cp1-legacy-bu","channelOrigin":"OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"${legacyProviderIdentityId}","generation":1,"pairingEpoch":0,"activeCredentialVersion":null,"requiresWindow":false,"providerAccountReference":null}', 'SENDING', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', NULL, '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-intent-accepted', 'cp1-conv-accepted', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'cp1-idempotency-accepted', 'cp1-intent-nonce-accepted', 'cp1-fingerprint-accepted', '{"canonicalRequest":{"channelId":"cp1-legacy-channel","expectedGeneration":1,"kind":"text","text":"Synthetic legacy message","previewFingerprint":"cp1-preview-fingerprint","recipient":"5511999990003"},"originalConversation":{"updatedAt":"2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z","status":"QUEUED","attendantUserId":"cp1-legacy-actor","attendantName":"Synthetic actor","attendantOrigin":"INTERNO"},"renderedText":"Synthetic legacy message","originalActorName":"Synthetic actor"}', '{"channelId":"cp1-legacy-channel","buId":"cp1-legacy-bu","channelOrigin":"OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"${legacyProviderIdentityId}","generation":1,"pairingEpoch":0,"activeCredentialVersion":null,"requiresWindow":false,"providerAccountReference":null}', 'EXTERNAL_ACCEPTED_LOCAL_BLOCKED', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', 'cp1-external-accepted-message', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-intent-uncertain', 'cp1-conv-uncertain', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'cp1-idempotency-uncertain', 'cp1-intent-nonce-uncertain', 'cp1-fingerprint-uncertain', '{"canonicalRequest":{"channelId":"cp1-evolution-channel","expectedGeneration":1,"kind":"text","text":"Synthetic legacy message","previewFingerprint":"cp1-preview-fingerprint","recipient":"5511999990004"},"originalConversation":{"updatedAt":"2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z","status":"QUEUED","attendantUserId":"cp1-legacy-actor","attendantName":"Synthetic actor","attendantOrigin":"INTERNO"},"renderedText":"Synthetic legacy message","originalActorName":"Synthetic actor"}', '{"channelId":"cp1-evolution-channel","buId":"cp1-legacy-bu","channelOrigin":"NAO_OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"cp1-evolution-provider-identity","generation":1,"pairingEpoch":1,"activeCredentialVersion":null,"requiresWindow":false,"providerAccountReference":null}', 'REJECTED', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', 'cp1-uncertain-external-id', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-intent-ambiguous', 'cp1-conv-group', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'cp1-idempotency-ambiguous', 'cp1-intent-nonce-ambiguous', 'cp1-fingerprint-ambiguous', '{"canonicalRequest":{"channelId":"cp1-evolution-channel","expectedGeneration":1,"kind":"text","text":"Synthetic legacy message","previewFingerprint":"cp1-preview-fingerprint","recipient":"120363000000000000@g.us"},"originalConversation":{"updatedAt":"2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z","status":"QUEUED","attendantUserId":"cp1-legacy-actor","attendantName":"Synthetic actor","attendantOrigin":"INTERNO"},"renderedText":"Synthetic legacy message","originalActorName":"Synthetic actor"}', '{"channelId":"cp1-evolution-channel","buId":"cp1-legacy-bu","channelOrigin":"NAO_OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"cp1-evolution-provider-identity","generation":1,"pairingEpoch":1,"activeCredentialVersion":null,"requiresWindow":false,"providerAccountReference":null}', 'AMBIGUOUS', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', NULL, '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-intent-external-accepted', 'cp1-conv-external-accepted', 'cp1-legacy-bu', 'cp1-legacy-actor', 'cp1-idempotency-external-accepted', 'cp1-intent-nonce-external-accepted', 'cp1-fingerprint-external-accepted', '{"canonicalRequest":{"channelId":"cp1-evolution-channel","expectedGeneration":1,"kind":"text","text":"Synthetic legacy message","previewFingerprint":"cp1-preview-fingerprint","recipient":"5511999990005"},"originalConversation":{"updatedAt":"2026-01-02T03:04:05.000Z","status":"QUEUED","attendantUserId":"cp1-legacy-actor","attendantName":"Synthetic actor","attendantOrigin":"INTERNO"},"renderedText":"Synthetic legacy message","originalActorName":"Synthetic actor"}', '{"channelId":"cp1-evolution-channel","buId":"cp1-legacy-bu","channelOrigin":"NAO_OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"cp1-evolution-provider-identity","generation":1,"pairingEpoch":1,"activeCredentialVersion":null,"requiresWindow":false,"providerAccountReference":null}', 'EXTERNAL_ACCEPTED', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', 'cp1-initial-external-accepted-id', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}');
      `);

    const oldSnapshot = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.$executeRawUnsafe(`SET LOCAL search_path TO ${quoteSchema}`);
      return tx.$queryRawUnsafe<Array<Record<string, unknown>>>(`
        SELECT (SELECT to_jsonb(c) - 'contact_id' FROM conversations c WHERE id = '${legacyConversationId}') AS conversation,
               (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(c) ORDER BY c.id) FROM (SELECT * FROM conversations ORDER BY id) c) AS all_conversations,
               (SELECT to_jsonb(m) FROM messages m WHERE id = '${legacyMessageId}') AS message,
               (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(m) ORDER BY m.id) FROM messages m WHERE id LIKE 'cp1-%') AS all_messages,
               (SELECT to_jsonb(p) FROM provider_resource_identities p WHERE id = '${legacyProviderIdentityId}') AS provider,
               (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(p) ORDER BY p.id) FROM provider_resource_identities p WHERE id LIKE 'cp1-%') AS all_providers,
               (SELECT to_jsonb(d) FROM dispatch_admissions d WHERE id = '${legacyAdmissionId}') AS admission,
               (SELECT to_jsonb(i) FROM initial_message_intents i WHERE id = '${legacyIntentId}') AS intent,
               (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(d) ORDER BY d.id) FROM dispatch_admissions d WHERE id LIKE 'cp1-%') AS all_admissions,
               (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(i) ORDER BY i.id) FROM initial_message_intents i WHERE id LIKE 'cp1-%') AS all_intents
      `);
    });

    runSql([
      migrationSql('20261023180000_instagram_adapter_type'),
      migrationSql('20261023190000_instagram_identity_routes'),
    ].join('\n'));

    const newSnapshot = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.$executeRawUnsafe(`SET LOCAL search_path TO ${quoteSchema}`);
      return tx.$queryRawUnsafe<Array<Record<string, unknown>>>(`
        SELECT (SELECT to_jsonb(c) - 'contact_id' FROM conversations c WHERE id = '${legacyConversationId}') AS conversation,
               (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(c) - 'contact_id' ORDER BY c.id) FROM (SELECT * FROM conversations ORDER BY id) c) AS all_conversations,
               (SELECT contact_id FROM conversations WHERE id = '${legacyConversationId}') AS contact_id,
               (SELECT to_jsonb(m) FROM messages m WHERE id = '${legacyMessageId}') AS message,
               (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(m) ORDER BY m.id) FROM messages m WHERE id LIKE 'cp1-%') AS all_messages,
               (SELECT to_jsonb(p) - 'business_identity_id' - 'unverified_staging' FROM provider_resource_identities p WHERE id = '${legacyProviderIdentityId}') AS provider,
               (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(p) - 'business_identity_id' - 'unverified_staging' ORDER BY p.id) FROM provider_resource_identities p WHERE id LIKE 'cp1-%') AS all_providers,
               (SELECT bool_and(NOT p.unverified_staging) FROM provider_resource_identities p WHERE id LIKE 'cp1-%') AS historical_providers_not_staged,
               (SELECT to_jsonb(d) FROM dispatch_admissions d WHERE id = '${legacyAdmissionId}') AS admission,
               (SELECT to_jsonb(i) FROM initial_message_intents i WHERE id = '${legacyIntentId}') AS intent,
               (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(d) ORDER BY d.id) FROM dispatch_admissions d WHERE id LIKE 'cp1-%') AS all_admissions,
               (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(i) ORDER BY i.id) FROM initial_message_intents i WHERE id LIKE 'cp1-%') AS all_intents,
               (SELECT count(*)::int FROM conversation_routes) AS route_count,
               (SELECT count(*)::int FROM conversations WHERE contact_id IS NULL) AS missing_contacts,
               (SELECT external_peer_id FROM contact_identities WHERE namespace = 'GROUP') AS group_peer
      `);
    });

    const before = oldSnapshot[0];
    const after = newSnapshot[0];
    expect(after.conversation).toMatchObject({ id: legacyConversationId, bu_id: 'cp1-legacy-bu', contact: '+55 (11) 99999-0000' });
    expect(after.conversation).toEqual(before.conversation);
    expect(after.all_conversations).toEqual(before.all_conversations);
    expect(after.message).toEqual(before.message);
    expect(after.all_messages).toEqual(before.all_messages);
    expect(after.provider).toEqual(before.provider);
    expect(after.all_providers).toEqual(before.all_providers);
    expect(after.historical_providers_not_staged).toBe(true);
    expect(after.admission).toEqual(before.admission);
    expect(after.intent).toEqual(before.intent);
    expect(after.all_admissions).toEqual(before.all_admissions);
    expect(after.all_intents).toEqual(before.all_intents);
    expect(after.route_count).toBe(0);
    expect(after.missing_contacts).toBe(0);
    expect(after.group_peer).toBe('120363000000000000@g.us');
    expect(after.contact_id).toEqual(expect.any(String));
  }, 120_000);

  it('aborts invalid and normalized-collision legacy peers before changing conversations or receipts', async () => {
    const abortSchema = `cp1_abort_${randomUUID().replaceAll('-', '')}`;
    const abortQuoteSchema = `"${abortSchema}"`;
    await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`CREATE SCHEMA ${abortQuoteSchema}`);
    const runSql = (sql: string) => execFileSync(
      process.execPath,
      ['node_modules/prisma/build/index.js', 'db', 'execute', '--stdin', '--schema', 'prisma/schema.prisma'],
      { cwd: process.cwd(), input: `SET search_path TO ${abortQuoteSchema};\n${sql}`, encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 120_000 },
    );
    const migrationSql = (name: string) => readFileSync(`prisma/migrations/${name}/migration.sql`, 'utf8');
    try {
      runSql(baselineMigrations.map(migrationSql).join('\n'));
      runSql(`
        INSERT INTO mantenedoras (id, name, updated_at) VALUES ('cp1-abort-owner', 'Synthetic abort owner', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO setores (id, mantenedora_id, name, created_at) VALUES ('cp1-abort-sector', 'cp1-abort-owner', 'Synthetic sector', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO business_units (id, mantenedora_id, setor_id, name, updated_at) VALUES ('cp1-abort-bu', 'cp1-abort-owner', 'cp1-abort-sector', 'Synthetic abort BU', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO conversations (id, bu_id, contact, contact_type, display_name, unread_count, created_at, updated_at) VALUES
          ('cp1-abort-collision-a', 'cp1-abort-bu', '5511999990000', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic A', 3, '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-abort-collision-b', 'cp1-abort-bu', '+55 (11) 99999-0000', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic B', 4, '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-abort-valid', 'cp1-abort-bu', '5511777770000', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic receipt peer', 8, '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-abort-invalid', 'cp1-abort-bu', 'not-a-phone', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic invalid', 5, '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-abort-malformed-digits', 'cp1-abort-bu', 'customer5511777770001', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic malformed digits', 9, '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-abort-empty', 'cp1-abort-bu', '', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic empty', 10, '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}'),
          ('cp1-abort-overlong', 'cp1-abort-bu', '${'1'.repeat(41)}', 'PESSOA', 'Synthetic overlong', 11, '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO messages (id, conversation_id, bu_id, direction, content, channel_origin, status, external_id, author_user_id, author_name, created_at)
          VALUES ('cp1-abort-message', 'cp1-abort-valid', 'cp1-abort-bu', 'IN', 'synthetic preserved receipt', 'OFICIAL', 'RECEBIDA', 'cp1-abort-external-message', 'cp1-abort-actor', 'Synthetic actor', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO dispatch_admissions (id, conversation_id, bu_id, caller, actor_id, owner_nonce, selected_context, state, expires_at, dispatch_deadline_at, created_at, updated_at) VALUES
          ('cp1-abort-admission', 'cp1-abort-valid', 'cp1-abort-bu', 'BFF', 'cp1-abort-actor', 'cp1-abort-admission-nonce', '{"channelId":"cp1-abort-channel","buId":"cp1-abort-bu","channelOrigin":"OFICIAL","providerResourceIdentityId":"cp1-abort-provider","generation":7,"pairingEpoch":3,"activeCredentialVersion":2,"requiresWindow":true,"providerAccountReference":"synthetic-account"}', 'UNCERTAIN', '${deadline}', '${deadline}', '${createdAt}', '${createdAt}');
        INSERT INTO initial_message_intents (id, conversation_id, bu_id, actor_id, idempotency_key, owner_nonce, request_fingerprint, request, selected_context, state, lease_expires_at, dispatch_deadline_at, external_message_id, created_at, updated_at) VALUES

      }

      expect(error).toMatch(/invalid_people=4/);
      expect(error).toMatch(/normalized_collisions=2/);
```

### api-app/test/number-onboarding-caller-scoping.e2e-spec.ts — 7196 bytes SHA256 8b9ef40da121a967540f149f44ec2f59f518d658069049ad02fbe61818fa6924

```text

import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { sealChannelCredential } from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-crypto';
import { META_APP_SECRET, criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, limparBanco, login, metaFake, subirApp, typebotFake, criarConversaFixture, criarRotaConversaFixture, obterSelecaoRespostaV2 } from './helpers';

describe('provider credential is scoped to each official resource across callers (PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication; let prisma: PrismaService;
  let tenant: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>; let cookie: string;
  const resources = [
    { buName: 'BFF resource', id: 'resource-bff-01', token: 'resource-token-bff' },
    { buName: 'Public resource', id: 'resource-public-02', token: 'resource-token-public' },
    { buName: 'Typebot resource', id: 'resource-typebot-03', token: 'resource-token-typebot' },
  ];

  beforeAll(async () => { ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp()); });
  afterAll(async () => { if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma); await app?.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Scoped caller tenant', 'scoped-caller@example.test');
    cookie = await login(app, 'scoped-caller@example.test');
    metaFake.credentialByPhone.clear(); metaFake.credentialCalls.length = 0;
    typebotFake.enabled = true;
  });

  it('uses distinct channel credentials for BFF, public API and Typebot; rejects cross-resource token reuse', async () => {
    const setor = await prisma.setor.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id } });
    const channels: Array<{ buId: string; channelId: string; resourceId: string; token: string }> = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < resources.length; i++) {
      const resource = resources[i];
      const bu = i === 0 ? tenant.businessUnit : await prisma.businessUnit.create({ data: {
        mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id, setorId: setor.id, name: resource.buName, phone: null,
      } });
      if (i > 0) await prisma.userBuRole.create({ data: { userId: tenant.owner.id, buId: bu.id, role: 'OWNER', grantedViaTipo: 'DIRETO' } });
      const { channel } = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, bu.id, 'OFICIAL', resource.id, { requiresWindow: false, provedWabaId: `synthetic-${resource.id}-waba` });
      const credential = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: channel.id } });
      const key = Buffer.from(process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY!, 'base64');
      const envelope = sealChannelCredential(resource.token, credential.keyId, key, {
        purpose: 'active', provider: 'OFICIAL', buId: bu.id, channelId: channel.id,
        generation: channel.generation, version: credential.version,
      });
      await prisma.channelCredential.update({ where: { channelId: channel.id }, data: {
        nonce: new Uint8Array(envelope.nonce), ciphertext: new Uint8Array(envelope.ciphertext), tag: new Uint8Array(envelope.tag),
      } });
      metaFake.credentialByPhone.set(resource.id, resource.token);
      channels.push({ buId: bu.id, channelId: channel.id, resourceId: resource.id, token: resource.token });
    }

    const bffConversation = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId: channels[0].buId, contact: '5584910000001', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED' });
    const bffInboundAt = new Date();
    const bffResource = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channels[0].channelId } });
    await prisma.message.create({ data: { conversationId: bffConversation.id, buId: channels[0].buId, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO', content: 'BFF inbound', channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA', providerResourceIdentityId: bffResource.id, officialInboundAt: bffInboundAt } });
    await criarRotaConversaFixture(prisma, { conversationId: bffConversation.id, channelId: channels[0].channelId });
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${bffConversation.id}/messages`).set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
      .send({ texto: 'reply from BFF', ...await obterSelecaoRespostaV2(app, bffConversation.id, cookie, channels[0].channelId) }).expect(201);

    const keyResponse = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/bff/integracao/api-keys').set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
      .send({ nome: 'Scoped public', buIds: [channels[1].buId] }).expect(201);
    const publicContact = '5584910000002';
    const publicInbound = { entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: { metadata: { phone_number_id: channels[1].resourceId }, messages: [{ id: 'wamid-scoped-public-inbound', from: publicContact, timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), type: 'text', text: { body: 'public inbound window' } }] } }] }] };
    const publicSignature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(JSON.stringify(publicInbound)).digest('hex')}`;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('x-hub-signature-256', publicSignature).send(publicInbound).expect(200);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/v1/messages').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${keyResponse.body.chave}`)
      .send({ account_id: channels[1].buId, telefone: '5584910000002', texto: 'reply from public API' }).expect(201);

    const bot = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'scoped-typebot', publishedTypebotId: 'published-scoped-typebot', status: 'ATIVO', name: 'Scoped bot', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/scoped' } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: channels[2].buId } });
    const body = { entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: { metadata: { phone_number_id: channels[2].resourceId }, messages: [{ id: 'wamid-scoped-typebot', from: '5584910000003', timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), type: 'text', text: { body: 'start scoped bot' } }] } }] }] };
    const signature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(JSON.stringify(body)).digest('hex')}`;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('x-hub-signature-256', signature).send(body).expect(200);

    expect(metaFake.credentialCalls).toEqual([
      { phoneNumberId: channels[0].resourceId, accessToken: channels[0].token },
      { phoneNumberId: channels[1].resourceId, accessToken: channels[1].token },
      { phoneNumberId: channels[2].resourceId, accessToken: channels[2].token },
    ]);
    const wrongBefore = metaFake.enviados.length;
    await expect(metaFake.enviarTexto(channels[0].resourceId, '5584910000001', 'wrong resource token', channels[1].token)).rejects.toThrow('scoped credential required');
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(wrongBefore);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: { in: channels.map((c) => c.buId) }, direction: 'OUT', status: 'ENVIADA' } })).toBe(3);
  });
});


```

### api-app/test/number-onboarding-message-acceptance.e2e-spec.ts — 6703 bytes SHA256 836aeda3eed8ae0a934ef36ad6eb37ce81673e734a429accdbf4b2fb3628ddc7

```text

import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { MessageAcceptanceService } from '../src/domain/inbox/message-acceptance.service';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { criarMantenedora, limparBanco, subirApp, criarConversaFixture } from './helpers';

describe('number onboarding keyed message acceptance (PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let acceptance: MessageAcceptanceService;
  let buId: string;
  let channelId: string;
  let identityId: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Acceptance fixture', 'acceptance-fixture@example.test');
    buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.create({
      data: { buId, type: 'OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTADO', phoneNumberId: 'phone-acceptance-fixture', ownNumberVerified: true, verifiedEpoch: 0 },
    });
    channelId = channel.id;
    const identity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({
      data: { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: 'phone-acceptance-fixture', buId, currentChannelId: channelId },
    });
    identityId = identity.id;
    await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId, contact: '5584999000002', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'IN_SERVICE' });
    acceptance = new MessageAcceptanceService(prisma);
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    if (prisma) {
      await prisma.message.deleteMany({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identityId } });
      await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.deleteMany({ where: { id: identityId } });
      await limparBanco(prisma);
    }
    await app?.close();
  });

  it('accepts one incoming key once, creating one conversation and unread increment', async () => {
    const input = baseInput({ externalMessageId: 'wamid-in-fixture-1', direction: 'IN', content: 'hello' });
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([acceptance.accept(input), acceptance.accept(input)]);
    expect(a.message.id).toBe(b.message.id);
    expect([a.creationWinner, b.creationWinner].filter(Boolean)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect([a.reconciled, b.reconciled].filter(Boolean)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identityId, externalId: input.externalMessageId } })).toBe(1);
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: a.conversation.id } });
    expect(conversation.unreadCount).toBe(1);
  });

  it('reports the first conversation once and keeps a second immediate message in the same conversation', async () => {
    const first = await acceptance.accept(baseInput({ externalMessageId: 'wamid-in-first-conversation', contact: '5584999000003' }));
    const second = await acceptance.accept(baseInput({ externalMessageId: 'wamid-in-second-message', contact: '5584999000003' }));
    expect(first.conversationCreated).toBe(true);
    expect(first.reopened).toBe(false);
    expect(second.conversationCreated).toBe(false);
    expect(second.reopened).toBe(false);
    expect(second.conversation.id).toBe(first.conversation.id);
    expect(second.conversation.unreadCount).toBe(2);
  });

  it('echo-first and sender result reconcile to the same OUT row without changing its identity or createdAt', async () => {
    const echo = await acceptance.accept(baseInput({
      externalMessageId: 'wamid-out-fixture-1', direction: 'OUT', content: 'echo body', contact: '5584999000002',
    }));
    const createdAt = echo.message.createdAt;
    const sender = await acceptance.accept(baseInput({
      externalMessageId: 'wamid-out-fixture-1', direction: 'OUT', content: 'trusted sender body', conversationId: echo.conversation.id,
      contact: '5584999000002',
      senderResult: true, authorName: 'Support fixture',
    }));
    expect(sender.message.id).toBe(echo.message.id);
    expect(sender.message.createdAt).toEqual(createdAt);
    expect(sender.message.authorName).toBe('Support fixture');
    expect(sender.message.content).toBe('trusted sender body');
    expect(sender.creationWinner).toBe(false);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identityId, externalId: 'wamid-out-fixture-1' } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { id: echo.conversation.id } })).toBe(1);
    const laterEcho = await acceptance.accept(baseInput({
      externalMessageId: 'wamid-out-fixture-1', direction: 'OUT', content: 'untrusted later echo', contact: '5584999000002',
      conversationId: echo.conversation.id,
    }));
    expect(laterEcho.message.id).toBe(echo.message.id);
    expect(laterEcho.message.createdAt).toEqual(createdAt);
    expect(laterEcho.message.content).toBe('trusted sender body');
    expect(laterEcho.message.authorName).toBe('Support fixture');
  });

  it('persists redacted diagnostics only while the verified identity remains current', async () => {
    const context = {
      providerResourceIdentityId: identityId, buId, channelId, generation: 1, pairingEpoch: 0, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL' as const,
    };
    await acceptance.persistVerifiedDiagnostic(context, 'messages.received', {
      providerId: 'safe-fixture', apikey: 'fake-apikey-fixture', nested: { access_token: 'fake-token-fixture' },
    });
    const event = await prisma.webhookEvent.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, eventType: 'messages.received' } });
    expect(event.payload).toEqual({
      providerId: 'safe-fixture', apikey: '[REDACTED]', nested: { access_token: '[REDACTED]' },
    });
    await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: identityId }, data: { currentChannelId: null, retiredAt: new Date() } });
    await expect(acceptance.persistVerifiedDiagnostic(context, 'messages.late', { content: 'must not persist' }))
      .rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'IDENTITY_NOT_CURRENT' } });
    expect(await prisma.webhookEvent.count({ where: { buId, eventType: 'messages.late' } })).toBe(0);
    await expect(prisma.$executeRaw`UPDATE provider_resource_identities SET bu_id = 'different-owner-fixture' WHERE id = ${identityId}`)
      .rejects.toThrow();
  });

  function baseInput(overrides: Partial<Parameters<MessageAcceptanceService['accept']>[0]>) {
    return {
      providerResourceIdentityId: identityId, externalMessageId: 'fixture-id', buId, channelId,
      generation: 1, pairingEpoch: 0, contact: '5584999000001', contactType: 'PESSOA' as const,
      channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL' as const, messageType: 'TEXTO' as const, direction: 'IN' as const,
      content: 'fixture', ...overrides,
    };
  }
});
```

### api-app/test/painel.e2e-spec.ts — 2933 bytes SHA256 910975e63b3ee48a983107a9aaf4906924a103f6b353481f03dccfb934d64237

```text

import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { criarMantenedora, criarUsuario, limparBanco, login, subirApp, criarConversaFixture } from './helpers';

describe('painel', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let A: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let B: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let cookie: string;

  const painel = (c: string) => request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/painel').set('Cookie', c);

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  });
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    A = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Alfa', 'dono@alfa.test');
    B = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Beta', 'dono@beta.test');
    cookie = await login(app, 'dono@alfa.test');
  });

  it('conta o atendimento e avisa do número sem canal', async () => {
    await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId: A.businessUnit.id, contact: '5511900000001', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED' });

    const r = await painel(cookie).expect(200);
    expect(r.body.atendimento.emEspera).toBe(1);
    expect(r.body.cadastros.numeros).toBe(1);
    expect(r.body.cadastros.setores).toBe(1);
    // Recém-criado, o número não tem canal — e é isso que explica "não chega nada".
    expect(r.body.cadastros.numerosSemCanal).toBe(1);
  });

  it('cross-tenant: NENHUM número da Mantenedora B entra na conta de A', async () => {
    await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId: B.businessUnit.id, contact: '5511900000002', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED' });

    const r = await painel(cookie).expect(200);
    // Agregado é tão vazável quanto linha: o total precisa respeitar o escopo.
    expect(r.body.atendimento.emEspera).toBe(0);
    expect(r.body.cadastros.numeros).toBe(1);
  });

  it('o ATENDENTE vê os números, e NÃO vê o extrato de atividades', async () => {
    await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'atendente@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
    const c = await login(app, 'atendente@alfa.test');

    const r = await painel(c).expect(200);
    expect(r.body.cadastros.numeros).toBe(1);
    // Ler o histórico é de quem administra — esconder o item de menu não bastaria.
    expect(r.body.ultimasAtividades).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('o ADMIN vê o extrato', async () => {
    await request(app.getHttpServer())
      .post('/api/bff/setores')
      .set('Cookie', cookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
      .send({ nome: 'Comercial' })
      .expect(201);

    const r = await painel(cookie).expect(200);
    expect(r.body.ultimasAtividades[0].texto).toBe('criou o setor Comercial');
  });
});


```

### api-app/test/setores.e2e-spec.ts — 17500 bytes SHA256 8eec2be051aa9d71116b57b84ff5c3e863aca2005f61782c33ea87bd868cd203

```text

import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { criarMantenedora, criarUsuario, limparBanco, login, subirApp, criarConversaFixture } from './helpers';

describe('setores (ADR-0017)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let A: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let cookieOwnerA: string;

  const req = () => request(app.getHttpServer());
  const post = (cookie: string, url: string, body: object) =>
    req().post(url).set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send(body);

  async function criarSetor(cookie: string, nome: string): Promise<string> {
    const r = await post(cookie, '/api/bff/setores', { nome }).expect(201);
    return r.body.id;
  }

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    A = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Alfa', 'dono@alfa.test');
    await criarMantenedora(app, 'Beta', 'dono@beta.test');
    cookieOwnerA = await login(app, 'dono@alfa.test');
  });

  describe('o Geral nasce com a Mantenedora', () => {
    it('a primeira BU já vem num setor — nenhum número fica sem', async () => {
      const bu = await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { id: A.businessUnit.id },
        select: { setor: true },
      });
      expect(bu.setor.name).toBe('Geral');
    });

    it('nenhum business_unit sem setor_id, em nenhuma Mantenedora', async () => {
      const semSetor = await prisma.$queryRaw<{ n: bigint }[]>`
        SELECT count(*) AS n FROM business_units WHERE setor_id IS NULL
      `;
      expect(Number(semSetor[0].n)).toBe(0);
    });
  });

  describe('CRUD — o trio (ADR-0012 §2)', () => {
    it('autorizado: OWNER cria, renomeia e inativa', async () => {
      const id = await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Comercial');

      const renomeado = await req()
        .patch(`/api/bff/setores/${id}`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwnerA)
        .set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .send({ nome: 'Vendas', status: 'INATIVO' })
        .expect(200);

      expect(renomeado.body).toMatchObject({ nome: 'Vendas', status: 'INATIVO', numeros: 0 });
    });

    it('papel insuficiente: ATENDENTE recebe 403 ao criar', async () => {
      await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'atendente@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      const cookie = await login(app, 'atendente@alfa.test');

      const r = await post(cookie, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Não vai' }).expect(403);
      expect(r.body.code).toBe('PAPEL_INSUFICIENTE');
    });

    it('cross-tenant: setor da Mantenedora B responde 404 para o OWNER de A', async () => {
      const cookieB = await login(app, 'dono@beta.test');
      const doB = await criarSetor(cookieB, 'Suporte');

      await req()
        .patch(`/api/bff/setores/${doB}`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwnerA)
        .set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .send({ nome: 'Invadido' })
        .expect(404);

      // E continua intacto do outro lado.
      const s = await prisma.setor.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: doB } });
      expect(s.name).toBe('Suporte');
    });

    it('o nome é único DENTRO da Mantenedora — duas contas podem ter "Comercial"', async () => {
      await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Comercial');
      const r = await post(cookieOwnerA, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Comercial' }).expect(409);
      expect(r.body.code).toBe('SETOR_DUPLICADO');

      const cookieB = await login(app, 'dono@beta.test');
      await post(cookieB, '/api/bff/setores', { nome: 'Comercial' }).expect(201);
    });

    it('setor COM número não é excluído — só inativado', async () => {
      const id = await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Comercial');
      await post(cookieOwnerA, '/api/bff/business-units', { nome: 'Número novo', setorId: id }).expect(201);

      const r = await req()
        .delete(`/api/bff/setores/${id}`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwnerA)
        .set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .expect(409);
      expect(r.body.code).toBe('SETOR_COM_NUMEROS');
    });
  });

  describe('concessão por setor é EXPANSÃO (ADR-0017 §2)', () => {
    let comercial: string;
    let bu1: string;
    let bu2: string;
    let ana: string;

    beforeEach(async () => {
      comercial = await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Comercial');
      bu1 = (await post(cookieOwnerA, '/api/bff/business-units', { nome: 'Num 1', setorId: comercial })).body.id;
      bu2 = (await post(cookieOwnerA, '/api/bff/business-units', { nome: 'Num 2', setorId: comercial })).body.id;
      ana = await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'ana@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
    });

    it('materializa uma linha POR NÚMERO do setor, com proveniência SETOR', async () => {
      const r = await post(cookieOwnerA, `/api/bff/setores/${comercial}/grants`, {
        userId: ana,
        papel: 'ATENDENTE',
      }).expect(201);
      expect(r.body.concedidas).toBe(2);

      const grants = await prisma.userBuRole.findMany({
        where: { userId: ana, grantedViaTipo: 'SETOR', grantedViaId: comercial },
        select: { buId: true },
      });
      expect(grants.map((g) => g.buId).sort()).toEqual([bu1, bu2].sort());
    });

    it('BU criada DEPOIS, no setor concedido, já nasce com o grant (regra 3)', async () => {
      await post(cookieOwnerA, `/api/bff/setores/${comercial}/grants`, { userId: ana }).expect(201);

      const bu3 = (await post(cookieOwnerA, '/api/bff/business-units', { nome: 'Num 3', setorId: comercial }))
        .body.id;

      const grant = await prisma.userBuRole.findFirst({ where: { userId: ana, buId: bu3 } });
      expect(grant?.grantedViaTipo).toBe('SETOR');
    });

    it('revogar o setor NÃO remove grant direto nem o da Mantenedora', async () => {
      // ana tem grant DIRETO na BU original (criarUsuario) e expansão no Comercial.
      await post(cookieOwnerA, `/api/bff/setores/${comercial}/grants`, { userId: ana }).expect(201);

      await req()
        .delete(`/api/bff/setores/${comercial}/grants/${ana}`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwnerA)
        .set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .expect(200);

      const restantes = await prisma.userBuRole.findMany({ where: { userId: ana } });
      expect(restantes).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(restantes[0].buId).toBe(A.businessUnit.id);
      expect(restantes[0].grantedViaTipo).toBe('DIRETO');
    });
  });

  describe('ver quem tem o setor — a leitura que faltava', () => {
    it('autorizado: lista quem recebeu o setor, com papel e desde quando', async () => {
      const comercial = await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Comercial');
      const ana = await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'ana@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      await post(cookieOwnerA, `/api/bff/setores/${comercial}/grants`, { userId: ana }).expect(201);

      const r = await req().get(`/api/bff/setores/${comercial}/grants`).set('Cookie', cookieOwnerA).expect(200);
      expect(r.body.grants).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(r.body.grants[0]).toMatchObject({ userId: ana, nome: 'ana@alfa.test', papel: 'ATENDENTE' });
    });

    it('papel insuficiente: ATENDENTE recebe 403', async () => {
      const comercial = await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Comercial');
      await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'atendente@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      const c = await login(app, 'atendente@alfa.test');

      await req().get(`/api/bff/setores/${comercial}/grants`).set('Cookie', c).expect(403);
    });

    it('cross-tenant: setor da Mantenedora B responde 404', async () => {
      const cookieB = await login(app, 'dono@beta.test');
      const doB = await criarSetor(cookieB, 'Suporte');
      await req().get(`/api/bff/setores/${doB}/grants`).set('Cookie', cookieOwnerA).expect(404);
    });

    it('a lista de pessoas diz DE QUAL ORIGEM veio cada acesso', async () => {
      const comercial = await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Comercial');
      const bu = (await post(cookieOwnerA, '/api/bff/business-units', { nome: 'Num', setorId: comercial })).body.id;
      const ana = await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'ana@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      await post(cookieOwnerA, `/api/bff/setores/${comercial}/grants`, { userId: ana }).expect(201);

      const r = await req().get('/api/bff/usuarios').set('Cookie', cookieOwnerA).expect(200);
      const dela = r.body.usuarios.find((u: { id: string }) => u.id === ana);

      // Sem a origem não dá para responder "por que esta pessoa alcança este
      // número?", que é a pergunta de quem revisa acesso.
      const porSetor = dela.grants.find((g: { buId: string }) => g.buId === bu);
      expect(porSetor).toMatchObject({ origem: 'SETOR', origemId: comercial });
      const direto = dela.grants.find((g: { buId: string }) => g.buId === A.businessUnit.id);
      expect(direto).toMatchObject({ origem: 'DIRETO', origemId: null });
    });
  });

  describe('renomear e inativar o número', () => {
    const patch = (cookie: string, buId: string, body: object) =>
      req().patch(`/api/bff/business-units/${buId}`).set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send(body);

    it('autorizado: renomeia, e o histórico guarda de/para', async () => {
      const r = await patch(cookieOwnerA, A.businessUnit.id, { nome: 'Comercial 01' }).expect(200);
      expect(r.body).toMatchObject({ nome: 'Comercial 01', status: 'ATIVO' });

      const l = await prisma.activityLog.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { action: 'numero.editado' } });
      expect(l.campo).toBe('nome');
      expect(l.para).toBe('Comercial 01');
      expect(l.texto).toContain('renomeou o número');
    });

    it('inativar NÃO apaga — a BU é dona das conversas', async () => {
      const conversa = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId: A.businessUnit.id, contact: '5511900001111', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED' });

      await patch(cookieOwnerA, A.businessUnit.id, { status: 'INATIVO' }).expect(200);

      expect(await prisma.businessUnit.findUnique({ where: { id: A.businessUnit.id } })).not.toBeNull();
      expect(await prisma.conversation.findUnique({ where: { id: conversa.id } })).not.toBeNull();
    });

    it('nome vazio é recusado', async () => {
      const r = await patch(cookieOwnerA, A.businessUnit.id, { nome: '  ' }).expect(400);
      expect(r.body.code).toBe('PAYLOAD_INVALIDO');
    });

    it('papel insuficiente: ATENDENTE recebe 403', async () => {
      await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'atendente@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      const c = await login(app, 'atendente@alfa.test');
      await patch(c, A.businessUnit.id, { nome: 'Invadido' }).expect(403);
    });

    it('cross-tenant: número da Mantenedora B responde 404', async () => {
      const B = await prisma.businessUnit.findFirstOrThrow({
        where: { mantenedora: { name: 'Beta' } },
      });
      await patch(cookieOwnerA, B.id, { nome: 'Invadido' }).expect(404);
      expect((await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: B.id } })).name).not.toBe('Invadido');
    });
  });

  describe('mover número entre setores — a escrita mais delicada do modelo', () => {
    let comercial: string;
    let suporte: string;
    let numero: string;
    let ana: string;
    let bia: string;

    beforeEach(async () => {
      comercial = await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Comercial');
      suporte = await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Suporte');
      numero = (await post(cookieOwnerA, '/api/bff/business-units', { nome: 'Num', setorId: comercial })).body.id;

      ana = await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'ana@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      bia = await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'bia@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      await post(cookieOwnerA, `/api/bff/setores/${comercial}/grants`, { userId: ana }).expect(201);
      await post(cookieOwnerA, `/api/bff/setores/${suporte}/grants`, { userId: bia }).expect(201);
    });

    const mover = (cookie: string, buId: string, setorId: string) =>
      post(cookie, `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/setor`, { setorId });

    it('quem tinha pelo setor antigo perde; quem tem o novo ganha', async () => {
      const r = await mover(cookieOwnerA, numero, suporte).expect(201);
      expect(r.body.setor.nome).toBe('Suporte');

      expect(await prisma.userBuRole.findFirst({ where: { userId: ana, buId: numero } })).toBeNull();
      const daBia = await prisma.userBuRole.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { userId: bia, buId: numero } });
      expect(daBia.grantedViaTipo).toBe('SETOR');
      expect(daBia.grantedViaId).toBe(suporte);
    });

    it('o grant DIRETO sobrevive à mudança de setor', async () => {
      const caio = await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, numero, 'caio@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');

      await mover(cookieOwnerA, numero, suporte).expect(201);

      const dele = await prisma.userBuRole.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { userId: caio, buId: numero } });
      expect(dele.grantedViaTipo).toBe('DIRETO');
    });

    it('recusa quando mover deixaria o número sem OWNER', async () => {
      // Um número cujo ÚNICO OWNER veio do setor de origem.
      const orfao = await prisma.businessUnit.create({
        data: { mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id, setorId: comercial, name: 'Orfão' },
      });
      await prisma.userBuRole.deleteMany({ where: { buId: orfao.id } });
      await prisma.userBuRole.create({
        data: {
          userId: A.owner.id,
          buId: orfao.id,
          role: 'OWNER',
          grantedViaTipo: 'SETOR',
          grantedViaId: comercial,
        },
      });

      const r = await mover(cookieOwnerA, orfao.id, suporte).expect(409);
      expect(r.body.code).toBe('NUMERO_FICARIA_SEM_OWNER');

      // E nada foi aplicado: a recusa não deixa estado pela metade.
      const bu = await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: orfao.id } });
      expect(bu.setorId).toBe(comercial);
      expect(await prisma.userBuRole.count({ where: { buId: orfao.id } })).toBe(1);
    });

    it('cross-setor: o atendente do Comercial recebe 404 na conversa do Suporte', async () => {
      const doSuporte = (
        await post(cookieOwnerA, '/api/bff/business-units', { nome: 'Num suporte', setorId: suporte })
      ).body.id;
      const conversa = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId: doSuporte, contact: '5511999990000', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED' });

      const cookieAna = await login(app, 'ana@alfa.test');
      await req().get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversa.id}/messages`).set('Cookie', cookieAna).expect(404);
    });
  });

  it('conceder um setor VAZIO é lembrado — o número criado depois já nasce com o acesso', async () => {
    // O caso que o modelo derivado perdia em silêncio: sem número, não havia
    // linha materializada de onde derivar a concessão.
    const novo = await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Financeiro');
    const ana = await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'ana@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');

    const r = await post(cookieOwnerA, `/api/bff/setores/${novo}/grants`, { userId: ana }).expect(201);
    expect(r.body.concedidas).toBe(0); // não há número ainda

    const bu = (await post(cookieOwnerA, '/api/bff/business-units', { nome: 'Num', setorId: novo })).body.id;

    const grant = await prisma.userBuRole.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { userId: ana, buId: bu } });
    expect(grant.grantedViaTipo).toBe('SETOR');
    expect(grant.grantedViaId).toBe(novo);
  });

  it('revogar apaga a CONCESSÃO — o próximo número não ressuscita o acesso', async () => {
    const novo = await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Financeiro');
    const ana = await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'ana@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
    await post(cookieOwnerA, `/api/bff/setores/${novo}/grants`, { userId: ana }).expect(201);

    await req()
      .delete(`/api/bff/setores/${novo}/grants/${ana}`)
      .set('Cookie', cookieOwnerA)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
      .expect(200);

    const bu = (await post(cookieOwnerA, '/api/bff/business-units', { nome: 'Num', setorId: novo })).body.id;
    expect(await prisma.userBuRole.findFirst({ where: { userId: ana, buId: bu } })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('setor INATIVO não interrompe atendimento — só some do cadastro', async () => {
    const comercial = await criarSetor(cookieOwnerA, 'Comercial');
    const numero = (await post(cookieOwnerA, '/api/bff/business-units', { nome: 'Num', setorId: comercial }))
      .body.id;
    const conversa = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId: numero, contact: '5511988887777', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED' });

    await req()
      .patch(`/api/bff/setores/${comercial}`)
      .set('Cookie', cookieOwnerA)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
      .send({ status: 'INATIVO' })
      .expect(200);

    // A conversa continua acessível: sumir com a inbox por engano de cadastro
    // seria perder atendimento (ADR-0017 §1).
    await req().get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversa.id}/messages`).set('Cookie', cookieOwnerA).expect(200);
  });
});
```
