```text
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { ChannelCredentialResolver } from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-resolver';
import { ChannelResolver } from '../src/domain/channels/transport/channel';
import {
  EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET,
  META_APP_SECRET,
  criarCanalVerificado,
  criarMantenedora,
  limparBanco,
  login,
  typebotFake,
  metaFake,
  subirApp,
} from './helpers';

describe('webhooks de entrada', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let A: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let B: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;

  const PHONE_A = 'phone-da-alfa';
  const INSTANCIA_A = 'instancia-da-alfa';
  const WABA_A = '123456789';
  const WABA_B = '456789123';

  const assinar = (corpo: object) =>
    'sha256=' + createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(JSON.stringify(corpo)).digest('hex');

  const eventoMeta = (de: string, texto: string, phoneNumberId = PHONE_A, timestamp: string | number | null = String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000))) => ({
    entry: [
      {
        changes: [
          {
            field: 'messages',
            value: {
              metadata: { phone_number_id: phoneNumberId },
              contacts: [{ profile: { name: 'Cliente' } }],
              messages: [{ id: `wamid-${texto}`, from: de, type: 'text', ...(timestamp === null ? {} : { timestamp }), text: { body: texto } }],
            },
          },
        ],
      },
    ],
  });

  const postMeta = (corpo: object, assinatura?: string) => {
    const req = request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('content-type', 'application/json');
    if (assinatura !== undefined) req.set('x-hub-signature-256', assinatura);
    return req.send(corpo);
  };

  const postEvolution = (corpo: object, segredo = EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET) =>
    request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', segredo).send(corpo);

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    A = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Alfa', 'dono@alfa.test');
    B = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Beta', 'dono@beta.test');
    await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, A.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', PHONE_A, { requiresWindow: true, provedWabaId: WABA_A });
    await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, A.businessUnit.id, 'NAO_OFICIAL', INSTANCIA_A, { requiresWindow: false });
  });

  it('recusa credencial Evolution no caminho aposentado sem gravar evento', async () => {
    const body = { event: 'connection.update', instance: INSTANCIA_A, data: { state: 'open' } };
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/webhooks/evolution/${EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET}`).send(body).expect(410);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', 'wrong').send(body).expect(403);
    expect(await prisma.webhookEvent.count()).toBe(0);
  });

  describe('Meta — assinatura (fecha o risco S2)', () => {
    it('aceita o evento assinado corretamente e cria a conversa', async () => {
      const corpo = eventoMeta('5584999991111', 'oi');
      await postMeta(corpo, assinar(corpo)).expect(200);

      const conversa = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id } });
      expect(conversa.contact).toBe('5584999991111');
      expect(conversa.status).toBe('QUEUED');
      expect(conversa.unreadCount).toBe(1);
      expect(conversa.displayName).toBe('Cliente');
    });

    it('persists only a positive, nonfuture signed Meta timestamp as trusted inbound time', async () => {
      const nowSeconds = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
      const valid = eventoMeta('5584999991112', 'trusted-time', PHONE_A, String(nowSeconds - 60));
      const missing = eventoMeta('5584999991113', 'missing-time', PHONE_A, null);
      const malformed = eventoMeta('5584999991114', 'malformed-time', PHONE_A, 'yesterday');
      const future = eventoMeta('5584999991115', 'future-time', PHONE_A, nowSeconds + 3600);
      const old = eventoMeta('5584999991116', 'old-signed-time', PHONE_A, nowSeconds - 30 * 60 * 60);
      const duplicateTrusted = eventoMeta('5584999991112', 'trusted-time', PHONE_A, nowSeconds + 3600);

      for (const body of [valid, missing, malformed, future, old, duplicateTrusted]) await postMeta(body, assinar(body)).expect(200);

      const identity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: PHONE_A } },
      });
      const rows = await prisma.message.findMany({
        where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identity.id },
        orderBy: { externalId: 'asc' },
      });
      const byId = new Map(rows.map((row) => [row.externalId, row]));
      expect(byId.get('wamid-trusted-time')?.officialInboundAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
      expect(Math.abs(byId.get('wamid-trusted-time')!.officialInboundAt!.getTime() - (nowSeconds - 60) * 1000)).toBeLessThan(1000);
      for (const id of ['wamid-missing-time', 'wamid-malformed-time', 'wamid-future-time']) {
        expect(byId.get(id)?.officialInboundAt).toBeNull();
      }
      expect(byId.get('wamid-old-signed-time')?.officialInboundAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
      expect(byId.get('wamid-old-signed-time')!.officialInboundAt!.getTime()).toBeLessThan(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
      expect(rows).toHaveLength(5);
    });

    it('does not promote an inbound timestamp received while the current resource is unmanaged', async () => {
      const identity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: PHONE_A } },
      });
      expect(identity.currentChannelId).not.toBeNull();
      await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: identity.id }, data: { managedLifecycle: false } });

      const contact = '5584999999901';
      const nowSeconds = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
      const unmanagedBody = eventoMeta(contact, 'unmanaged-lifecycle-inbound', PHONE_A, String(nowSeconds - 60));
      const providerCallsBefore = metaFake.providerCalls;
      const providerSendsBefore = metaFake.enviados.length;
      await postMeta(unmanagedBody, assinar(unmanagedBody)).expect(200);

      const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow({
        where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id, contact },
      });
      const first = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({
        where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identity.id, externalId: 'wamid-unmanaged-lifecycle-inbound' },
      });
      expect.soft(first).toMatchObject({ direction: 'IN', status: 'RECEBIDA', officialInboundAt: null });
      expect.soft(conversation.unreadCount).toBe(1);
      const resolver = app.get(ChannelResolver);
      expect.soft((await resolver.inspectForConversation({ conversationId: conversation.id, buId: A.businessUnit.id })).windowOpen)
        .toBe(false);

      await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: identity.id }, data: { managedLifecycle: true } });
      expect.soft((await resolver.inspectForConversation({ conversationId: conversation.id, buId: A.businessUnit.id })).windowOpen)
        .toBe(false);
      await postMeta(unmanagedBody, assinar(unmanagedBody)).expect(200);
      const replayed = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({
        where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identity.id, externalId: 'wamid-unmanaged-lifecycle-inbound' },
      });
      expect.soft(replayed.officialInboundAt).toBeNull();
      expect.soft(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: conversation.id } })).toBe(1);
      expect.soft((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversation.id } })).unreadCount).toBe(1);

      const qualifiedBody = eventoMeta(contact, 'managed-lifecycle-inbound', PHONE_A, String(nowSeconds - 1));
      await postMeta(qualifiedBody, assinar(qualifiedBody)).expect(200);
      const qualified = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({
        where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identity.id, externalId: 'wamid-managed-lifecycle-inbound' },
      });
      expect.soft(qualified.officialInboundAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
      expect.soft((await resolver.inspectForConversation({ conversationId: conversation.id, buId: A.businessUnit.id })).windowOpen)
        .toBe(true);
      expect.soft(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: conversation.id } })).toBe(2);
      expect.soft((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversation.id } })).unreadCount).toBe(2);
      expect(metaFake.providerCalls).toBe(providerCallsBefore);
      expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(providerSendsBefore);
    });

    it('opens the shared window only from the current resource trusted timestamp, never createdAt or a retired resource', async () => {
      const withoutTimestamp = eventoMeta('5584999991116', 'window-no-time', PHONE_A, null);
      await postMeta(withoutTimestamp, assinar(withoutTimestamp)).expect(200);
      const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow({
        where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id, contact: '5584999991116' },
      });
      const resolver = app.get(ChannelResolver);
      const reference = { conversationId: conversation.id, buId: A.businessUnit.id };
      const closed = await resolver.inspectForConversation(reference);
      expect(closed.windowOpen).toBe(false);
      expect(closed.reason).toBe('JANELA_FECHADA');

      const identity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: PHONE_A } },
      });
      const retired = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: {
        provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: 'phone-retired-window', buId: A.businessUnit.id,
        currentChannelId: null, retiredAt: new Date(), managedLifecycle: false, cleanupState: 'UNMANAGED',
      } });
      await prisma.message.create({ data: {
        conversationId: conversation.id, buId: A.businessUnit.id, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO',
        content: 'fixture', channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA', externalId: 'retired-window-proof',
        providerResourceIdentityId: retired.id, officialInboundAt: new Date(),
      } });
      expect((await resolver.inspectForConversation(reference)).windowOpen).toBe(false);

      const signed = eventoMeta('5584999991116', 'window-trusted', PHONE_A, String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - 30));
      await postMeta(signed, assinar(signed)).expect(200);
      const reopened = await resolver.inspectForConversation(reference);
      expect(reopened.windowOpen).toBe(true);
      expect(reopened.expiresAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
      expect(identity.currentChannelId).not.toBeNull();
    });

    it('rejeita identificadores opacos sem convertê-los em outro telefone', async () => {
      const corpo = eventoMeta('customer-bsuid-2026', 'opaque-contact');
      await postMeta(corpo, assinar(corpo)).expect(200);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count()).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.message.count()).toBe(0);
    });

    it('RECUSA evento sem assinatura — é o que os dois sistemas antigos aceitam hoje', async () => {
      const corpo = eventoMeta('5584999992222', 'injetada');
      await postMeta(corpo).expect(403);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count()).toBe(0);
    });

    it('RECUSA assinatura de outro segredo', async () => {
      const corpo = eventoMeta('5584999993333', 'forjada');
      const errada = 'sha256=' + createHmac('sha256', 'outro-segredo').update(JSON.stringify(corpo)).digest('hex');
      await postMeta(corpo, errada).expect(403);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count()).toBe(0);
    });

    it('RECUSA corpo alterado depois de assinado — a prova é sobre os bytes brutos', async () => {
      const original = eventoMeta('5584999994444', 'valor-original');
      const assinatura = assinar(original);
      const adulterado = eventoMeta('5584999994444', 'valor-trocado');
      await postMeta(adulterado, assinatura).expect(403);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count()).toBe(0);
    });

    it('a verificação de endpoint devolve o challenge só com o verify token certo', async () => {
      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get('/webhooks/meta?hub.mode=subscribe&hub.verify_token=verify-token-de-teste&hub.challenge=12345')
        .expect(200)
        .expect('12345');

      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get('/webhooks/meta?hub.mode=subscribe&hub.verify_token=errado&hub.challenge=12345')
        .expect(403);
    });
  });

  describe('Meta — alocação da BU', () => {
    it('número desconhecido não cria nada, e não encosta na outra Mantenedora', async () => {
      const corpo = eventoMeta('5584999995555', 'perdida', 'phone-que-ninguem-tem');
      await postMeta(corpo, assinar(corpo)).expect(200);

      expect(await prisma.conversation.count()).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: B.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(0);
      // Sem uma identidade durável não há BU confiável para associar nem payload para reter.
      expect(await prisma.webhookEvent.count()).toBe(0);
    });

    it('a conversa pendura na BU dona do NÚMERO que recebeu', async () => {
      const corpo = eventoMeta('5584999996666', 'oi');
      await postMeta(corpo, assinar(corpo)).expect(200);

      const conversa = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow();
      expect(conversa.buId).toBe(A.businessUnit.id);
    });

    it('partitions a signed mixed-owner batch by each verified phone resource', async () => {
      await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, B.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', 'phone-da-beta', { requiresWindow: true, provedWabaId: WABA_B });
      await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: {
        provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: 'phone-retired-beta', buId: B.businessUnit.id,
        currentChannelId: null, retiredAt: new Date(), managedLifecycle: false, cleanupState: 'UNMANAGED',
      } });
      const corpo = { entry: [{ changes: [
        { field: 'messages', value: { metadata: { phone_number_id: PHONE_A }, contacts: [{ profile: { name: 'Cliente Alfa' } }], messages: [{ id: 'wamid-mixed-alfa', from: '5584999910101', type: 'text', text: { body: 'para alfa' } }] } },
        { field: 'messages', value: { metadata: { phone_number_id: 'phone-da-beta' }, contacts: [{ profile: { name: 'Cliente Beta' } }], messages: [{ id: 'wamid-mixed-beta', from: '5584999920202', type: 'text', text: { body: 'para beta' } }] } },
        { field: 'messages', value: { metadata: { phone_number_id: 'phone-unknown-batch' }, messages: [{ id: 'wamid-mixed-unknown', from: '5584999930303', type: 'text', text: { body: 'must not persist unknown raw' } }] } },
        { field: 'messages', value: { metadata: { phone_number_id: 'phone-retired-beta' }, messages: [{ id: 'wamid-mixed-retired', from: '5584999940404', type: 'text', text: { body: 'must not persist retired raw' } }] } },
      ] }] };
      await postMeta(corpo, assinar(corpo)).expect(200);
      const alfa = await prisma.message.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId_externalId: { providerResourceIdentityId: (await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: PHONE_A } } })).id, externalId: 'wamid-mixed-alfa' } } });
      const betaIdentity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: 'phone-da-beta' } } });
      const beta = await prisma.message.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId_externalId: { providerResourceIdentityId: betaIdentity.id, externalId: 'wamid-mixed-beta' } } });
      expect(alfa.buId).toBe(A.businessUnit.id);
      expect(beta.buId).toBe(B.businessUnit.id);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(1);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: B.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(1);
      expect(await prisma.message.count()).toBe(2);
      const diagnostics = await prisma.webhookEvent.findMany({ orderBy: { receivedAt: 'asc' } });
      expect(diagnostics).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(JSON.stringify(diagnostics)).not.toMatch(/must not persist unknown raw|must not persist retired raw|wamid-mixed-unknown|wamid-mixed-retired/);
    });

    it('keys identical provider message IDs independently per resource and skips ID-less Meta records', async () => {
      await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, B.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', 'phone-resource-key-b', { requiresWindow: true, provedWabaId: WABA_B });
      const body = { entry: [{ changes: [
        { field: 'messages', value: { metadata: { phone_number_id: PHONE_A }, messages: [{ id: 'wamid-same-id-different-resource', from: '5584999960606', type: 'text', text: { body: 'A resource payload' } }] } },
        { field: 'messages', value: { metadata: { phone_number_id: 'phone-resource-key-b' }, messages: [{ id: 'wamid-same-id-different-resource', from: '5584999960606', type: 'text', text: { body: 'B resource payload' } }] } },
        { field: 'messages', value: { metadata: { phone_number_id: PHONE_A }, messages: [{ from: '5584999970707', type: 'text', text: { body: 'must not become a message' } }] } },
      ] }] };
      await postMeta(body, assinar(body)).expect(200);
      const rows = await prisma.message.findMany({ where: { externalId: 'wamid-same-id-different-resource' }, orderBy: { buId: 'asc' } });
      expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(new Set(rows.map((row) => row.providerResourceIdentityId)).size).toBe(2);
      expect(new Set(rows.map((row) => row.buId))).toEqual(new Set([A.businessUnit.id, B.businessUnit.id]));
      expect(await prisma.message.count()).toBe(2);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count()).toBe(2);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { contact: '5584999970707' } })).toBe(0);
    });
  });

  it('deduplicates ten concurrent Meta deliveries per key across three new contacts', async () => {
    const bot = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: {
      mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'meta-dedupe-bot', publishedTypebotId: 'meta-dedupe-published',
      name: 'Dedupe', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/dedupe',
    } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: A.businessUnit.id } });
    typebotFake.enabled = true;
    const createdIds: string[] = [];
    for (let repetition = 0; repetition < 3; repetition += 1) {
      const body = eventoMeta(`558499991020${repetition}`, `dedupe-meta-${repetition}`);
      const signature = assinar(body);
      const responses = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => postMeta(body, signature)));
      expect(responses.every((response) => response.status === 200)).toBe(true);
      createdIds.push(`wamid-dedupe-meta-${repetition}`);
    }
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: { in: createdIds }, direction: 'IN' } })).toBe(3);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(3);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.aggregate({ where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id }, _sum: { unreadCount: true } })).toMatchObject({ _sum: { unreadCount: 3 } });
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(3);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  it('deduplicates ten concurrent Evolution deliveries per key across three message IDs', async () => {
    const ids: string[] = [];
    for (let repetition = 0; repetition < 3; repetition += 1) {
      const id = `evo-dedupe-${repetition}`;
      const body = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance: INSTANCIA_A, data: {
        key: { remoteJid: '5584999950505@s.whatsapp.net', fromMe: false, id },
        pushName: 'Cliente', message: { conversation: `duplicate-${repetition}` },
      } };
      const responses = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => postEvolution(body)));
      expect(responses.every((response) => response.status === 200)).toBe(true);
      ids.push(id);
    }
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: { in: ids }, direction: 'IN' } })).toBe(3);
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id, contact: '5584999950505' } });
    expect(conversation.unreadCount).toBe(3);
  });

  describe('Evolution — segredo por cabeçalho', () => {
    const upsert = (jid: string, texto: string, fromMe = false, pushName = 'Contato') => ({
      event: 'messages.upsert',
      instance: INSTANCIA_A,
      data: { key: { remoteJid: jid, fromMe, id: `evo-${texto}` }, pushName, message: { conversation: texto } },
    });

    it('recusa segredo errado', async () => {
      await postEvolution(upsert('5584999997777@s.whatsapp.net', 'x'), 'segredo-errado-mesmo-tamanho').expect(403);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count()).toBe(0);
    });

    it('aceita com o segredo certo e grava o contato sem o JID', async () => {
      await postEvolution(upsert('5584999998888@s.whatsapp.net', 'oi')).expect(200);

      const conversa = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow();
      expect(conversa.contact).toBe('5584999998888');
      expect(conversa.contactType).toBe('PESSOA');
      expect(conversa.displayName).toBe('Contato');
      expect(await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow()).toMatchObject({ officialInboundAt: null });
    });

    it('rejects Evolution message keys without a provider ID before conversation or unread effects', async () => {
      const body = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance: INSTANCIA_A, data: {
        key: { remoteJid: '5584999980808@s.whatsapp.net', fromMe: false },
        pushName: 'Cliente', message: { conversation: 'no external message id' },
      } };
      await postEvolution(body).expect(409).expect(({ body: response }) => expect(response.code).toBe('EXTERNAL_MESSAGE_ID_REQUIRED'));
      expect(await prisma.message.count()).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count()).toBe(0);
    });

    it('mensagem NOSSA (fromMe) não usa pushName como nome do contato', async () => {
      await postEvolution(upsert('5584999999999@s.whatsapp.net', 'resposta', true, 'Nome do nosso número')).expect(200);

      const conversa = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow();
      expect(conversa.displayName).toBeNull();
      expect(conversa.unreadCount).toBe(0);
    });

    it('grupo entra como GRUPO e mantém o identificador', async () => {
      await postEvolution(upsert('123456789@g.us', 'no grupo')).expect(200);
      const conversa = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow();
      expect(conversa.contactType).toBe('GRUPO');
      expect(conversa.contact).toBe('123456789@g.us');
    });

    it('connection.update marca o canal como conectado e grava o número da wuid', async () => {
      await postEvolution({
        event: 'connection.update',
        instance: INSTANCIA_A,
        data: { state: 'open', wuid: '5584912345678@s.whatsapp.net' },
      }).expect(200);

      const canal = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { evolutionInstanceName: INSTANCIA_A } });
      expect(canal.status).toBe('CONECTADO');
      const bu = await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: A.businessUnit.id } });
      // O wuid do webhook não substitui o telefone canônico confirmado na BU.
      expect(bu.phone).toBe('5584999990000');
    });

    it('close remove a prontidão e o sender resolver bloqueia o canal', async () => {
      const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL' } });
      await postEvolution({ event: 'connection.update', instance: INSTANCIA_A, data: { state: 'close' } }).expect(200);
      const closed = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      expect(closed).toMatchObject({ status: 'DESCONECTADO', providerConnectionState: 'DISCONNECTED', receiptVerifiedAt: expect.any(Date) });
      await expect(app.get(ChannelCredentialResolver).resolve(channel.id, 'send')).rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY' } });
    });

    it('não persiste estado arbitrário antes da prova de identidade', async () => {
      const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL' } });
      await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel.id }, data: { ownNumberVerified: false, verifiedEpoch: null, status: 'CONECTANDO', providerConnectionState: 'PENDING' } });
      await postEvolution({ event: 'connection.update', instance: INSTANCIA_A, data: { state: 'SENSITIVE_FIXTURE_MARKER' } }).expect(200);
      const event = await prisma.webhookEvent.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id } });
      expect(JSON.stringify(event.payload)).not.toContain('SENSITIVE_FIXTURE_MARKER');
      expect(event.payload).toMatchObject({ instance: INSTANCIA_A, state: 'PENDING_IDENTITY' });
      expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(0);
    });

    it('redige componentes secretos URL em diagnóstico verificado e preserva metadados úteis', async () => {
      await postEvolution({ event: 'provider.unrecognized', instance: INSTANCIA_A, data: {
        stage: 'SAFE_STAGE', callback: 'https://example.invalid/webhooks/evolution/secret-path?%72efresh_token=FIXTURE_REFRESH&%70in=FIXTURE_PIN&state=SAFE_STATE',
      } }).expect(200);
      const event = await prisma.webhookEvent.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: A.businessUnit.id } });
      expect(JSON.stringify(event.payload)).not.toMatch(/FIXTURE_REFRESH|FIXTURE_PIN|secret-path/);
      expect(event.payload).toMatchObject({ data: { stage: 'SAFE_STAGE' } });
    });
  });

  describe('fila de atendimento (ADR-0009 §2)', () => {
    const receber = async (texto: string) => {
      const corpo = eventoMeta('5584911112222', texto);
      await postMeta(corpo, assinar(corpo)).expect(200);
    };

    it('mensagem nova reabre conversa FECHADA e solta a atribuição', async () => {
      await receber('primeira');
      const conversa = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow();
      await prisma.conversation.update({
        where: { id: conversa.id },
        data: { status: 'CLOSED', attendantName: 'Fulano', attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO' },
      });

      await receber('voltei');

      const depois = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversa.id } });
      expect(depois.status).toBe('QUEUED');
      expect(depois.attendantName).toBeNull();
    });

    it('conversa EM ATENDIMENTO não é rebaixada por mensagem nova', async () => {
      await receber('primeira');
      const conversa = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow();
      await prisma.conversation.update({
        where: { id: conversa.id },
        data: { status: 'IN_SERVICE', attendantName: 'Fulano', attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO' },
      });

      await receber('mais uma');

      const depois = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversa.id } });
      expect(depois.status).toBe('IN_SERVICE');
      expect(depois.attendantName).toBe('Fulano');
      expect(depois.unreadCount).toBe(2);
    });
  });

  describe('o dado recebido respeita o escopo', () => {
    it('CROSS-TENANT: o dono de Beta não enxerga a conversa que entrou na BU de Alfa', async () => {
      const corpo = eventoMeta('5584933334444', 'segredo da alfa');
      await postMeta(corpo, assinar(corpo)).expect(200);

      const cookieBeta = await login(app, 'dono@beta.test');
      const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieBeta)
        .expect(404);
      expect(res.body.code).toBe('NAO_ENCONTRADO');
    });
  });
});
```

```text
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import * as ts from 'typescript';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

const inboxDirectory = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../../inbox');
const consumerPaths = [
  resolve(inboxDirectory, 'conversations.service.ts'),
  resolve(inboxDirectory, 'incoming.service.ts'),
  resolve(inboxDirectory, 'initial-message.service.ts'),
  ...readdirSync(inboxDirectory)
    .filter((name) => /^outbound.*\.ts$/.test(name))
    .map((name) => resolve(inboxDirectory, name)),
];

const providerDispatchNames = /^(?:enviarTexto|sendText|sendMessage|enviarMensagem)$/;
const concreteName = /^(?:Meta|Evolution|Instagram|IG)(?:[A-Z_].*)?$/i;
const providerToken = /^(?:META_CLIENT|EVOLUTION_CLIENT|INSTAGRAM_CLIENT|INSTAGRAM_MESSAGING_CLIENT|INSTAGRAM_HTTP_CLIENT|IG_CLIENT|IG_MESSAGING_CLIENT)$/;
const branchDispatchName = /^(?:send|dispatch|client|instagramClient|metaClient|evolutionClient|eligibilityPolicy|policy|InstagramEligibilityPolicy|MetaEligibilityPolicy|EvolutionEligibilityPolicy|IgClient|IGClient)\w*$/i;
const channelMetadataProperties = new Set(['instagramUsername', 'instagramInboundAt']);

function isChannelMetadataProperty(node: ts.Identifier): boolean {
  if (!channelMetadataProperties.has(node.text)) return false;
  const parent = node.parent;
  if (ts.isPropertyAssignment(parent) && parent.name === node) return true;
  if (ts.isPropertySignature(parent) && parent.name === node) return true;
  if (!ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(parent) || parent.name !== node) return false;
  const use = parent.parent;
  if (ts.isCallExpression(use) && use.expression === parent) return false;
  if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(use) && use.expression === parent) {
    const call = use.parent;
    return !(ts.isCallExpression(call) && call.expression === use && providerDispatchNames.test(use.name.text));
  }
  if (ts.isElementAccessExpression(use) && use.expression === parent) {
    const call = use.parent;
    return !(ts.isCallExpression(call) && call.expression === use && providerDispatchNames.test(propertyName(use) ?? ''));
  }
  return true;
}

function propertyName(node: ts.Expression): string | undefined {
  if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(node)) return node.name.text;
  if (ts.isElementAccessExpression(node) && node.argumentExpression && ts.isStringLiteralLike(node.argumentExpression)) {
    return node.argumentExpression.text;
  }
  return undefined;
}

function hasProviderOrigin(node: ts.Node): boolean {
  let found = false;
  const visit = (current: ts.Node) => {
    if ((ts.isBinaryExpression(current) && [ts.SyntaxKind.EqualsEqualsToken, ts.SyntaxKind.EqualsEqualsEqualsToken,
      ts.SyntaxKind.ExclamationEqualsToken, ts.SyntaxKind.ExclamationEqualsEqualsToken].includes(current.operatorToken.kind))
      || ts.isCaseClause(current)) {
      const text = current.getText();
      if (/\b(?:provider|channelOrigin|adapterOrigin|type)\b/i.test(text)
        && /['"](?:OFICIAL|NAO_OFICIAL|INSTAGRAM)['"]/.test(text)) found = true;
    }
    if (!found) ts.forEachChild(current, visit);
  };
  visit(node);
  return found;
}

function hasConcreteBranchSignal(node: ts.Node): boolean {
  let found = false;
  const visit = (current: ts.Node) => {
    if (ts.isIdentifier(current) && !isChannelMetadataProperty(current) && (branchDispatchName.test(current.text)
      || concreteName.test(current.text) || providerToken.test(current.text))) found = true;
    if (!found) ts.forEachChild(current, visit);
  };
  visit(node);
  return found;
}

function providerDispatchViolations(source: string): string[] {
  const file = ts.createSourceFile('consumer.ts', source, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true, ts.ScriptKind.TS);
  const violations = new Set<string>();
  const concreteImports = new Set<string>();
  const policyNamespaces = new Set<string>();
  const providerModules = /(?:^|\/)integrations\/(?:meta|evolution|channels|instagram)\//i;
  const credentialModule = /channel-credential-resolver/;
  const policyModule = /(?:channel-eligibility-policy|channel-window-policy)/;
  const concretePolicyName = /^(?:Official|Meta|Evolution|Instagram|Ig)[A-Z].*Policy$/;

  for (const statement of file.statements) {
    if (!ts.isImportDeclaration(statement) || !ts.isStringLiteral(statement.moduleSpecifier)) continue;
    const moduleName = statement.moduleSpecifier.text;
    if (providerModules.test(moduleName)) violations.add('provider client or adapter import');
    if (credentialModule.test(moduleName)) violations.add('credential resolver import');
    if (policyModule.test(moduleName) && statement.importClause?.namedBindings
      && ts.isNamespaceImport(statement.importClause.namedBindings)) {
      policyNamespaces.add(statement.importClause.namedBindings.name.text);
    }
    if (!statement.importClause?.namedBindings || !ts.isNamedImports(statement.importClause.namedBindings)) continue;
    const names = statement.importClause.namedBindings.elements;
    for (const element of names) {
      const imported = (element.propertyName ?? element.name).text;
      const local = element.name.text;
      if (providerToken.test(imported) || providerToken.test(local) || concreteName.test(imported) || concreteName.test(local)) {
        violations.add('provider injection token or client type');
        concreteImports.add(local);
      }
      if (policyModule.test(moduleName) && (concretePolicyName.test(imported)
        || /EligibilityPolicy$/.test(imported) && imported !== 'ChannelEligibilityPolicy')) {
        violations.add('concrete eligibility policy import');
        concreteImports.add(local);
      }
    }
  }

  const identifierRefs = (node: ts.Node) => {
    if (ts.isIdentifier(node) && !isChannelMetadataProperty(node)
      && (providerToken.test(node.text) || concreteName.test(node.text) || concreteImports.has(node.text))) {
      violations.add('provider token or client reference');
    }
    ts.forEachChild(node, identifierRefs);
  };
  identifierRefs(file);
  const namespacePolicyRefs = (node: ts.Node) => {
    if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(node) && ts.isIdentifier(node.expression)
      && policyNamespaces.has(node.expression.text) && concretePolicyName.test(node.name.text)) {
      violations.add('concrete eligibility policy namespace access');
    }
    ts.forEachChild(node, namespacePolicyRefs);
  };
  namespacePolicyRefs(file);

  const visit = (node: ts.Node) => {
    if (ts.isCallExpression(node)) {
      const method = propertyName(node.expression);
      if (method && providerDispatchNames.test(method)) {
        // sendMessage is part of the abstract Channel contract. It is forbidden only
        // when selected in a concrete provider-origin branch or on a known concrete client.
        if (method !== 'sendMessage') violations.add('provider text dispatch');
        else if (ts.isPropertyAccessExpression(node.expression)
          && ts.isIdentifier(node.expression.expression)
          && concreteImports.has(node.expression.expression.text)) violations.add('provider text dispatch');
      }
    }
    if (ts.isIfStatement(node) && hasProviderOrigin(node.expression)) {
      if (hasConcreteBranchSignal(node.thenStatement) || node.elseStatement && hasConcreteBranchSignal(node.elseStatement)) {
        violations.add('provider-specific dispatch branch');
      }
    } else if (ts.isConditionalExpression(node) && hasProviderOrigin(node.condition)) {
      if (hasConcreteBranchSignal(node.whenTrue) || hasConcreteBranchSignal(node.whenFalse)) {
        violations.add('provider-specific dispatch branch');
      }
    } else if (ts.isSwitchStatement(node)
      && /\b(?:provider|channelOrigin|adapterOrigin|type)\b/i.test(node.expression.getText())
      && node.caseBlock.clauses.some((clause) => ts.isCaseClause(clause)
        && /['"](?:OFICIAL|NAO_OFICIAL|INSTAGRAM)['"]/.test(clause.expression.getText()))) {
      if (node.caseBlock.clauses.some((clause) => hasConcreteBranchSignal(clause))) {
        violations.add('provider-specific dispatch branch');
      }
    }
    ts.forEachChild(node, visit);
  };
  visit(file);
  return [...violations];
}

describe('common outbound dependency boundary', () => {
  it('keeps provider clients and dispatch inside channel adapters', () => {
    const violations = consumerPaths.flatMap((path) => {
      const source = readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
      return providerDispatchViolations(source).map((rule) => `${path}: ${rule}`);
    });
    expect(violations).toEqual([]);
  });

  it.each([
    "import { MetaClient } from '../../integrations/meta/meta.client';",
    "import { MetaChannelFactory } from '../../integrations/channels/meta-channel';",
    "import { ChannelCredentialResolver } from '../channels/channel-credential-resolver';",
    "if (context.channelOrigin === 'OFICIAL') return sendMeta();",
    "switch (binding.type) { case 'NAO_OFICIAL': return sendEvolution(); }",
    "const send = context.channelOrigin === 'OFICIAL' ? sendMeta() : sendEvolution();",
    "switch (context.provider) { case 'OFICIAL': return sendMessage(message); }",
    "if (context.adapterOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM') return instagramClient.sendMessage(message);",
    "const dispatch = context.adapterOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM' ? instagramDispatch : commonDispatch;",
    "switch (context.adapterOrigin) { case 'INSTAGRAM': return sendInstagram(); default: return sendCommon(); }",
    "import { InstagramMessagingClient as DirectMessages } from '../../integrations/instagram/instagram-messaging.client';",
    "import { InstagramEligibilityPolicy as IgPolicy } from '../inbox/channel-eligibility-policy';",
    "import { instagramUsername } from './provider-reference';",
    "type instagramInboundAt = { sendText(text: string): void };",
    "const instagramUsername = source; void instagramUsername;",
    "instagramUsername.sendText('message');",
    "payload.instagramUsername.sendMessage(message);",
    "payload.instagramInboundAt['sendText'](message);",
    "payload.instagramUsername();",
    "const INSTAGRAM_CLIENT = Symbol('instagram');",
    "const IG_CLIENT = Symbol('instagram');",
    "if (route.adapterOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM') policy = new InstagramEligibilityPolicy(prisma);",
    "import { OfficialChannelWindowPolicy } from '../inbox/channel-window-policy';",
    "import * as policies from '../inbox/channel-eligibility-policy'; const policy = new policies.OfficialEligibilityPolicy(windows);",
    "if (route.adapterOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM') return channel.sendMessage(message);",
  ])('detects synthetic provider coupling outside the adapter allowlist: %s', (source) => {
    expect(providerDispatchViolations(source).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('allows abstract dispatch, generic policy calls, and qualified address mapping', () => {
    expect(providerDispatchViolations('channel.sendMessage(message); eligibilityPolicy.evaluate(input);')).toEqual([]);
    expect(providerDispatchViolations("if (context.adapterOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM') return qualifiedInstagramPeerAddress(context);"))
      .toEqual([]);
    expect(providerDispatchViolations(`
      type ChannelFields = { instagramUsername: string | null; instagramInboundAt: Date | null };
      const fields: ChannelFields = { instagramUsername: null, instagramInboundAt: null };
      if (context.channelOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM') {
        return { instagramUsername: fields.instagramUsername, instagramInboundAt: context.instagramInboundAt };
      }
    `)).toEqual([]);
    expect(providerDispatchViolations('fields.instagramInboundAt?.getTime();')).toEqual([]);
    expect(providerDispatchViolations("const platform = route.adapterOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM' ? 'instagram' : 'whatsapp';"))
      .toEqual([]);
    expect(providerDispatchViolations("import type { ChannelEligibilityPolicy } from '../inbox/channel-eligibility-policy';"))
      .toEqual([]);
    expect(providerDispatchViolations("import { Channel } from '../channels/transport/channel'; import type { ChannelEligibilityPolicy } from '../inbox/channel-eligibility-policy'; class Consumer { private readonly eligibilityPolicy: ChannelEligibilityPolicy; send(channel: Channel) { return channel.sendMessage(message); } }"))
      .toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

### api-app/src/domain/inbox/channel-eligibility-policy.spec.ts — 3478 bytes SHA256 beb3e5a338f6deefb05dc30d753171fdceb89dda08564e9d1209398c5d5e2172

```text

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { OfficialChannelWindowPolicy } from './channel-window-policy';
import {
  ChannelEligibilityPolicyRegistry, EvolutionEligibilityPolicy, InstagramEligibilityPolicy, OfficialEligibilityPolicy,
} from './channel-eligibility-policy';

const reference = { conversationId: 'conversation', providerResourceIdentityId: 'resource' };
const closed = { open: false, expiresAt: null };

describe('configured channel eligibility bindings', () => {
  it('preserves the official no-window exception for text, initial, reply, and automation without querying a window', async () => {
    const windows = {
      inspect: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(closed),
      inspectInTransaction: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(closed),
      inspectMany: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(new Map()),
    } as unknown as OfficialChannelWindowPolicy;
    const registry = new ChannelEligibilityPolicyRegistry([
      new OfficialEligibilityPolicy(windows), new EvolutionEligibilityPolicy(),
      new InstagramEligibilityPolicy({} as never),
    ]);
    const policy = registry.forCandidate('OFICIAL', false);
    expect(policy.requiresWindow).toBe(false);
    await expect(policy.inspect(reference)).resolves.toEqual({ open: true, expiresAt: null });
    await expect(policy.inspectMany([reference])).resolves.toEqual(new Map([[JSON.stringify(['conversation', 'resource']), { open: true, expiresAt: null }]]));
    for (const operation of ['INITIAL', 'REPLY', 'AUTOMATION'] as const) {
      expect(policy.evaluate({ operation, humanActor: operation !== 'AUTOMATION', kind: 'text', inboundWindowOpen: false }))
        .toEqual({ eligible: true, reason: 'AVAILABLE' });
    }
    expect(windows.inspect).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(windows.inspectMany).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('keeps qualified-window policy for true official bindings, Instagram strict, and Evolution windowless', async () => {
    const windows = { inspect: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(closed), inspectMany: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(new Map()) } as unknown as OfficialChannelWindowPolicy;
    const registry = new ChannelEligibilityPolicyRegistry([
      new OfficialEligibilityPolicy(windows), new EvolutionEligibilityPolicy(),
      new InstagramEligibilityPolicy({} as never),
    ]);
    const official = registry.forCandidate('OFICIAL', true);
    expect(official.evaluate({ operation: 'INITIAL', humanActor: true, kind: 'text', inboundWindowOpen: false }))
      .toEqual({ eligible: false, reason: 'WINDOW_CLOSED' });
    await expect(official.inspect(reference)).resolves.toEqual(closed);
    expect(registry.forCandidate('OFICIAL', false).evaluate({ operation: 'INITIAL', humanActor: true,
      kind: 'approved-template', inboundWindowOpen: false })).toEqual({ eligible: true, reason: 'AVAILABLE' });
    const instagram = registry.forCandidate('INSTAGRAM', true);
    expect(instagram.evaluate({ operation: 'REPLY', humanActor: true, kind: 'text', inboundWindowOpen: false }))
      .toEqual({ eligible: false, reason: 'WINDOW_CLOSED' });
    expect(() => registry.forCandidate('INSTAGRAM', false)).toThrow(/Invalid requiresWindow binding/);
    expect(registry.forCandidate('NAO_OFICIAL', false).evaluate({ operation: 'AUTOMATION', humanActor: true,
      kind: 'text', inboundWindowOpen: false })).toEqual({ eligible: true, reason: 'AVAILABLE' });
    expect(() => registry.forCandidate('NAO_OFICIAL', true)).toThrow(/Invalid requiresWindow binding/);
  });
});
```

### api-app/src/domain/inbox/contact-identity.spec.ts — 1395 bytes SHA256 6587b7b53ac5ce9726f8c8bcf48cce47efbc2ede8b0f7bf9fe3113026a7ab848

```text

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { whatsappPeerAddress } from './contact-identity';

describe('WhatsApp peer identity', () => {
  it('uses the same BU-local person key for manual start and either verified adapter', () => {
    const manual = whatsappPeerAddress({ buId: 'bu-1', externalPeerId: '+55 (11) 98765-4321', contactType: 'PESSOA' });
    const officialIngress = whatsappPeerAddress({ buId: 'bu-1', externalPeerId: '5511987654321', contactType: 'PESSOA' });
    const evolutionIngress = whatsappPeerAddress({ buId: 'bu-1', externalPeerId: '55 11 98765 4321', contactType: 'PESSOA', groupScope: 'provider-resource-2' });
    expect(officialIngress).toEqual(manual);
    expect(evolutionIngress).toEqual(manual);
  });

  it('keeps group identity namespace separate from a person with the same external string', () => {
    expect(whatsappPeerAddress({ buId: 'bu-1', externalPeerId: '5511987654321', contactType: 'GRUPO' }))
      .not.toEqual(whatsappPeerAddress({ buId: 'bu-1', externalPeerId: '5511987654321', contactType: 'PESSOA' }));
  });

  it.each(['', 'customer42', '1234567', '+012345678', '1234567890123456', `${' '.repeat(20)}12345678${' '.repeat(13)}`])(
    'rejects an invalid or ambiguous person address %j', (peer) => {
      expect(() => whatsappPeerAddress({ buId: 'bu-1', externalPeerId: peer, contactType: 'PESSOA' })).toThrow();
    },
  );
});
```

### api-app/src/domain/inbox/conversation-start.service.spec.ts — 5231 bytes SHA256 1e2e797f83fe12d4609b0f1d6cb05b982b70baf492861da24368d9496a2ee606

```text

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ConversationStartService, normalizeContactPhone } from './conversation-start.service';

describe('manual conversation start', () => {
  it.each([
    ['+55 (11) 98765-4321', '5511987654321'],
    ['1 202 555 0142', '12025550142'],
  ])('normalizes %s without inferring a country code', (input, expected) => {
    expect(normalizeContactPhone(input)).toBe(expected);
  });

  it.each(['', '1234567', '1234567890123456', 'abc12345678', '+012345678', '\t12345678', '12345678\n'])('rejects invalid phone %j', (input) => {
    expect(() => normalizeContactPhone(input)).toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects raw phone input longer than 40 characters before normalization', () => {
    const paddedNumber = `${' '.repeat(20)}12345678${' '.repeat(13)}`;
    expect(paddedNumber.length).toBeGreaterThan(40);
    expect(() => normalizeContactPhone(paddedNumber)).toThrow();
  });

  it('creates under the qualified-identity lock and reuses without changing the existing conversation', async () => {
    const existing = {
      id: 'conversation-1', buId: 'bu-1', contact: '5511987654321', contactType: 'PESSOA',
      status: 'IN_SERVICE', unreadCount: 3, lastMessageAt: new Date('2026-01-02T03:04:05Z'),
    };
    const created = { ...existing, status: 'QUEUED', unreadCount: 0, lastMessageAt: null };
    const identity = { contactId: 'contact-1', contact: { id: 'contact-1', conversations: [] } };
    const tx = {
      $queryRaw: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),
      contactIdentity: { findUnique: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null), create: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(identity) },
      contact: { create: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ id: 'contact-1' }) },
      conversation: {
        findFirst: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null),
        create: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(created),
      },
    };
    const prisma = {
      contactIdentity: { findUnique: vi.fn().mockResolvedValueOnce(null).mockResolvedValueOnce({ ...identity, contact: { id: 'contact-1', conversations: [existing] } }) },
      $transaction: vi.fn((callback: (value: typeof tx) => unknown) => callback(tx)),
    };
    const channels = { hasReadyChannelForNewConversation: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(true) };
    const service = new ConversationStartService(prisma as never, channels as never);

    const first = await service.start('bu-1', '+55 (11) 98765-4321');
    const reused = await service.start('bu-1', '5511987654321');

    expect(first).toEqual({ conversation: created, created: true });
    expect(first.conversation).toMatchObject({ status: 'QUEUED', unreadCount: 0, lastMessageAt: null });
    expect(reused).toEqual({ conversation: existing, created: false });
    expect(tx.$queryRaw).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(tx.conversation.create).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(channels.hasReadyChannelForNewConversation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(channels.hasReadyChannelForNewConversation).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, 'bu-1');
    expect(prisma.contactIdentity.findUnique).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(prisma.contactIdentity.findUnique).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      where: { buId_platform_namespace_scopeReference_externalPeerId: {
        buId: 'bu-1', platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: 'PERSON', scopeReference: 'BU', externalPeerId: '5511987654321',
      } },
      include: { contact: { include: { conversations: { where: { buId: 'bu-1' }, take: 2 } } } },
    });
  });

  it('does not create a new conversation when no current channel is locally ready', async () => {
    const tx = {
      $queryRaw: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue([]),
      conversation: { findFirst: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null), create: vi.fn() },
      contactIdentity: { findUnique: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null), create: vi.fn() },
      contact: { create: vi.fn() },
    };
    const prisma = {
      contactIdentity: { findUnique: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null) },
      $transaction: vi.fn((callback: (value: typeof tx) => unknown) => callback(tx)),
    };
    const channels = { hasReadyChannelForNewConversation: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(false) };
    const service = new ConversationStartService(prisma as never, channels as never);
    await expect(service.start('bu-1', '5511987654321')).rejects.toMatchObject({
      response: { code: 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY' },
    });
    expect(tx.conversation.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('reuses a formatted legacy-contact row by its canonical identity before resolving readiness', async () => {
    const existing = { id: 'existing', buId: 'bu-1', contact: '5511987654321' };
    const prisma = { contactIdentity: { findUnique: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ contact: { id: 'contact-1', conversations: [{ ...existing, contact: '+55 (11) 98765-4321' }] } }) }, $transaction: vi.fn() };
    const channels = { hasReadyChannelForNewConversation: vi.fn() };
    const result = await new ConversationStartService(prisma as never, channels as never).start('bu-1', '+55 (11) 98765-4321');
    expect(result).toEqual({ conversation: { ...existing, contact: '5511987654321' }, created: false });
    expect(channels.hasReadyChannelForNewConversation).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(prisma.$transaction).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```text
      }]) },

      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ conversation: { id: 'conversation-1', contactId: 'contact-1', contact: '5584999990000', unreadCount: 1 }, message: { id: 'message-1' }, creationWinner: true, reconciled: false, conversationCreated: true, reopened: false })

      ingress: { channelId: 'channel-1', providerResourceIdentityId: 'identity-1', channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', generation: 1, pairingEpoch: 1 },

      contactIdentity: { findFirst: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ id: 'peer-1' }) },

      }]) },
      buChannel: { findUnique: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ type: 'OFICIAL', phoneNumberId: 'phone-1' }) },
      typebotSession: { findUnique: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null), create: vi.fn() },

    const typebot = {

      iniciar: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ sessionId: 'session-1', messages: [
        { type: 'text', content: { text: 'first output' } },
        { type: 'text', content: { text: 'second output' } },
      ] }),
      continuar: vi.fn(),
    };
    const accepted = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      conversation: { id: 'conversation-1', contactId: 'contact-1', contact: '5584999990000', unreadCount: 1 },
      message: { id: 'message-1' }, creationWinner: true, reconciled: false, conversationCreated: true, reopened: false,
    });
    const send = vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(error);
    const service = new IncomingService(prisma as never, { publicarNaBu: vi.fn() } as never, typebot as never, { accept: accepted } as never, { send } as never);

    await expect(service.registrar({ buId: 'bu-1', canal: 'OFICIAL', contato: '5584999990000', ehGrupo: false, direcao: 'IN', tipo: 'TEXTO', conteudo: 'oi', externalId: 'in-1', providerResourceIdentityId: 'identity-1', channelId: 'channel-1', generation: 1, pairingEpoch: 1 })).resolves.toBeTruthy();

    expect(accepted).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(send).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(send).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ text: 'first output' }));
    expect(send).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ text: 'second output' }));
```

```text
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { EvolutionHttpClient, EvolutionIndisponivelError } from './evolution.client';

describe('EvolutionHttpClient instance creation response', () => {
  const before = {
    url: process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL,

  afterEach(() => {
    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
    if (before.url === undefined) delete process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL; else process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL = before.url;
    if (before.key === undefined) delete process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY; else process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY = before.key;
    if (before.webhook === undefined) delete process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL; else process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL = before.webhook;
    if (before.secret === undefined) delete process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET; else process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET = before.secret;
  });

  it('returns only QR material when create confirms the exact instance name', async () => {
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL = 'https://evolution.invalid';
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY = 'fixture-key';

    process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET = 'fixture-secret';

  });

  it.each([

    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL = 'https://evolution.invalid';
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY = 'fixture-key';

    expect(String(fetchFake.mock.calls[0][0])).toContain('/instance/delete/lh-exact-name');

    { label: 'string', json: async () => '[]' },

    { label: 'malformed row', json: async () => [{ name: 'another-instance' }, null] },

    { label: 'populated optional fields', row: { name: 'lh-target', ownerJid: '15551234567@s.whatsapp.net', connectionStatus: 'open' }, expected: { name: 'lh-target', ownerJid: '15551234567@s.whatsapp.net', connectionStatus: 'open' } },

    expect(fetchFake).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);

  it('rejects redirects without following them or exposing the configured key', async () => {
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_URL = 'https://evolution.invalid';
    process.env.EVOLUTION_API_KEY = 'fixture-secret-key';
    const fetchFake = vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new TypeError('redirect fixture with secret URL'));
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchFake);
    const error = await new EvolutionHttpClient().criarInstancia('lh-safe-name').catch((caught: unknown) => caught as Error);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(EvolutionIndisponivelError);
    expect(error.message).not.toMatch(/fixture-secret-key|secret URL/);
    expect(fetchFake.mock.calls[0][1].redirect).toBe('error');

  it('returns ACCEPTED only with an external ID and treats HTTP and post-dispatch failures as UNKNOWN once', async () => {

      .mockRejectedValueOnce(new TypeError('timeout after dispatch'))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => { throw new SyntaxError('malformed'); } })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, status: 503, json: async () => ({ key: { id: 'contradictory-id' } }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({}) });

    const client = new EvolutionHttpClient();
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: 'evo-id-1' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED' });
    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))

    await expect(client.enviarTexto('fixture-instance', 'user:opaque', 'hello'))
```
